### 2026-09-11 — 冷会话打开的窗口化读取 / Windowed cold-session opening

- EN: The Web session-open path no longer folds the whole log. `session.history.follow` serves an ordinary cold Session's opening snapshot from ONE indexed suffix window when the mounted persistence exposes the fork seek surface (`messageCut` + `readFrom`) and `ctx.sessionProjectionCache` holds a current-format record for that Session: the page uses the same message-cut ladder as older pages, the projection tail is folded by `ctx.sessionProjections.restore` over that same read, and the page tail stays the reported cursor. Everything the window cannot prove — a live Session, a subagent address, a non-seekable persistence, no usable record, a window short of a page, a stored tail ending inside an open turn (whose balanced view carries synthetic closers the log lacks), a record beyond the stored log — falls back to today's full observation, so the wire block always equals a full observation's values and `asOfSeq`. An uncached observation now installs the record the next open folds from: a fail-soft write-back of the log's **durable prefix** (the cold read reports how much of its balanced log the stored log really holds), never `checkpoint(session)`, whose rows lead the log by the restored Session's resume marker — so no written row ever passes the stored log end. Promotion keeps working without an observation: the windowed open activates the Session in the background by id (`SessionController.activate` → `ApiSessionAgentController.resolveAgent`). Fork-owned modules: `packages/api/session-controller/src/fork/open-window.ts` and `packages/session/session-projection-cache/src/fork/checkpoint-read.ts`; injections in `history.ts`, `index.ts`, the cache's `index.ts`, and `page-boundary.ts` (windowed read); `tsconfig.host.json` lists the new source file. Agent Note: `.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-09-11-windowed-session-open.md`. Verified: `pnpm vitest run packages/api/session-controller/tests packages/session/session-projection-cache/tests packages/session-query/session-query/tests packages/session/session-projection/tests packages/api/gateway/tests` 54 files / 1235 tests green; `tsc -b tsconfig.host.json tsconfig.client.json` clean; oxlint clean on the touched paths. Both fast paths had been dead in production until this round: `ctx.get('sessionPersistence')` answers a cordis tracker proxy, and a method taken off that proxy only receives the provider's own `this` when it is called on the proxy (`vendor/cordis/src/utils.ts`, `createShadowMethod`); both sites used to wrap the extracted methods in a plain object, so `SqliteSessionPersistence.messageCut`/`readFrom` ran with the wrapper as `this`, threw, and were swallowed by the fallback. `history.ts` now extracts them through one `seekSurface` helper that binds both to the proxy, and the tracker-provider tests fail if that binding is dropped. Fixing the shared surface also activates the pre-existing `page()`/`loadOlder` indexed fast path for the first time — same code, newly reachable. Production measurement then showed that activation as a regression on the deployed store, where 932 of 938 session rows are still v0: `messageCut` answers in the stored physical seq space while `loadStoredFrom`'s historical arm restores the whole log through the format catalog into a re-based space, so a window plan paid one cut query plus a full log read per attempted margin (6.8 s on the 1.04M-event session) and got an empty page, then fell back to the observation path and read the log again. Both fast paths now gate on a new `seekable(id)` probe in the fork seek surface — `SqliteStore.seekable` answers from the session row's format version and reads no event rows — and `readIndexedSuffix` calls it before `messageCut` and before any `readFrom`. The historical arm of `loadStoredFrom` keeps serving whole-log readers and documents that it cannot serve an indexed page cut. Resolved in the same round: `FORK_SURFACE.md`/.zh now carry rows for both new fork modules (the windowed open, the checkpoint read plus write-back) and the updated `history.ts` row, matching the registration this entry initially flagged as missing.
- ZH: Web 会话打开路径不再折叠整段日志。当挂载的持久化后端暴露 fork 寻址面（`messageCut` + `readFrom`）且 `ctx.sessionProjectionCache` 持有该 Session 的当前格式记录时，`session.history.follow` 用一个索引后缀窗口提供普通冷会话的打开快照：页面复用与较旧分页相同的消息切点阶梯，投影尾部在同一次读取上用 `ctx.sessionProjections.restore` 折叠，页尾始终是报告的游标。窗口无法证明的一切——实时会话、子代理地址、无寻址面的持久化、没有可用记录、窗口容不下整页、已存尾部停在未闭合回合内（其平衡视图带日志并不持有的合成 closer）、记录超出已存日志——都回落到既有的完整观测，因此线上块始终等于完整观测的值与 `asOfSeq`。未命中缓存的观测现在会装上供下一次打开折叠的记录：对日志的**持久前缀**做软失败写回（冷读会报告其平衡日志中真正已存的部分），绝不用 `checkpoint(session)`——后者的行会因恢复出的 Session 的恢复标记而领先日志——因此写出的行绝不越过已存日志末端。提升机制在没有观测对象时照常工作：窗口化打开按 id 在后台激活（`SessionController.activate` → `ApiSessionAgentController.resolveAgent`）。Fork 自有模块：`packages/api/session-controller/src/fork/open-window.ts` 与 `packages/session/session-projection-cache/src/fork/checkpoint-read.ts`；注入点：`history.ts`、`index.ts`、缓存的 `index.ts` 与 `page-boundary.ts`（窗口化读取）；`tsconfig.host.json` 列出新源文件。Agent Note：`.agents/notes/implemented/architecture/2026-09-11-windowed-session-open.md`。已验证：`pnpm vitest run packages/api/session-controller/tests packages/session/session-projection-cache/tests packages/session-query/session-query/tests packages/session/session-projection/tests packages/api/gateway/tests` 54 个文件 / 1235 项全绿；`tsc -b tsconfig.host.json tsconfig.client.json` 干净；改动路径 oxlint 干净。两条快速路径在本轮之前在生产中都是死的：`ctx.get('sessionPersistence')` 返回 cordis tracker 代理，而只有在该代理上调用时，从它取出的方法才会拿到提供方自己的 `this`（`vendor/cordis/src/utils.ts` 的 `createShadowMethod`）；两处旧代码把取出的方法包进普通对象，于是 `SqliteSessionPersistence.messageCut`/`readFrom` 以包装对象为 `this` 运行、抛错，并被回退逻辑吞掉。`history.ts` 现在通过一个 `seekSurface` 辅助函数取出并 `bind` 到该代理，若去掉绑定，tracker 提供方测试即失败。由于两条快速路径共用同一表面，既有 `page()`/`loadOlder` 索引快速路径也首次真正启用——代码未变，只是首次可达。随后的生产测量表明，这次启用在生产库上是回退：938 行会话中 932 行仍是 v0。`messageCut` 在存储物理 seq 空间作答，而 `loadStoredFrom` 的历史分支会用格式目录把整段日志恢复到重排后的空间，于是窗口计划每次尝试都要付一次切点查询加一次整段日志读取（104 万事件会话 6.8 秒）且拿到空页，再回落到观测路径重读一遍日志。现在两条快速路径都先经过 fork 寻址面新增的 `seekable(id)` 探针——`SqliteStore.seekable` 只凭会话行的格式版本作答、不读任何事件行——`readIndexedSuffix` 在 `messageCut` 与任何 `readFrom` 之前调用它。`loadStoredFrom` 的历史分支继续服务整段日志读取者，并写明它无法服务索引分页切点。同轮已解决：`FORK_SURFACE.md`/.zh 现为本轮两个新 fork 模块（窗口化打开、检查点读取与写回）补行，并更新了 `history.ts` 行，与本条目最初标记缺失的登记一致。

### 2026-09-11 — 已发布 v0 会话的 descriptor 版本兼容 / Released-v0 descriptor version compatibility

- EN: The released-v0 edge (`packages/session/session-format-v0-to-v1`) admitted `subagent/descriptor` payloads at version 3 alone, so every v0 Session holding a descriptor written since the fork's version 3 → 4 bump refused migration and, because the store publishes a migrated log before granting write access, refused `persistence.open(id, 'write')` as well — 569 of the 791 affected Sessions were version 4 and 222 version 2, 437 of them above 1,000 stored events, all unable to accept a new turn. The edge now reads the installed version from a fork-owned module (`src/fork/subagent-descriptor-compat.ts`, `CURRENT_SUBAGENT_DESCRIPTOR_VERSION` = 4, asserted equal to `SUBAGENT_DESCRIPTOR_VERSION` by `tests/descriptor-compat.spec.ts` through a relative import of the owning definition rather than a dependency on `@deepseek-ai/dsh-subagent`, whose peers would enter every historical restore), admits the version 4 members `cwd`/`skillFilter` in the frozen inventory and payload rules, and stamps the installed version onto a released older descriptor whose members the installed schema declares for its mode — one-shot payloads keeping only `label` — while an undeclared member, a newer version, or a version below 2 still refuses with `SessionFormatUnsupportedMigrationError`. Descriptor version, schema, and the subagent package are unchanged; `.agents/notes/implemented/bug-fix/2026-09-11-released-v0-descriptor-admission.md`. Verified: `pnpm vitest run packages/session/session-format-v0-to-v1/tests packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` 12 files / 237 tests green (the new store test reproduces `subagent/descriptor 2 uses unsupported descriptor version 4` on the unpatched tree and passes after), `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0, oxlint clean on the format package, scoped coverage 100/100/100/100 on every changed `src` file, and the record lane byte-identical before and after (99 failed / 16 passed / 2 skipped, all failures from the disabled sandbox composition).
- ZH: released-v0 边界（`packages/session/session-format-v0-to-v1`）只接受版本 3 的 `subagent/descriptor` 载荷，于是每个持有版本 3 → 4 提升之后写下的 descriptor 的 v0 Session 都无法迁移，并且由于存储在授予写权限之前先发布迁移后的日志，`persistence.open(id, 'write')` 同样被拒——791 个受影响 Session 中 569 个为版本 4、222 个为版本 2，其中 437 个超过 1,000 条已存事件，全部无法接受新一轮对话。该边界现在从 fork 自有模块读取当前版本（`src/fork/subagent-descriptor-compat.ts`，`CURRENT_SUBAGENT_DESCRIPTOR_VERSION` = 4，由 `tests/descriptor-compat.spec.ts` 相对导入拥有该值的定义来断言其等于 `SUBAGENT_DESCRIPTOR_VERSION`，而不依赖 `@deepseek-ai/dsh-subagent`——后者的同级包会进入每一次历史恢复），在冻结清单与载荷规则中接受版本 4 的 `cwd`/`skillFilter`，并把当前版本盖在成员为当前 schema 按其模式所声明的 released 旧 descriptor 上（one-shot 载荷只保留 `label`），而未知成员、更高版本或低于版本 2 的版本仍以 `SessionFormatUnsupportedMigrationError` 拒绝。descriptor 版本、schema 与 subagent 包均未改动；Agent Note：`.agents/notes/implemented/bug-fix/2026-09-11-released-v0-descriptor-admission.md`。已验证：`pnpm vitest run packages/session/session-format-v0-to-v1/tests packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` 12 文件 / 237 项全绿（新增存储测试在未打补丁的树上复现 `subagent/descriptor 2 uses unsupported descriptor version 4`，打补丁后通过）、`pnpm run typecheck` 退出码 0、格式包 oxlint 干净、改动过的 `src` 文件在限定覆盖运行中均为 100/100/100/100、录制通道改动前后逐字节一致（99 failed / 16 passed / 2 skipped，全部失败来自被禁用的沙箱组合）。

### 2026-09-11 — TurnProcess fold summary retired to upstream's label / TurnProcess 折叠摘要退役为上游标签

- EN: First application of the dual-path rule. `TurnProcessNodeView` builds the same fold disclosure label inline upstream, so the fork's `chat/fork/turn-process-summary.ts` (counted categories + wall-clock duration + folded prefix) is deleted and the view restored to upstream's version; the fork-only `message.turnProcess.collapsed` locale key goes with it. `TurnProcessNodeView.tsx` and `ui-chat`'s `locale.ts` now match upstream exactly. The turn duration stays visible in the footer's usage details. The fold-button expectations in `chat-view.client.spec.tsx` return to upstream's plain label; ui-chat 348/348 and the package typecheck pass.
- ZH: 双路径准则的首次落地。上游在 `TurnProcessNodeView` 内联构建同一折叠标签，故删除 fork 的 `chat/fork/turn-process-summary.ts`（分类计数 + 墙钟时长 + 折叠前缀），视图恢复为上游版本；fork 自有的 `message.turnProcess.collapsed` 字典键随之移除。`TurnProcessNodeView.tsx` 与 `ui-chat` 的 `locale.ts` 现与上游完全一致。轮次时长仍在页脚用量详情中可见。`chat-view.client.spec.tsx` 的折叠按钮断言回到上游的纯标签形式；ui-chat 348/348 全绿，包类型检查通过。

### 2026-09-11 — Dual-path principle and the first full dual-path audit / 双路径准则与首次全量双路径审计

- EN: The fork policy now carries a dual-path rule: when both sides changed one behavior, the fork deletes its copy and adopts upstream's, even where the fork's version measures better — one implementation to maintain, test, and re-apply at each sync beats a marginal edge; a retained copy names the production consumer or reproduced defect upstream's version cannot serve. `FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh` gain the same rule plus a resolved/retained dual-path table. Audit against upstream `dsh-v0.1.5-rc.2` with two read-only investigations: the TurnProcess fold label was the only fully retire-able dual path and is deleted; every other dual path is retained with evidence — paging and the cold-open window (upstream's `paginate`/`follow` lack turn-aligned cuts and fold whole logs), the reflow-stable anchor (upstream re-follows the tail only and clears the anchor at settlement), the mobile drawer (upstream has no phone regime), the sandbox swap (upstream's executors need bwrap or Landlock; this container has neither, so adoption would fail-close every bash call), and the activity/rebuild-churn modules (upstream applies each activity immediately, mints fresh projections and snapshots per rebuild, re-sorts promoted rows each sync, and clones plus hashes sessions per search). Partial overlaps remain as the next adoption candidates: entry/items identity in `snapshot-identity.ts` and the reconcile/recency helpers in `order-stability.ts` duplicate upstream code. The audit also corrected the stale sandbox row: the `sandbox` row is upstream's `@deepseek-ai/dsh-sandbox-local` on both sides and `sandbox-policy` is enabled with `mode: danger-full-access`; only `permission` is disabled.
- ZH: fork 政策新增双路径准则：同一行为两边都改时，删除 fork 版本、采用上游实现，即使 fork 版本更优——每个同步周期要维护、测试、重放的实现只有一个，胜过边际优势；保留的副本必须写明上游无法服务的生产消费者或未修复的已复现缺陷。`FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh` 同步补入该准则与「已解决/保留」双路径表。以两次只读调查对上游 `dsh-v0.1.5-rc.2` 完成审计：唯一可完全退役的双路径是 TurnProcess 折叠标签（已删）；其余双路径均带证据保留——分页与冷开窗口（上游 `paginate`/`follow` 无轮次对齐切点且折叠整段日志）、回流稳定锚点（上游只重跟尾部并在结算时清空锚点）、移动端抽屉（上游没有手机形态）、沙箱替换（上游执行器需要 bwrap 或 Landlock，本容器两者皆无，采用即让每条 bash 调用 fail-close）、活动/重建抖动模块（上游对每个活动立即应用、每次重建新造投影与快照、每次同步重排提升行、每次搜索克隆并哈希会话）。局部重叠仍是下一步采用上游代码的候选：`snapshot-identity.ts` 的 entry/items identity 与 `order-stability.ts` 的 reconcile/recency 辅助函数重复了上游实现。审计同时修正了过期的沙箱行描述：`sandbox` 行两边同为上游的 `@deepseek-ai/dsh-sandbox-local`，`sandbox-policy` 已启用且 `mode` 钉为 `danger-full-access`，仅 `permission` 为 `disabled: true`。

### 2026-09-11 — Governance: retirement deletes, one path per behavior, release-note traversal, second-port verification / 治理：退役即删除、单一实现、release note 遍历、换端口验证

- EN: `AGENTS.md` gains three operating rules beside the dual-path rule. (1) *Retirement deletes the code*: a retired fork surface loses its module, injections, locale keys, config rows, and tests in the same change, and no behavior keeps a fork path beside an upstream path — `FORK_SURFACE.md` keeps one `retired` line as runbook history only. (2) *Release-note traversal* on every upstream sync: the notes between the fork's base tag and the target tag name the protocol, session-format, and API breaks a diff hides, and each implied adaptation is planned before merging; the sync runbook now starts with that step in both languages. (3) A new *Landing changes in the running deployment* section: every product-visible change is exercised end-to-end on a second `dsh web` instance (own `DSH_HOME` and bind patch, e.g. port 3097+) against the built artifacts before production is touched, and the production restart is scheduled from a detached delayed script that probes health and rolls back. The `verify-doc-budgets` ceiling for AGENTS.md rises 2150 → 2450 (the rules are policy, not deletable prose). Verified: no retired surface keeps code (`chunkEntryFor` 0 files, `turn-process-summary` 0 files, detail-sheet CSS gone; the remaining `packChunkRuns`/`submission-policy` hits are upstream's own modules), `verify-doc-budgets` 8/8, doc budget gate green.
- ZH: `AGENTS.md` 在双路径准则之外新增三条运行规则。（1）*退役即删除代码*：退役的 fork 面在同一改动中删除模块、注入、字典键、配置行与测试，任何行为不得同时保留 fork 路径与上游路径——`FORK_SURFACE.md` 只留一行 `retired` 作为 runbook 历史。（2）上游同步必做 *release note 遍历*：基线 tag 到目标 tag 之间的说明点出了 diff 看不出的协议、会话格式与 API 破坏性变更，合并前须规划每项适配；同步 runbook 两种语言的首步均改为该步骤。（3）新增 *Landing changes in the running deployment* 章节：任何产品可见改动先在第二个 `dsh web` 实例（独立 `DSH_HOME` 与 bind patch，如 3097+）上以构建产物完成端到端验证，生产重启由脱离终端的延时脚本调度并自带健康探测与回滚。AGENTS.md 的 `verify-doc-budgets` 上限由 2150 提高到 2450（政策内容不可删减）。已验证：退役面无残留代码（`chunkEntryFor` 0 文件、`turn-process-summary` 0 文件、详情 sheet CSS 已清除；残留的 `packChunkRuns`/`submission-policy` 命中是上游自身模块），`verify-doc-budgets` 8/8 通过。

### 2026-09-11 — Streaming session snapshots publish at frame cadence / 流式会话快照按帧发布

- EN: `Session.publishAssistantEntry`'s `transient` branch (a live chunk presentation) marked the session snapshot dirty with `markDirty()`, whose microtask flush runs once per chunk; every `useSession` consumer therefore re-rendered for each streaming frame (~160/s on a long session). It now calls `markFrameDirty()` — the frame-cadence API the notifier already carries for visible stream state — so a frame's worth of chunks publishes once, with the same final snapshot. New regression test in `session.client.spec.ts`: three chunk frames produce zero notifications before the frame boundary and exactly one after; with the previous call the same test fails `expected 3 to be +0`. Affected suites: session-controller + ui-conversation + ui-chat 1,530/1,530.
- ZH: `Session.publishAssistantEntry` 的 `transient` 分支（实时 chunk 呈现）此前用 `markDirty()` 标记会话快照脏，其微任务冲刷每个 chunk 跑一次，于是每个 `useSession` 消费者逐流式帧重渲染（长会话约 160 次/秒）。现改为 `markFrameDirty()`——notifier 本就为可见流状态提供的按帧 API——一帧内的 chunk 只发布一次，最终快照不变。`session.client.spec.ts` 新增回归测试：三个 chunk 帧在帧边界前零通知、之后恰好一次；改回旧调用时同一测试失败 `expected 3 to be +0`。受影响套件：session-controller + ui-conversation + ui-chat 共 1,530/1,530 全绿。

### 2026-09-11 — Reduced-motion guards for the four unguarded infinite animations / 四处无限动画补上 reduced-motion 守卫

- EN: Measured with CDP traces on the production page: an idle transcript column costs ~1,000 ms of main-thread time per second, of which the running-turn `background-position` shimmer (already guarded) is the single largest paint source; four infinite animations carried no `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` guard, so enabling the preference left them running. Guards added to `ui-primitives/StateDot.cell` (static mid-phase `opacity: 0.6` keeps the ongoing state readable), `ui-conversation/InputBar.pending`, `ui-conversation/TodoPanel.glyphProgress`, and `ui-input-trigger/MenuView.skeletonBar`, following the pattern upstream already uses in its other animation files. The guards are inert unless the reader enables the preference, so the default rendering path is unchanged. Verified: ui-primitives + ui-conversation + ui-input-trigger 1,369/1,369.
- ZH: 在生产页面用 CDP trace 实测：空闲转录列每秒消耗约 1,000 ms 主线程，其中运行中回合的 `background-position` 流光（已有守卫）是最大的绘制来源；另有四处无限动画缺少 `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` 守卫，导致开启偏好后它们仍在跑。现为 `ui-primitives/StateDot.cell`（保留静态中间相 `opacity: 0.6`，运行态仍可读）、`ui-conversation/InputBar.pending`、`ui-conversation/TodoPanel.glyphProgress`、`ui-input-trigger/MenuView.skeletonBar` 补上守卫，写法与上游其余动画文件一致。守卫仅在读者开启该偏好时生效，默认渲染路径不变。已验证：ui-primitives + ui-conversation + ui-input-trigger 共 1,369/1,369 全绿。

### 2026-09-11 — Stable message-actions hook restores the rewind button / 稳定动作条钩子恢复 rewind 按钮

- EN: Upstream's commit ("reshape the usage trigger as an icon-row pill"), carried in by the `dsh-v0.1.5-rc.2` sync, removed the `data-time-hover-root` marker. The fork's `dsh-rewind` plugin anchors its per-message rewind button there, so `actionContainerOf()` returned null for every row and the plugin rendered nothing — verified on the live page (`[data-time-hover-root]` 0, `.dsh-rewind-trigger` 0, no console error). `MessageIconActions`'s action strip now carries `data-message-actions` as a stable, additive hook for third-party actions; the plugin prefers it and keeps the legacy marker as a fallback.
- ZH: 上游提交（"reshape the usage trigger as an icon-row pill"，随 `dsh-v0.1.5-rc.2` 同步进入）删除了 `data-time-hover-root` 标记。fork 的 `dsh-rewind` 插件把每条消息的 rewind 按钮锚定在它上面，于是 `actionContainerOf()` 对每一行都返回 null、插件什么都不渲染——线上实测：`[data-time-hover-root]` 为 0、`.dsh-rewind-trigger` 为 0、控制台无报错。`MessageIconActions` 的动作条现带 `data-message-actions` 作为第三方动作的稳定、纯新增钩子；插件优先用它，并保留旧标记作为回退。

### 2026-09-12 — Deployment-scoped reduced-motion opt-in / 部署级 reduced-motion 开关

- EN: The reduced-motion guards answer the browser-wide `prefers-reduced-motion`, so using them meant changing that setting for every site. The deployment now carries its own opt-in: `?reduce-motion=1` stores the choice (`=0`/`=false` clears it) and each boot marks `<html data-reduce-motion>` from the shell kernel before any entry activates; `packages/client/web/src/base.css` then completes animations and transitions in one step under that marker (not `animation: none`, so transition-driven state machines still settle). Unmarked readers render exactly as before, and the browser setting stays untouched. Verified: shell suite 20/20 (stored choice, URL override, valueless parameter, blocked storage, root marker).
- ZH: reduced-motion 守卫响应的是浏览器全局的 `prefers-reduced-motion`，因此使用它等于为所有站点改设置。现在部署自带开关：`?reduce-motion=1` 保存选择（`=0`/`=false` 清除），每次启动由 shell 内核在任何条目激活前给 `<html data-reduce-motion>` 打标；`packages/client/web/src/base.css` 在该标记下把动画与过渡压成一步完成（而非 `animation: none`，依赖 transition 的状态机仍会结算）。未打标的读者渲染与从前完全一致，浏览器设置不受影响。已验证：shell 套件 20/20（存储选择、URL 覆盖、无值参数、存储被禁、根标记）。

### 2026-09-12 — Reduced motion becomes the deployment default / reduce-motion 改为本部署默认

- EN: Follow-up to the opt-in above, on the owner's call: the deployment now runs without motion by default. The shell kernel marks `<html data-reduce-motion>` on every boot, so no URL is needed; `?reduce-motion=0` stores a per-browser opt-out and `?reduce-motion=1` clears it. The browser-wide `prefers-reduced-motion` setting stays untouched, and other sites are unaffected. Measured effect of the marker (3096, staged build): idle main thread 993 → 76 ms/s, Paint/PrePaint/Layout/Commit → 0, running-turn shimmer renders as a solid label.
- ZH: 承接上一条开关，按 owner 决定：本部署默认无动效。shell 内核每次启动即给 `<html data-reduce-motion>` 打标，无需任何 URL；`?reduce-motion=0` 写入按浏览器的退出选择，`?reduce-motion=1` 清除。浏览器全局的 `prefers-reduced-motion` 不受影响，其他站点照旧。标记的实测效果（3096 暂存构建）：空闲主线程 993 → 76 ms/s，Paint/PrePaint/Layout/Commit 归零，运行中回合流光渲染为常亮文字。

### 2026-09-12 — Deployment rule: the second instance owns its Session database / 部署规范：第二实例必须自持会话库

- EN: A staging `dsh web` instance started with its own `DSH_HOME` still opened the production SQLite database: the staged profile declared the production `session-persistence-sqlite` path, and the staged `profiles/<name>` entry was a symlink back to the production profile, so the isolation never reached the Session store. Both instances then appended to the same Session, their cursors diverged, and both sides lost turns to append conflicts (`session <key> append starts at seq N / stored next seq M`). AGENTS.md's "Landing changes in the running deployment" section now requires the second instance to own its session database — an own file, or a snapshot copy of the production database when the real Sessions are needed — and to prove single ownership with an fd scan before starting.
- ZH: 一个只用独立 `DSH_HOME` 启动的 staging `dsh web` 实例仍然打开了生产 SQLite 库：staging 的 profile 写着生产 `session-persistence-sqlite` 路径，而 staged `profiles/<name>` 是指回生产 profile 的软链，隔离从未落到会话库上。两个实例于是同时向同一 Session 追加，游标分叉、双方 turn 都因追加冲突失败（`session <key> append starts at seq N / stored next seq M`）。AGENTS.md 的 "Landing changes in the running deployment" 一节现要求第二实例自持会话库——独立文件，或在需要真实会话时用生产库快照副本——并在启动前用 fd 扫描证明只有一个持有者。

### 2026-09-12 — Composer keystrokes stop re-rendering the conversation column / 打字不再重渲染整个会话列

- EN: `ConversationRoot` subscribed to the whole input store for one purpose — handing the input dock its owner props — and `ConversationSession` subscribed to the input store plus the stored draft for one purpose — seeding and mirroring the composer draft. Because both components render the column, every keystroke re-rendered the header, the loaded transcript window (`conversation.view`), and the composer stack; per-keystroke work therefore grew with the history window a reader had loaded. The subscriptions now live in two null leaves (`InputDockSlot`, `DraftMirror`), so a keystroke re-renders the dock and the draft leaf alone; the dock still receives the live snapshot and the seed effect keeps its mount-only semantics. Measured on an isolated staging instance against the unpatched build at the same page state (1,519 loaded rows / 30,404 nodes): per-keystroke JS 3 ms → 1 ms; total interaction stayed at one 16 ms frame on both builds, so this is a work reduction, not a measured end-to-end win on the measuring host. `ui-conversation` 395/395.
- ZH: `ConversationRoot` 订阅整个 input store 只为了一件事——把 input dock 的 owner props 传下去；`ConversationSession` 订阅 input store 与持久化草稿也只为了一件事——播种与镜像 composer 草稿。由于这两个组件渲染的就是整列，每敲一键都会重渲染 header、已加载的 transcript 窗口（`conversation.view`）与 composer 栈，于是每键开销随读者加载的历史窗口增长。订阅现下沉到两个 null 叶子（`InputDockSlot`、`DraftMirror`），一次按键只重渲染 dock 与草稿叶子；dock 仍拿到实时快照，播种 effect 保持 mount-only 语义。在隔离 staging 上、与未打补丁构建在同一页面状态对比（1,519 行 / 30,404 节点）：每键 JS 3 ms → 1 ms；两种构建的总交互延迟都停留在 16 ms 一帧，因此这是减少工作量，不是本机可测的端到端收益。`ui-conversation` 395/395 全绿。

### 2026-09-12 — Deployment theme palette in the shell / 部署主题配色进 shell

- EN: The deployment's skin was applied by the third-party `@linxin666/dsh-client-ui-skin-center` plugin, which the plugin cleanup removed, so the locally installed "夕港 / Harbor" tokens (and the owner's blue recolor in its `patches.css`) stopped applying while the skin files stayed on disk. Upstream's theme plugin owns only the light/dark/system preference and content font size — there is no palette extension point — so the palette now lives in the shell: `packages/client/web/src/fork/deployment-theme.ts` resolves `?theme=` / `localStorage['dsh:theme']` and marks `<html data-dsh-theme="harbor">` before any entry activates, `packages/client/web/src/fork/themes.css` carries the token overrides, and the boot kernel applies both. The palette is the deployment default (first frame, no flash); `?theme=default` stores a per-browser opt-out and `?theme=harbor` clears it. The skin's wallpaper and runtime hooks stay out of the repository (third-party art, and no applier once the plugin is gone). Web shell suite: 26/26 with the new spec.
- ZH: 本部署的皮肤此前由第三方插件 `@linxin666/dsh-client-ui-skin-center` 应用，插件清理删掉了它，于是本地安装的「夕港 / Harbor」token（以及你在其 `patches.css` 里的蓝白重染）不再生效，而皮肤文件仍在磁盘上。上游 theme 插件只管 light/dark/system 偏好与正文字号、没有配色扩展点，因此配色改由 shell 承载：`packages/client/web/src/fork/deployment-theme.ts` 解析 `?theme=` / `localStorage['dsh:theme']` 并在任何条目激活前给 `<html data-dsh-theme="harbor">` 打标，`packages/client/web/src/fork/themes.css` 承载 token 覆盖，启动内核负责调用。配色是本部署默认（首帧即生效、无闪屏）；`?theme=default` 写入按浏览器的退出选择，`?theme=harbor` 清除。皮肤的壁纸与运行时 hooks 不入库（第三方美术资产，且插件删除后已无应用方）。web shell 套件含新增 spec 共 26/26。

### 2026-09-12 — Reduced-motion default keeps transitions / reduced-motion 默认不再压过渡

- EN: The deployment default overrode `transition-duration` to 1 ms on every element alongside the animation overrides. The right Sidebar's panel machinery observes its own `transform` transition and reports "panel transform transition is unavailable" once that transition has already completed at observation time: seven `apps/web/tests/sidebar-right.e2e.ts` cases fail on any fresh full build (a long-lived checkout hides it behind its stale built `packages/client/web/lib/base.css`). `packages/client/web/src/base.css` now completes animations only; transitions keep their authored duration, so panels still slide while animation-driven motion stays off. Verified on a fresh full build: `sidebar-right.e2e.ts` 15/15, web shell suite 26/26.
- ZH: 部署默认此前连同动画覆盖一起把所有元素的 `transition-duration` 压到 1 ms。右侧栏的面板机制会观测自身的 `transform` 过渡，过渡在观测时已经结束时它报 "panel transform transition is unavailable"：任何一次全新完整构建上，`apps/web/tests/sidebar-right.e2e.ts` 有 7 个用例失败（长期存在的检出会因为陈旧的 `packages/client/web/lib/base.css` 而掩盖这一点）。`packages/client/web/src/base.css` 现在只压动画；过渡保持作者时长，面板照常滑动，动画动效仍然关闭。已在全新完整构建上验证：`sidebar-right.e2e.ts` 15/15，web shell 套件 26/26。

### 2026-09-12 — Theme palette reaches the app subtree / 主题配色覆盖到应用子树

- EN: The palette was declared only on the marked root element. Upstream declares the alias tokens on `body` (light) and `body[data-ds-dark-theme]` (dark), and a declaration on the body beats the value inherited from the root for that whole subtree — in dark mode the deployment rendered a dusk root behind panels still on upstream's `#151517`/`#f9fafb`. `themes.css` now declares the palette on the marked root and on its body (`:root[data-dsh-theme="harbor"] body`, specificity 0-2-1 over upstream's 0-1-1), so both modes carry it. Verified on the deployed build: body tokens `--dsw-alias-bg-base` `#141a2e`, `--dsw-alias-brand-primary` `#6ea8fe` with `body[data-ds-dark-theme]` set.
- ZH: 配色此前只声明在带标记的根元素上。上游把 alias token 声明在 `body`（亮色）与 `body[data-ds-dark-theme]`（暗色），而 body 上的声明会整棵子树盖过从根继承下来的值——暗色模式下本部署于是出现「根是暮蓝、面板仍是上游 `#151517`/`#f9fafb`」。`themes.css` 现在把配色同时声明在带标记的根与其 body（`:root[data-dsh-theme="harbor"] body`，特异性 0-2-1 高于上游的 0-1-1），两种模式都生效。已在部署构建上验证：`body[data-ds-dark-theme]` 存在时 body 的 `--dsw-alias-bg-base` = `#141a2e`、`--dsw-alias-brand-primary` = `#6ea8fe`。

### 2026-09-12 — Running-Session indicator stays readable / 运行中指示器恢复可读

- EN: The deployment's reduced-motion default completes animations in one step, so `ui-primitives/StateDot`'s pixel chase held its 0.15 base opacity and the running-Session indicator beside the sidebar rows was barely visible (measured on the live page: all eight cells at `opacity: 0.15`). The chase is the only signal that a Session is running, so `StateDot.module.css` now keeps it alive under `:root[data-reduce-motion]`, and the deployment palette lifts `--dsw-static-deepseek-450` — the static step the chase and the default file-type icon ride — from `rgb(86 134 254)` to the brand-light `#8fbaff`. Verified: ui-primitives + web shell suites 862/862.
- ZH: 部署的无动效默认把动画压成一步完成，于是 `ui-primitives/StateDot` 的点阵追逐停在基础 `opacity: 0.15`，侧栏行旁的「运行中」指示器几乎看不见（线上实测：八个格子的 `opacity` 全是 0.15）。该追逐是 Session 正在运行的唯一信号，因此 `StateDot.module.css` 现在在 `:root[data-reduce-motion]` 下保留它；同时部署配色把 `--dsw-static-deepseek-450`（追逐与默认文件类型图标所用的静态步进）从 `rgb(86 134 254)` 提到品牌浅蓝 `#8fbaff`。已验证：ui-primitives + web shell 套件 862/862。
### 2026-09-12 — Turn Process fold returns for partial history / 部分历史下恢复 Turn Process 折叠

- EN: The 0.1.5-rc.2 sync silently reverted the fork's removal of the `historyIncomplete` suppression in `ChatNodeSeat.tsx` (applied 2026-08-28, re-applied 2026-09-04 over upstream's `perf(ui-chat): scope turn process updates`): the surface had no `FORK_SURFACE.md` row and no `// Fork patch` marker, so merging upstream's version of the file reclaimed it. Upstream gates `processWindowReady` on `!historyIncomplete`, which withholds the fold control and hides no members until every page is loaded; `PAGE_MESSAGES = 8` keeps `hasMore` true in nearly every real session, so since the 2026-09-11 deployment a closed Turn rendered every intermediate Tool call, Context row, and Assistant message inline. Restored the surface (prop and gate dropped; `ChatView` stops passing `hasMore`), added the `// Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md)` marker, registered the row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh`, restored the two partial-history tests (red without the change: `expected null not to be null`), and refreshed the recorded web goldens the replay lane compares, which the 2026-09-11 label retirement had left on the deleted `Collapsed … · {duration}` text while the view renders upstream's inline label. The `2026-08-29-turn-process-fold-with-partial-history` note now records the retained half only, with the stale summary facts rewritten in place.
- ZH: 0.1.5-rc.2 同步静默回退掉 fork 在 `ChatNodeSeat.tsx` 里对 `historyIncomplete` 抑制门的删除（8/28 打上，9/4 在上游 `perf(ui-chat): scope turn process updates` 之上重放）：这条表面既没有 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 行、也没有 `// Fork patch` 标记，合并上游版本的文件时被一并收回。上游把 `processWindowReady` 门控在 `!historyIncomplete` 上，全部页载完之前不显示折叠控件、也不隐藏成员；`PAGE_MESSAGES = 8` 让真实会话的 `hasMore` 几乎恒为真，于是自 9/11 那次部署起，已关闭回合把中间的 Tool 调用、Context 行与 Assistant 消息全部内联展开。本次恢复该表面（删除 prop 与门；`ChatView` 不再传 `hasMore`），补上 `// Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md)` 标记，在 `FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh` 登记该行，恢复两个部分历史折叠测试（无此改动即红：`expected null not to be null`），并刷新 replay lane 所比较的录制 web 金样——9/11 的标签退役把金样留在已删除的 `Collapsed … · {duration}` 文案上，而视图渲染的是上游内联标签。`2026-08-29-turn-process-fold-with-partial-history` Note 现在只记录保留下来的那一半，过期的摘要事实就地改写。

### 2026-09-12 — Markdown preview takes Tokyo Night, with element-level contrast / markdown 预览改用 Tokyo Night 并补元素级对比度

- EN: The markdown palette was Dracula, whose warm purple-gray code surface (`#282a36`) floated on the dusk-blue page, and the shell overrode no `--dsw-alias-link`, so links stayed on upstream's blue. The palette now follows the "tokyo-night" theme in the owner's xbot checkout, shifted onto the dusk surfaces: code block `#161a2e` with an `#1f2540` banner, links cyan `#7dcfff`, comment/punctuation steps lifted for the darker background, and the full `--shiki-*` set retuned (keyword `#bb9af7`, string `#9ece6a`, function `#7aa2f7`, constant `#ff9e64`). Four rules scoped to `[data-chat-flow]` add the contrast no upstream token carries — two-tier headings (h1/h2 `#7aa2f7`, h3+ `#c8d3f5`; links stay cyan so a heading never reads as a link), inline-code chips (`#9ece6a` on `#2b3358` with an `#45507a` border), and table headers (`#1f2540`). The campaign ran through a served reference page (`/md-theme-lab.html`) that renders the real markdown surface with five candidate palettes and live token pickers, so the owner picked without restarting the deployment. Web shell suite: 26/26.
- ZH: markdown 配色此前是 Dracula，暖紫灰的代码面（`#282a36`）浮在暮蓝页面上；shell 也没有覆盖 `--dsw-alias-link`，链接还是上游蓝。现在改用 owner 的 xbot 检出里 "tokyo-night" 主题并贴到暮蓝上：代码块 `#161a2e`、顶栏 `#1f2540`、链接青 `#7dcfff`，注释与标点步进为更深的底抬高，`--shiki-*` 全套重调（keyword `#bb9af7`、string `#9ece6a`、function `#7aa2f7`、constant `#ff9e64`）。另有四条限定在 `[data-chat-flow]` 内的规则补上没有上游 token 承载的对比度——两级标题（h1/h2 `#7aa2f7`，h3+ `#c8d3f5`；链接保持青色，标题不会被误读成链接）、行内代码芯片（`#9ece6a` 落在 `#2b3358` 上、边框 `#45507a`）、表头（`#1f2540`）。选色过程走的是部署内提供的参考页 `/md-theme-lab.html`：它按真实 markdown 渲染五套候选配色并提供实时取色器，owner 无需重启即可定稿。web shell 套件 26/26。


### 2026-09-12 — Hash-named shell assets ship immutable / 内容哈希的 shell 资产带 immutable 缓存


- EN: `frontend-static` answered every dist file with `content-type` alone, so the browser re-fetched the shell's content-hashed JavaScript and CSS on every reload while the plugin bundles under `/plugins` were already `immutable` (measured on the running deployment before the change: a reload transferred 438 KiB gzip in shell JavaScript and CSS — 415 KiB + 23 KiB — out of 448 KiB total, decoding to 1,352 KiB, which Node's zlib at level 1 (the `compression` middleware's own implementation) reproduces to the byte on those files (209,631 B + 214,790 B; the `gzip` CLI's level 1 is a different implementation and lands ~10 KiB higher)). Hits inside the Vite hash-named output directories (`assets/`, `preview/`; `apps/web/vite.config.ts` fixes `[hash]` for every emitted name) now answer `cache-control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`; index responses and the stable-named root entries (`favicon.svg`, `manifest.webmanifest`, `preview.html`, the served reference page) keep their previous headers, so a rebuilt bundle or a changed index still reaches the browser. Upstream exposes no cache-header configuration on this server, hence the marked patch registered in `FORK_SURFACE.md`. Evidence: new REAL-composition spec assertions in `packages/host/frontend-static/tests/frontend-static.spec.ts` (red without the change: `expected null to be 'public, max-age=31536000, immutable'`); `tsc -b packages/host/frontend-static/tsconfig.json` and oxlint clean; a second `dsh web` instance on 3097 serving the deployment's real dist answers `immutable` on `/assets/index-*.js`, `/assets/vendor-*.js`, `/assets/vendor-*.css`, and `/preview/bootstrap-*.js`, and no cache header on `/`, `/index.html`, `/favicon.svg`, `/manifest.webmanifest`, or the other stable-named root entries; its warm reload transferred 10 KiB in total (was 448 KiB) with the app mounting at 435 ms cold and 350 ms warm.
- ZH: `frontend-static` 此前对任何 dist 文件只回 `content-type`，于是浏览器每次刷新都重新拉取 shell 里带内容 hash 的 JS 与 CSS，而 `/plugins` 下的插件包早已是 `immutable`（改动前在线上实测：一次刷新的 shell JS 与 CSS 共 438 KiB gzip（415 KiB + 23 KiB），占该次刷新总传输 448 KiB，解压后 1,352 KiB；本地用 Node 的 zlib level 1（`compression` 中间件自己的实现）跑同一批文件逐字节吻合（209,631 B + 214,790 B；`gzip` 命令行的 level 1 是另一套实现，约高 10 KiB））。现在命中 Vite 内容哈希输出目录（`assets/`、`preview/`；`apps/web/vite.config.ts` 为每个产物名固定 `[hash]`）的请求带 `cache-control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`；index 响应与稳定命名的根文件（`favicon.svg`、`manifest.webmanifest`、`preview.html`、部署内的参考页）保持原有头，重建的 bundle 或变更的 index 仍能到达浏览器。上游这个服务没有缓存头配置项，因此是带标记的补丁，并登记在 `FORK_SURFACE.md`。证据：`packages/host/frontend-static/tests/frontend-static.spec.ts` 新增真实组合断言（无此改动即红：`expected null to be 'public, max-age=31536000, immutable'`）；`tsc -b packages/host/frontend-static/tsconfig.json` 与 oxlint 干净；3097 起的第二个 `dsh web` 实例服务部署真实 dist，`/assets/index-*.js`、`/assets/vendor-*.js`、`/assets/vendor-*.css`、`/preview/bootstrap-*.js` 都带 `immutable`，而 `/`、`/index.html`、`/favicon.svg`、`/manifest.webmanifest` 以及其他稳定命名的根文件无缓存头；其热刷新总传输 10 KiB（此前 448 KiB），冷加载挂载 435 ms、热加载 350 ms。

- EN: `frontend-static` answered every dist file with `content-type` alone, so the browser re-fetched the shell's content-hashed JavaScript and CSS on every reload while the plugin bundles under `/plugins` were already `immutable` (measured on the running deployment before the change: a reload transferred 415 KiB + 23 KiB gzip, 1,351 KiB decoded, which Node's zlib at level 1 (the `compression` middleware's own implementation) reproduces to the byte on those files (209,631 B + 214,790 B; the `gzip` CLI's level 1 is a different implementation and lands ~10 KiB higher)). ### 2026-09-12 — Sync-safety registry pass, and the restart belongs to the human / 同步安全登记补课，重启交还给人

- EN: An audit of `FORK_SURFACE.md` against the tree found one dead row, one half-live row, one unregistered fork module, two CSS surfaces no row named, and fork-only CSS blocks that no marker would have protected from a wholesale upstream stylesheet adoption. (1) The `ui-chat` StatsLine row named `StatsLine.tsx`, which upstream deleted when it replaced the stats strip with the two icon pills; the row is retired. (2) The Conversation-header row claimed a session-log capsule hide whose rule died when upstream moved that control into the header's 28px ellipsis menu; it now names both surviving media blocks (the 767.98px drawer-opener clearance and the 560px phone trim). (3) `packages/session/session-format-v0-to-v1/src/fork/subagent-descriptor-compat.ts` — with one marked delegation in each of `dispositions.ts`, `migration.ts`, `payload-validation.ts`, `validation.ts`, and `tests/relationships.spec.ts` — had no row; it has one now. (4) Two CSS surfaces no row named are registered: the 767.98px drawer-opener clearance (folded into the Conversation-header row) and the TurnProcess disclosure pill, whose upstream class names mean a stylesheet adoption silently restores the divider. (5) Every fork-only CSS block now carries a `/* Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md) */` marker: four reduced-motion guards, two `overflow-anchor: none` declarations, the two narrow-viewport blocks, the composer safe area, the `AppFrame.module.css` mobile-shell section, and the pill. The same pass takes the deployment restart out of agent hands: `AGENTS.md` now ends an agent's deployment work at built artifacts (reset, rebuild, verify the bundle, report the restart as pending) and leaves restarting `dsh-web` to the human, because a restart drops every live Session's stream and the browser reader attached to it.
- ZH: 用 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 对照代码做了一次审计，发现一行已死、一行只剩一半、一个 fork 模块没登记、两条 CSS 表面无行可查，以及一批"整体采用上游样式表就会静默丢掉"的 fork 专属 CSS 块没有任何标记保护。（1）`ui-chat` StatsLine 行指的是 `StatsLine.tsx`，上游用两个图标胶囊取代统计条时已删除该文件；该行退役。（2）对话头部那一行声称隐藏 session-log 胶囊，但那条规则在上游把控件挪进头部 28px 省略号菜单时就死了；现在它同时描述存活的两个媒体块（767.98px 的抽屉入口让位与 560px 的手机收紧）。（3）`packages/session/session-format-v0-to-v1/src/fork/subagent-descriptor-compat.ts`（连同 `dispositions.ts`、`migration.ts`、`payload-validation.ts`、`validation.ts`、`tests/relationships.spec.ts` 各一处标记委托）此前没有任何登记行，现已补上。（4）两条此前无行可查的 CSS 表面完成登记：767.98px 的抽屉入口让位（并入对话头部行），以及 TurnProcess 折叠控件的胶囊样式——后者沿用上游类名，整体采用上游样式表会静默恢复成通栏分隔线。（5）所有 fork 专属 CSS 块现都带 `/* Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md) */` 标记：四处 reduced-motion 守卫、两处 `overflow-anchor: none`、两处窄视口媒体块、composer 安全区、`AppFrame.module.css` 的移动端 chrome 段，以及上述胶囊样式。同一轮把部署重启交还给人：`AGENTS.md` 现在把 agent 的部署工作终止在"构建产物就绪"（reset、重建、验证 bundle、报告重启待办），重启 `dsh-web` 只由人执行——重启会中断每个活跃 Session 的流以及挂在它上面的浏览器读者。

### 2026-09-12 — Documentation gates green again / 文档门禁恢复全绿

- EN: Four `doc-quick` gates and six `doc-sync` leaves were red on the fork's `master`, so every later change session had to separate its own breakage from standing noise; every cause sat in a fork-owned or fork-generated surface. Active Agent Notes still linked `implemented/` paths whose targets the 0.1.5-rc.2 merge had moved into the frozen `archived/` tree, and the archived `2026-07-30-web-queue-steer-action` triplet had been edited after sealing; that triplet is repaired by restoring its sealed bytes, not by re-sealing the manifest, because the archive policy forbids editing frozen artifacts and CI's `DSH_ARCHIVE_BASE_REF` guard rejects a re-seal against the pre-change base. Eleven bilingual pairs carried stale `.i18n.yaml` records from the same merge (the merge kept upstream's older records while the content combined both parents); one of them, `packages/client/connection/README.zh.md`, was a real gap — the optional-browser-auth commit had updated only the English side — so its Chinese counterpart sentence was written before recording. `docs/config-catalog.md`, `docs/persistence-catalog.md`, `docs/subsystems/skills.{md,zh.md}`, `apps/cli/composition.md`, `docs/event-producer-consumer.{md,zh.md}`, and two generated `api-catalog.ts` files were stale against their generators: the fork's own surfaces had never reached them (the `defer`, `browserAuth`, and `websocketPerMessageDeflate` config fields, the `session-persistence-sqlite` package, the web-search providers, the skill `restrict` registrar, the sandbox executor substitution, and `session-persistence-sqlite` as an event consumer). `packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/README.md` exceeded the 100-word Summary budget, linked four dead note paths plus a config-catalog anchor its package never had, and still listed the removed `preparedSessionCacheSize` field. Five fork modules were missing or carrying stale `@param`/`@returns` tags under `verify-export-jsdoc`; `nextSessionOrderAccount` needed its binding-pattern parameter replaced by an identifier, which the gate requires. Verified: `pnpm run test:docs` 16/16 (was 12/16) and every previously red `doc-sync` leaf exits 0 (`doc graphs`, `export jsdoc`, `config catalog`, `persistence catalog`, `cordis catalog`, `cordis inspect catalog`, `archived agent notes`, `doc-typecheck`); `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0 for the one source-signature change; the touched client/host specs pass (98 tests).
- ZH: fork 的 `master` 上有 4 个 `doc-quick` 门禁与 6 个 `doc-sync` 叶子长期为红，之后每个会话都得先把自己的破坏和既有噪声分开；所有成因都落在 fork 自有或 fork 生成的表面上。活跃 Agent Note 仍指向 `implemented/` 路径，而 0.1.5-rc.2 合并已把目标移入冻结的 `archived/` 树；归档 triple `2026-07-30-web-queue-steer-action` 在封存后又被改过——该 triple 的修法是**恢复封存字节**而不是重签 manifest：归档策略禁止改动冻结产物，且 CI 的 `DSH_ARCHIVE_BASE_REF` 守卫会拒绝相对变更前基线的重签。十一个双语对带着同一次合并留下的过期 `.i18n.yaml` 记录（合并保留了上游更旧的记录，而内容是两侧父提交的合成）；其中 `packages/client/connection/README.zh.md` 是真实缺口——可选浏览器认证的提交只改了英文侧——因此先补写中文对应句再记录。`docs/config-catalog.md`、`docs/persistence-catalog.md`、`docs/subsystems/skills.{md,zh.md}`、`apps/cli/composition.md`、`docs/event-producer-consumer.{md,zh.md}` 与两个生成的 `api-catalog.ts` 相对各自生成器均已过期：fork 自有表面从未进入其中（`defer`、`browserAuth`、`websocketPerMessageDeflate` 三个配置字段、`session-persistence-sqlite` 包、四个 web-search 提供方、skill 的 `restrict` 注册器、sandbox 执行器替换，以及作为事件消费者的 `session-persistence-sqlite`）。`packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/README.md` 的 Summary 超出 100 词预算、有四个失效注记链接与一个该包从未存在的 config-catalog 锚点，并且还在列出已删除的 `preparedSessionCacheSize` 字段。五个 fork 模块在 `verify-export-jsdoc` 下缺 `@param`/`@returns` 或标签过期；`nextSessionOrderAccount` 需要把解构参数改成标识符参数——这是该门禁的硬要求。已验证：`pnpm run test:docs` 16/16（此前 12/16），此前为红的 `doc-sync` 叶子全部 exit 0（`doc graphs`、`export jsdoc`、`config catalog`、`persistence catalog`、`cordis catalog`、`cordis inspect catalog`、`archived agent notes`、`doc-typecheck`）；唯一一处源码签名改动在 `pnpm run typecheck` 下 exit 0；受影响的 client/host 规格共 98 个测试通过。

### 2026-09-12 — Review follow-ups for the documentation-gate cleanup / 文档门禁清理的评审后续

- EN: Three review rounds over the gate cleanup found differences the gates cannot see inside pairs whose records had just been re-recorded, so those records asserted a consistency the content did not have. Fixed: the regenerated source line numbers in the `event-producer-consumer` Chinese table (5 cells), the Chinese `tool-catalog` tool count (10 → 9), four Chinese `config-catalog` identifiers (`compaction`, `userQuestions`, `CredentialProvider`, `SettingsProvider`), a dropped clause in each of the `web/web` and `session-query-sqlite` Chinese READMEs, two Chinese-only gateway caveats the source backs (so the English gained the equivalent sentences), CJK/Latin spacing in the `ui-layout` Chinese README, the `persistence-catalog` Chinese citations (`types.ts:55/:53` → `:58/:56`), and punctuation/blank-line nits. Finally, `docs/subsystems/client-modules.{md,zh.md}` described two scheduling phases and a graph revision over rows and descriptors alone, while the source has three phases (`bootstrap`, `application`, `deferred`) and hashes the entries, the batches, and the trusted authorities. Every touched pair was re-recorded; `pnpm run test:docs` stays 16/16.
- ZH: 针对门禁清理的三轮评审发现：刚被重录的配对里仍存在门禁看不见的差异——记录于是声称了内容并不具备的一致性。已修：`event-producer-consumer` 中文表的 5 个行号、中文 `tool-catalog` 的工具数（10 → 9）、中文 `config-catalog` 的四个标识符（`compaction`、`userQuestions`、`CredentialProvider`、`SettingsProvider`）、`web/web` 与 `session-query-sqlite` 中文 README 各一个漏译从句、gateway 两条仅中文有且源码支持的事实（因此英文补齐对应句）、`ui-layout` 中文 README 的 CJK/拉丁间距、`persistence-catalog` 中文引用（`types.ts:55/:53` → `:58/:56`），以及若干标点与空行小项。最后，`docs/subsystems/client-modules.{md,zh.md}` 此前写两个调度阶段、且说图 revision 只对 row 与描述求哈希，而源码是三个阶段（`bootstrap`、`application`、`deferred`），且对 entries、batches 与 trusted authorities 求哈希。所有涉及的配对已重录；`pnpm run test:docs` 保持 16/16。

### 2026-09-12 — In-place history rewrite for edit-and-resend / 编辑重发：会话历史就地重写

- EN: The fork's rewind semantics change from forking a new session to rewriting the same one in place: editing a historical user message and sending discards that message's whole turn and every event after it from the durable log on admission, and the edited prompt continues the same session id — header, workspace, and title stay — at that cut; the submitting client rebuilds its window from the rewritten log. Landed in four layers: an optional rewrite capability `SessionHandle.truncate?(toSeq, options?)` on the upstream persistence seam (contract landed by the Lead); the fork's SQLite backend implementing `truncateLog` (one immediate transaction: rows from the cut deleted, the one packed row spanning the cut rewritten to keep its members below it, `incrementRevision`, commit; rollback on failure; the handle re-bases its cursor and its shrink guard now keys on the stored revision); host orchestration in `commands.prompt` (request carries `rewriteFrom`, queue-only, live-Agent-idle-only, subagent and inbox-pending refusal, `disposeAgent` → write-handle `truncate` → `close`, response carries `rewrote`; the pure cut validation lives in the fork module `src/fork/rewrite-history.ts`); and the client input machine (`InputState.rewriteFrom` armed beside the draft, carried by the native submission, cleared with the committed draft and restored with it on failure) plus the window rebuild (`resync()` when the host reports `rewrote === true`; a visible `session/rewrite-missed` failure when a not-yet-restarted host admits an armed prompt without rewriting).
- EN: Why: the plugin cannot do this itself — it has no host half, `ctx.agents` exposes no disposal, and agent lifetime is fiber-owned — so the rewrite runs on the host, atomically with admission (one operation, one settlement point), and the intent rides the prompt request instead of a staging RPC. Fork-policy trade-offs: one new fork surface row; the shipped JSONL backend does not implement the optional capability and a consumer that needs the rewrite fails loud (`session/rewrite-unsupported`); the cut is the seq of the armed turn's `turn/start` extended backwards over that message's admission `agent/inbox/spliced` inserts — never mid-turn, never into the fork-inherited prefix (`resolveRewriteCut` refuses both), and never leaving the replaced message's own admission record behind; a message queued while an earlier turn was still running keeps its insert inside that earlier turn, which the cut retains, so the rewrite appends the one balancing removal splice (`planInboxRepair`) that keeps the folded inbox empty and stops the resumed driver from replaying the replaced message as a new turn; the rewrite also discards the Session's projection-cache record, and the cache's seed reads now refuse rows at or past the durable prefix they would fold — the cache may be behind a log, never ahead of it.
- ZH: fork 的 rewind 语义从开新会话改为就地重写同一会话：编辑一条历史 user message 并发送时，接纳操作先把该消息所在的整个轮次及其后的所有事件从持久日志中丢弃，编辑后的提示词在同一会话 id 上、于该切点处续写——header、workspace 与标题保留——提交客户端随后从重写后的日志重建窗口。分四层落地：上游持久化 seam 新增可选重写能力 `SessionHandle.truncate?(toSeq, options?)`（契约由 Lead 落地）；fork 的 SQLite 后端实现 `truncateLog`（一个 immediate 事务：删除切点起的行、重写唯一跨切点的 packed 行以保留其切点以下成员、`incrementRevision`、提交；失败回滚；句柄游标重置，收缩守卫改按已存 revision 判定）；host 编排在 `commands.prompt`（请求携带 `rewriteFrom`，仅 queue、仅 live Agent idle、拒绝子代理与 inbox 有排队者，`disposeAgent` → 写句柄 `truncate` → `close`，响应携带 `rewrote`；纯函数切点校验在 fork 模块 `src/fork/rewrite-history.ts`）；以及客户端输入机（`InputState.rewriteFrom` 与草稿并存，随原生提交上行，随已提交草稿清空、失败时随草稿恢复）与窗口重建（host 回报 `rewrote === true` 时 `resync()`；未重启的旧 host 接纳了武装提示词却未重写时产生可见的 `session/rewrite-missed` 失败）。
- ZH: 为什么：插件自身做不到——没有 host 半身、`ctx.agents` 没有 dispose、agent 生命周期归 fiber——因此重写在 host 上、与接纳原子完成（一次操作、一个结算点），意图随 prompt 请求上行而非另设 staging RPC。fork 政策取舍：新增一条 fork 分叉面；随产品交付的 JSONL 后端不实现该可选能力，需要重写的消费者 fail loud（`session/rewrite-unsupported`）；切点是武装轮次的 `turn/start` seq，并向前越过该消息自己的准入 `agent/inbox/spliced` 事件——绝不切入轮次中间、绝不进入 fork 继承前缀（`resolveRewriteCut` 对两者拒绝），也绝不留下去被替换消息的准入记录；上一轮运行中排队发送的消息，其 insert 留在上一轮内、会被切点保留，因此重写会补写唯一一条中和移除 splice（`planInboxRepair`），让折叠后的 inbox 保持为空、恢复后的 driver 不会把被替换消息重放成新轮次；重写同时丢弃该会话的投影缓存记录，缓存的种子读也改为拒绝水位在目标 durable prefix 之上的行——缓存只可落后于日志、绝不可超前。

### 2026-09-12 — Worktrees stay inside the repository / worktree 只能开在本仓库内

- EN: The working-tree rule said "a git worktree under `.worktrees/<slug>`" but never forbade creating one elsewhere, and three were: a tree at `/data/user/nolanho/code/.worktree/session-chunk-rows` (a sibling root, so it never appeared in the main checkout's `.worktrees/` scan) and ad-hoc trees under `/tmp` that outlived the sessions that made them. The paragraph now pins the path to `<repo>/.worktrees/<slug>`, forbids `/tmp`, sibling paths, and other clones, and requires removing the worktree once its branch is landed: only that directory is git-ignored and swept with the repository, so a worktree elsewhere survives as a dead tree nothing links to. The `AGENTS.md` budget rises 2680 → 2760 words so the file keeps its 5% headroom at 2,621 words.
- ZH: 工作区规则此前只写"在 `.worktrees/<slug>` 下的 git worktree"，没有禁止建在别处，结果出现了三处：`/data/user/nolanho/code/.worktree/session-chunk-rows`（另一个根目录，因此永远不会出现在主检出的 `.worktrees/` 扫描里），以及若干留在 `/tmp` 下、创建它们的会话结束后仍然存活的临时工作树。该段现在把路径钉死为 `<repo>/.worktrees/<slug>`，禁止 `/tmp`、检出旁的路径与其他 clone，并要求分支落地后即移除工作树：只有该目录被 git 忽略、随仓库一起清理，建在别处的工作树只会成为无人引用的死目录。`AGENTS.md` 预算 2680 → 2760 词，使文件在 2,621 词时仍保有 5% 余量。

### 2026-09-12 — Phone settings dialog and conversation header / 手机端设置弹窗与会话 header

- EN: Two phone-viewport defects are fixed in the client. (1) The settings modal layer rendered inside ui-layout's mobile drawer, whose `transform: translate(0)` while open makes the drawer the containing block for the layer's `position: fixed; inset: 0`; measured at 412×905 the overlay resolved to 319×905, the panel to the drawer's width, the nav rail kept its 188px and the content column got 131px — labels wrapped to one or two words, the panel's `overflow: hidden` clipped the trailing controls, the close button shrank to 16×28, and the mask covered only the drawer, so the page behind the "modal" stayed tappable. The layer now mounts under `document.body` through the fork module `packages/client/ui-settings-general/src/client/fork/portal.tsx` (one marked import plus one marked call in `SettingsPanel`; `react-dom` and `@types/react-dom` devDependencies, matching ui-primitives), and the new `@media (max-width: 560px)` block makes the panel a full-screen single column whose nav rail pins its title and scrolls its cells horizontally, with a 36px close and `env(safe-area-inset-*)` padding. (2) The conversation header's `.headerCorner { margin-right: -16px }` is tuned for the desktop 28px right padding while the fork's phone block sets 12px, so the corner control ended 4px past the viewport at 360/412/480/560px; the phone block now resets that margin, restores the session breadcrumb with an 88px floor and `.crumb`'s ellipsis instead of hiding it, and makes `.headerActions` shrinkable. Verified: 39 files / 457 tests green in the two touched packages, the full client suite at two pre-existing ui-tool failures that fail identically on the base tree, the portal and both media blocks pinned by new specs, and a live 412×905 check on a second instance. Ledger correction: an audit of the 2026-08-27 mobile pass found six of its seven rule groups absent from the tree (and two re-solved elsewhere), so that Agent Note now carries a status-at-this-revision section and the rules that ship today live in `2026-09-12-phone-settings-dialog-and-header`.
- ZH: 修复客户端的两处手机视口缺陷。(1) 设置模态层此前渲染在 ui-layout 的移动抽屉内部，抽屉打开时的 `transform: translate(0)` 使其成为该层 `position: fixed; inset: 0` 的包含块；在 412×905 下实测 overlay 解到 319×905、panel 等于抽屉宽度、导航轨仍占 188px、内容列只剩 131px——标签折成一两词一行，panel 的 `overflow: hidden` 裁掉右侧控件，关闭按钮被挤到 16×28，遮罩只盖住抽屉，使"模态"背后的页面仍可点击。该层现经 fork 模块 `packages/client/ui-settings-general/src/client/fork/portal.tsx` 挂载到 `document.body`（`SettingsPanel` 中一处带标记的 import 加一处带标记的调用；`react-dom` 与 `@types/react-dom` 进 devDependencies，与 ui-primitives 一致），新增的 `@media (max-width: 560px)` 块把面板变成铺满视口的单列：导航轨标题固定、单元横向滚动，关闭按钮 36px，导航与选项区带 `env(safe-area-inset-*)` 内边距。(2) 会话 header 的 `.headerCorner { margin-right: -16px }` 是按桌面 28px 右内边距配的，而 fork 的手机块把它设为 12px，于是角落控件在 360/412/480/560px 下都越出视口 4px；手机块现在归零该外边距、以 88px 底线加 `.crumb` 省略号恢复会话面包屑（不再隐藏），并让 `.headerActions` 可收缩。已验证：两个涉及包 39 文件 / 455 测试全绿；完整客户端套件仅剩两个 ui-tool 既有失败（与基线树表现一致）；portal 与两个媒体块由新增测试钉住；并在第二个实例上以 412×905 实机校验。账本校正：对 2026-08-27 移动端治理的审计发现其七组规则中有六组不在树中（另有两组已由别处重解），因此那条 Agent Note 现带有"本版本实际情况"一节，而实际交付的规则记录在 `2026-09-12-phone-settings-dialog-and-header`。

### 2026-09-12 — Collapsed Think rows read the reasoning character count / 折叠态 Think 行改显推文字符数

- EN: A collapsed Think disclosure in the conversation transcript no longer previews reasoning prose; the row renders the block's exact, locale-grouped character count (`思考 · 1,234 字符` / `Think · 1,234 characters`) while the expanded body keeps upstream's complete text, so the collapsed process scan carries no reasoning prose. `ReasoningRow.tsx` loses upstream's `firstLine`/`latestLine` and the `data-follow-end` streaming pin — a fixed-width count needs no horizontal tail alignment, and the in-component layout scheduler / smooth-scroll path the stress note described is gone with it — while the sweep animation, `data-state` attributes, and the visually-hidden running label stay. The count is the block text's UTF-16 code-unit length, this package's existing `json.truncated` convention, and it grows live while the block streams. `token-format.ts` gains `formatExactCount`, which owns the locale digit grouping, with `formatExactTokens` delegating; `locale.ts` gains `message.think.chars` (`{count} 字符` / `{count} characters`). Registered as a Tier C row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`, recorded by the new Agent Note `2026-09-12-collapsed-think-character-count`, and the active stress note's superseded horizontal-tail claims were rewritten in place with its bilingual record re-taken. The 19 comparable Think-bearing `snapshots/web/**/*.expected.md` goldens take their count lines from a `DSH_SNAPSHOT=refresh` run filtered to those rows; a wholesale refresh was rejected because it also rewrites unrelated fork drift (the removed Access-mode chip, the TurnProcess duration label) and machine-specific session rows into the corpus. Eight further Think-bearing goldens keep the prose form deliberately: the three `snapshots/web/seeded-history/*.expected.md` files (six rows) belong to the suite the fork excludes from the lane, and the two `apps/web/tests/expected/steer-all/*.expected.md` files (two rows) belong to a scenario whose `session.jsonl` fixture is missing, so the block's full length beyond the shown first line is unknown. Evidence: the focused spec fails 3/4 against the old source and passes 4/4 with it (`npx vitest run packages/client/ui-chat/tests/reasoning-row.client.spec.tsx`); `packages/client/ui-chat` + `packages/client/connection` are 45 files / 517 tests green (`packages/client/ui-chat` alone 30 files / 346 tests before the rebase); `pnpm run test:gui` is 379 passed with only the two pre-existing `ui-tool` file-routing failures that fail identically on the base tree; `tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` and `pnpm run build` exit 0 in both the worktree and the deployment clone; oxlint is 0/0 on the touched areas; `verify-export-jsdoc`, `verify-agent-note-format` (349 notes), `verify-agent-note-classification`, and `verify-translation-pairing` (818 pairs) are clean, with `verify-client-ui-i18n`'s single `fork/open-file-routing.ts` finding identical on the base tree; `DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay pnpm run test:web` was run and its 48 failed files / 66 failed tests are the lane's pre-existing set (22 `persisted replay` session-fixture mismatches from the fork composition's `danger-full-access` rows and missing permission events, 27 `llm-replay` fixtures missing or unconsumed, 10 harbour-palette colour assertions, 6 environment timeouts, 2 fixture-match failures) — no failure names the Think row, and the committed session fixtures it diffs are unchanged from upstream; the surgical count lines were cross-checked independently (the `present-svg` session's reasoning blocks measure 242 and 56 code units, exactly the golden's counts); and the change was exercised in a real browser on a second `dsh web` instance before the deployment clone was rebuilt — 127.0.0.1:3099, its own `DSH_HOME` and a `.backup` snapshot of the production session database, single ownership proven on both databases (`/proc/*/fd`), 81 collapsed Think rows all reading their count, and the expanded `216 characters` row rendering a 216-character body. The opt-in stress lane could not execute on this tree: `vitest.web-stress.config.ts` is the only browser config missing the shared `standardDecoratorPlugin`, so its suite fails at load with `SyntaxError: Invalid or unexpected token` (reproduced with the unmodified file), and adding the plugin lets it load but its `?fixture` page never renders — both pre-existing; the lane's assertion was moved to the displayed count but stays unexecuted.
- ZH: 对话转录中折叠的 Think 披露行不再预览推理正文；该行渲染块的精确、按区域分组的字符数（`思考 · 1,234 字符` / `Think · 1,234 characters`），展开正文保持上游的完整文本，因此折叠态的过程扫读不再夹带推文散文。`ReasoningRow.tsx` 去掉上游的 `firstLine`/`latestLine` 与 `data-follow-end` 流式钉住——固定宽度的计数不需要横向跟尾，压力注记描述过的组件内布局调度器与平滑滚动路径也随之消失——而扫光动画、`data-state` 属性与视觉隐藏的运行中标签保留。计数取块文本的 UTF-16 码元长度，即本包既有 `json.truncated` 的约定，并在推理流式到达期间实时增长。`token-format.ts` 新增 `formatExactCount`（承载按区域分组的精确整数格式化），`formatExactTokens` 委托给它；`locale.ts` 新增 `message.think.chars`（`{count} 字符` / `{count} characters`）。登记为 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 的 Tier C 行，由新注记 `2026-09-12-collapsed-think-character-count` 记录；仍在生效的压力注记里已被取代的横向跟尾表述就地改写，其双语记录重录。19 个可比较、含 Think 行的 `snapshots/web/**/*.expected.md` golden 只取一次 `DSH_SNAPSHOT=refresh` 运行中这些行的计数；整体刷新被否决，因为它会把无关的 fork 漂移（被移除的访问模式 chip、TurnProcess 时长标签）与本机相关的会话行一并写入语料。另有 8 个含 Think 行的 golden 有意保留散文形态：三个 `snapshots/web/seeded-history/*.expected.md`（6 行）属于本 fork 从该泳道排除的套件；两个 `apps/web/tests/expected/steer-all/*.expected.md`（2 行）所属场景缺少 `session.jsonl` fixture，因此该块在所示首行之后的完整长度未知。证据：聚焦规格在旧源码上 3/4 失败、改动后 4/4 通过（`npx vitest run packages/client/ui-chat/tests/reasoning-row.client.spec.tsx`）；`packages/client/ui-chat` 与 `packages/client/connection` 共 45 文件 / 517 测试全绿（rebase 前 `packages/client/ui-chat` 单独为 30 文件 / 346 测试）；`pnpm run test:gui` 379 个文件通过，仅剩两个在基线树上同样失败的既有 `ui-tool` 文件路由失败；`tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` 与 worktree、部署 clone 两处的 `pnpm run build` 均 exit 0；oxlint 在改动面为 0/0；`verify-export-jsdoc`、`verify-agent-note-format`（349 条注记）、`verify-agent-note-classification`、`verify-translation-pairing`（818 对）干净，`verify-client-ui-i18n` 唯一一处 `fork/open-file-routing.ts` 发现与基线树一致；`DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay pnpm run test:web` 已运行，48 个失败文件 / 66 个失败测试均为该泳道既有的失败集（22 处 `persisted replay` 会话 fixture 不一致，源于 fork 组装的 `danger-full-access` 行与缺失的 permission 事件；27 处 `llm-replay`
