是唯一缺少共享 `standardDecoratorPlugin` 的浏览器配置，套件在加载阶段即报 `SyntaxError: Invalid or unexpected token`（用未改动的文件复现），补上插件后可加载但其 `?fixture` 页面始终不渲染——两者均为既有问题；该泳道的断言已改为显示的字符数，但保持未执行状态。

### 2026-09-12 — Phone header metadata budget / 手机 header 的元信息预算

- EN: A session with background jobs grew the phone header row from 30px to 78px: the job badge's label ("9 background jobs") wrapped inside the actions box (shrunk to 113px on a 412px viewport) into a four-line column, the lineage chip and the agent-preset chip clipped mid-word, and the session title was left two characters — and the row re-wrapped on every job start or finish. The phone block now carries a metadata budget: the session-header agent-preset label renders nothing below 560px (`.label { display: none }` — static context with no control, a glyph identical for every preset, and a `title` that carries the description rather than the name); the job badge keeps its count on one line and ellipsizes (`white-space: nowrap` + `text-overflow: ellipsis` + `min-width: 0` on the count, and a `max-width: 100%` bound on `.trigger`, whose inline-flex shrink-to-fit otherwise overflows the row instead of narrowing), still without clipping so the badge's absolutely positioned menu survives; and the lineage slot is deliberately left visible, because for a subagent session it renders that session's own title and it is the only phone path into the subagent catalog (the drawer tree and session search filter subagent children out). The session title keeps its 88px floor. Verified: 42 files / 572 tests green in the three touched packages, the full client suite at its two pre-existing ui-tool failures, and live 412x905 measurements on an isolated instance running a fresh production database snapshot — title row 30px, the preset label absent, the lineage chip present (clipped but tappable), and a badge injected with the real served classes one line at 28px with the ellipsis engaged instead of overflowing the trailing utilities.
- ZH: 带后台任务的会话把手机 header 行从 30px 撑到 78px：job 徽标的文案（"9 background jobs"）在收缩到 113px 的操作盒里折成四行列，lineage chip 与 agent-preset chip 被截断在词中，会话标题只剩两个字符——而且每次任务开始或结束都重新折行。手机块现在带有元信息预算：会话 header 的 agent-preset 标签在 560px 以下不渲染（`.label { display: none }`——无控件的静态上下文、图标对每个 preset 都相同、`title` 携带的是描述而非名称）；job 徽标保持计数单行并省略（计数上 `white-space: nowrap` + `text-overflow: ellipsis` + `min-width: 0`，并在 `.trigger` 上加 `max-width: 100%` 宽度约束——其 inline-flex 的 shrink-to-fit 否则会溢出该行而不是收窄），且仍不裁切，因此徽标绝对定位的菜单存活；lineage slot 则刻意保持可见，因为在子代理会话里它渲染该会话自己的标题，且它是进入子代理目录的唯一手机路径（抽屉树与会话搜索都过滤子代理子节点）。会话标题保留 88px 底线。已验证：三个涉及包 42 文件 / 572 测试全绿；完整客户端套件仅剩既有两个 ui-tool 失败；并在隔离实例（生产数据库全新快照）上 412×905 实测——标题行 30px、preset 标签不渲染、lineage chip 在位（可裁切但可点）、以真实交付 class 注入的徽标单行 28px 且省略号生效而非溢出尾部控件。
### 2026-09-13 — Collapsed Think rows also read the settled stream duration / 折叠态 Think 行再显示落定耗时

- EN: The collapsed Think row appends the block's settled streaming duration to the character count (`思考 · 1,234 字符 · 12.3s` / `Think · 1,234 characters · 12.3s`), filling the space the removed prose preview left; a running block and a block whose message carries no stream records keep the count alone. The duration costs no extra session read: the settled `assistant/message` already embeds its exact timed model stream (`AssistantStreamRecord[]`, `time0` plus per-delta `dt`), which the client loads for the transcript anyway, so the Assistant Definition folds `reasoningSpans` per block index inside `settleMessage` — several packed `reasoning-chunks` runs for one index union into one span, and a retry resets them with the rest of the step state — and `AssistantChatData.reasoningSpans` carries them through `AssistantMarkdown` to `ReasoningRow`, where `message.think.charsWithDuration` composes the localized count with the package's existing compact duration formatter. Registered in the `FORK_SURFACE.md` Tier C row and in the updated Agent Note. Evidence: `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/reasoning-row.client.spec.tsx` pins count-only while running, `1,234 字符 · 12.3秒` once settled with a span, and no duration without one; the definition spec folds an `assistant/message` whose packed stream holds two `reasoning-chunks` runs for one index and asserts the union span; the two touched specs plus the chat-view suite are 153 tests green; `pnpm run test:gui` is 381 passed with only the two pre-existing `ui-tool` file-routing failures that fail identically on the base tree; `tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` and `pnpm run build` exit 0 in both the worktree and the deployment clone; package oxlint is 0/0, `verify-export-jsdoc` is clean, and `verify-client-ui-i18n` keeps its one pre-existing finding; the web goldens carrying Think rows were refreshed for the new copy through the same Think-row-only filter — 18 files gain `· {{duration}}` (the lane normalizes the rendered duration), while `cordis-tool-round` correctly keeps count-only rows because its recorded stream holds no reasoning deltas; and the change was exercised in a real browser on a second `dsh web` instance (127.0.0.1:3101, own `DSH_HOME`, `.backup` snapshot of the production session database, single ownership proven on both databases) where all 81 collapsed Think rows rendered a count and a duration, and the rendered values match spans computed independently from the copied database (for example `2,620 characters · 2.7s` and `14,546 characters · 17.7s`). The deployment clone was rebuilt and its `lib/client.js` verified to carry the new copy after the second instance had been checked.
- ZH: 折叠的 Think 行在字符数之后追加该块落定的流式耗时（`思考 · 1,234 字符 · 12.3s` / `Think · 1,234 characters · 12.3s`），把此前被移除的文本预览腾出的空间用起来；运行中的块、以及消息未携带流记录的块只显示计数。该耗时不需要任何额外会话读取：落定的 `assistant/message` 本已内嵌精确的带时间戳模型流（`AssistantStreamRecord[]`，`time0` 加每片 `dt`），而客户端本就为转录加载这条消息；于是 Assistant Definition 在 `settleMessage` 内按块索引折出 `reasoningSpans`——同一索引的多段 packed `reasoning-chunks` 合并为一段跨度，重试时随该步骤其余状态一并重置——再由 `AssistantChatData.reasoningSpans` 经 `AssistantMarkdown` 传到 `ReasoningRow`，由 `message.think.charsWithDuration` 把本地化计数与本包既有的紧凑时长格式化器组合。已登记进 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 的 Tier C 行与更新后的 Agent Note。证据：`packages/client/ui-chat/tests/reasoning-row.client.spec.tsx` 固定了运行中只显示计数、落定且有跨度时显示 `1,234 字符 · 12.3秒`、无跨度时不显示耗时；定义规格折叠一条 packed 流中含两段同索引 `reasoning-chunks` 的 `assistant/message`，断言合并后的跨度；两个受改动规格加 chat-view 套件共 153 个测试通过；`pnpm run test:gui` 381 个文件通过，仅剩两个在基线树上同样失败的既有 `ui-tool` 文件路由失败；worktree 与部署 clone 两处的 `tsc -b tsconfig.client.json`、`pnpm run build` 均 exit 0；该包 oxlint 0/0、`verify-export-jsdoc` 干净、`verify-client-ui-i18n` 保持其唯一既有发现；含 Think 行的 web golden 沿用「只替换 Think 行」过滤方式刷新为新文案——18 个文件带上 `· {{duration}}`（该泳道会把渲染出的耗时归一化为该标记），而 `cordis-tool-round` 正确地保持只显示计数，因为其录制流里没有任何推理增量；改动在第二个 `dsh web` 实例（127.0.0.1:3101，自有 `DSH_HOME`、生产会话数据库的 `.backup` 快照，两个数据库的单属关系均已用 `/proc/*/fd` 证明）经真实浏览器验证：81 个折叠 Think 行全部同时渲染计数与耗时，且渲染值与从副本数据库独立计算出的跨度逐条吻合（例如 `2,620 characters · 2.7s`、`14,546 characters · 17.7s`）。第二实例验证完成后才重建部署 clone，并确认其 `lib/client.js` 携带新文案。
### 2026-09-13 — The generic tool row accepts a caller-supplied expanded body / 通用工具行接受调用方自定义的展开体

- EN: The fork's out-of-tree `dsh-apollo` client plugin takes over the `wait_subagent` card and must keep the generic collapsed row every other tool renders, so it cannot use a keyed `tool.call.toolview` entry (that replaces the whole row) and must not copy the row's markup (that drifts on every upstream sync); the package could not serve it either, because `@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-ui-tool`'s `/client` entry exported only `apply`/`inject` and contract types while `ToolRow`'s expanded body accepted only the argument JSON (`bodyRaw`) and the flattened result text (`output`). `ToolRow` now takes an optional `bodyContent: ReactNode`: it renders as the expanded body's first child at block level, outside the Input/Output card (`ioCard`/`ioText`), and the caller owns its layout; it replaces the default Input (arguments) section, so the argument JSON is not formatted, no Input label is printed, and no card renders when there is also no output; and its presence makes the row expandable on its own. The Output section, the card chain, the collapsed summary, the error summary and suffix rules, and the Inspect pill are unchanged, and a row without the prop renders exactly as before (the `inputBody` value substitutes for `cardBody`, so no existing path changes). `GenericToolCard` forwards the same optional prop, and the browser entry exports `GenericToolCard` and `GenericToolCardProps`; the value export is deliberate and user-signed-off, and is the recorded exception to the client export-discipline rule, because the consumer is package-external and has no slot-based route to the shared row. Registered as a Tier C row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`, recorded by the Agent Note `2026-09-13-tool-row-custom-body-content`, documented in `docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md`. Evidence: `pnpm vitest run packages/client/ui-tool/tests` is 309 passed with only the two pre-existing file-routing failures (`chat-code-subcalls`, `toolview-slot`) that fail identically on the base tree (verified by stashing the change and re-running both specs); the four added specs in `tests/tool-row.client.spec.tsx` pin expansion from `bodyContent` alone, the absent Input section and card, the unformatted arguments, the surviving Output section, and the `GenericToolCard` forward. `ui-tool` sits on the GUI-debt coverage exclusion list (`vitest.config.ts`), so no per-file threshold covers these branches.
- ZH: fork 的包外客户端插件 `dsh-apollo` 接管 `wait_subagent` 卡片，而折叠态必须保持其他所有工具共用的通用行，因此不能用 keyed `tool.call.toolview` 条目（那会替换整行），也不能复制该行标记（每次上游同步都会漂移）；而包本身此前也服务不了它——`@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-ui-tool` 的 `/client` 入口只导出 `apply`/`inject` 与契约类型，`ToolRow` 的展开体又只接受参数 JSON（`bodyRaw`）与展平的结果文本（`output`）。`ToolRow` 现接受可选的 `bodyContent: ReactNode`：以块级元素渲染为展开体的第一个子节点，位于 Input/Output 卡片（`ioCard`/`ioText`）之外，排版由调用方自负；它取代默认的「参数」（INPUT）段——不再格式化参数 JSON、不打印 INPUT 标签、同样没有输出时也不渲染卡片——它的存在本身就让该行可展开。OUTPUT 段、卡片链、折叠摘要、错误摘要与后缀规则、Inspect 胶囊均不变，不传该属性的行与改动前渲染完全一致（以 `inputBody` 替代 `cardBody`，既有路径无一改变）。`GenericToolCard` 透传同一可选属性，浏览器入口导出 `GenericToolCard` 与 `GenericToolCardProps`；这个值导出是有意为之并已获用户同意，作为客户端导出纪律规则的登记例外——消费方在包外，且没有任何基于 slot 的途径取得这个共享行。已登记为 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 的 Tier C 行，由 Agent Note `2026-09-13-tool-row-custom-body-content` 记录，并在 `docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md` 中说明。证据：`pnpm vitest run packages/client/ui-tool/tests` 309 通过，仅剩两个既有的文件路由失败（`chat-code-subcalls`、`toolview-slot`），二者在基线树上表现完全相同（已用 stash 移除改动后重跑两个规格验证）；`tests/tool-row.client.spec.tsx` 新增的四个规格钉住「仅凭 `bodyContent` 即可展开」「INPUT 段与卡片缺席」「参数不被格式化」「OUTPUT 段保留」与「`GenericToolCard` 透传」。`ui-tool` 位于 GUI 债务覆盖排除清单（`vitest.config.ts`），因此这些分支不受 per-file 阈值覆盖。

### 2026-09-13 · tool row: owner-supplied collapsed summary
- **What** — `GenericToolCard` (already exported for out-of-tree toolviews) accepts an optional `summary` that replaces the arguments-derived collapsed summary; `ToolRow` keeps owning the row itself. Added with the `bodyContent` extension so a toolview that owns the expanded body also owns its one line (`wait_subagent` shows `等待 N 个子代理` / `已结束` instead of the raw id/timeout JSON).
- **Why** — the derived summary for a tool whose arguments carry no readable key is the argument JSON; the Web client ignores host `presentCall`, so only the toolview can supply it.

### 2026-09-13 — In-place rewrite keeps the Session in every client's list / 就地重写期间会话不再从客户端列表消失

- EN: Sending an edited message dropped the GUI to the new-Session landing frame (workspace chip "Choose workspace", inert composer, no transcript) for the whole rewrite, then snapped back to the conversation. The rewrite disposes the Session's Agent to release its persistence write ownership before truncating, and `AgentHandle.dispose()` removes the session from the store; the Session Layer pairs that with `session/disposed`, which this controller relays as `api-session/removed`. Clients read that as the Session leaving the list — the summary drops, `sessions.list` masks `current` to `undefined`, the session-current binding goes absent, and `ConversationRoot` renders `hero = sessionId === undefined` — while the same request immediately resumes an Agent under the same id and `api-session/added` restores it, so the frame's length is the rewrite's own duration (agent teardown, truncation, projection discard, resume) and a large live Session on the deployment shows it far longer than a small one. The new fork module `packages/api/session-controller/src/fork/rewrite-hold.ts` holds that announcement across the dispose-to-rebuild window: `SessionCommandController.prompt` enters the hold before `rewriteHistory` and leaves it after `resolveAgent` settles, `index.ts`'s `session/disposed` relay asks `commands.deferRemoval` before emitting, and a window that ends with no live Agent re-announces the removal so a rewrite that failed after disposal still tells clients the Session is gone. The relay is untouched for every other disposal path. Registered in the `FORK_SURFACE.md` Tier C orchestration row and recorded by the Agent Note `2026-09-13-rewrite-removal-hold`. Evidence: a host spec over the production composition (real sqlite persistence, the production Agent loop, the real projection cache) pins that a rewrite over a LIVE Agent resolves `rewrote: true` with `api-session/removed` never emitted — the same case fails against the unpatched source with `["session-rewrite-quiet"]` — plus a unit spec for the hold's four transitions including the failed-rewrite fallback; the focused suites are 82 files / 1382 tests green and `pnpm run typecheck` exits 0; and a real browser drove the same edit-and-resend on a second isolated `dsh web` instance (127.0.0.1:3097, own `DSH_HOME`, own database) with a 20 ms DOM probe around the send — before the change the landing frame appears at t=62 ms with the composer unmounted, after it no frame and no blank conversation state at any sample, the transcript swapping straight from the replaced message to the edited one.
- ZH: 发送编辑后的消息会让 GUI 在整个重写期间退回新会话画面（工作区 chip 显示 "Choose workspace"、输入框不可用、转录消失），随后又跳回对话。重写需要先拆除该会话的 Agent 以释放持久化写入所有权再截断，而 `AgentHandle.dispose()` 会把该 session 从 store 中移除；Session 层为它配对发出 `session/disposed`，本控制器把它中继为 `api-session/removed`。客户端据此认为会话离开了列表——摘要被删除、`sessions.list` 把 `current` 掩为 `undefined`、会话-current 绑定变为 absent、`ConversationRoot` 于是渲染 `hero = sessionId === undefined`——而同一请求随即以相同 id 重新 resume Agent，`api-session/added` 把它恢复，所以画面的时长就是重写自身的时长（agent 拆除、截断、投影缓存丢弃、resume），部署环境里体积大的活跃会话上明显比小会话久。新的 fork 模块 `packages/api/session-controller/src/fork/rewrite-hold.ts` 在 dispose 到 rebuild 的整段窗口内扣住该公告：`SessionCommandController.prompt` 在 `rewriteHistory` 之前进入 hold、在 `resolveAgent` 结算之后离开；`index.ts` 的 `session/disposed` 中继在发出前先询问 `commands.deferRemoval`；若窗口结束时没有活跃 Agent，则重新公告该移除，使 disposal 之后失败的重写仍如实告知客户端会话已消失。对其它所有 disposal 路径，中继行为不变。已登记进 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 的 Tier C 编排行，并由 Agent Note `2026-09-13-rewrite-removal-hold` 记录。证据：一个跑生产组装（真实 sqlite 持久化、生产 Agent 循环、真实投影缓存）的宿主规格钉住「对活跃 Agent 的重写返回 `rewrote: true` 且从未发出 `api-session/removed`」——同一用例在未打补丁的源码上失败，实测 `["session-rewrite-quiet"]`——另有一个单元规格覆盖 hold 的四种迁移，含重写失败的回退；聚焦套件 82 文件 / 1382 测试全绿，`pnpm run typecheck` exit 0；真实浏览器在第二个隔离的 `dsh web` 实例（127.0.0.1:3097，自有 `DSH_HOME` 与自有数据库）上以 20 ms DOM 探针驱动同一次「从此处编辑并重发」——改动前该画面在 t=62 ms 出现且输入框被卸载，改动后不再出现该画面、任何采样都没有空的对话状态，转录从被替换的消息直接切到编辑后的消息。

### 2026-09-16 — 委派纵深的两处修复 / Two repairs in the delegation stack

- EN: Restore the two driver call sites the 0.1.5-rc.2 sync dropped — `request.skillFilter` into `applyChildComposition` and `request.cwd` into `childSessionMeta`, both in `subagent-in-process-driver/src/index.ts`. Without them every one-shot delegation silently ran in the parent's workspace with the parent's full skill catalog (foreground delegations and the non-continuable background path), while the fork's own `child-isolation.spec.ts` could not pass. That spec's setup also registered `sessionProjections` twice now that `mountAgentLoopTestDependencies` provides it; removed. `FORK_SURFACE.md` now names all four call sites so the next sync replays them.
- ZH: 恢复 0.1.5-rc.2 同步时丢掉的两处驱动接线 —— `subagent-in-process-driver/src/index.ts` 里 `request.skillFilter` 进 `applyChildComposition`、`request.cwd` 进 `childSessionMeta`。缺了它们，每一次 one-shot 委派都在父工作区里、带着父的全量技能目录静默运行（前台委派与非 continuable 的后台路径），而 fork 自己的 `child-isolation.spec.ts` 也不可能通过。该 spec 的 setup 在 `mountAgentLoopTestDependencies` 已自带 `sessionProjections` 后重复注册，已移除。`FORK_SURFACE.md` 现在登记全部四处调用点，避免下次同步再漏。
- EN: `tool-agent-team`'s `team:policy` section rendered membership with the throwing `membership()`; an agent admitted by the `maybeInstall` creation-time race (delegation depth 2, descriptor after `agent/created`) then failed its next request render, so the child turn ended as `reason.kind = 'error'` and its parent reported `subagent run failed` for a child that had actually run. The section now renders empty for a non-member, pinned by a regression test.
- ZH: `tool-agent-team` 的 `team:policy` 段用会抛错的 `membership()` 解析归属；被 `maybeInstall` 创建期竞态放行进来的 agent（委派深度 2，descriptor 晚于 `agent/created`）在下一次请求渲染时失败，整轮以 `reason.kind = 'error'` 收尾，父侧遂对「其实已经跑过」的子代理报 `subagent run failed`。现在非成员渲染为空段，并补了回归测试。

### 2026-09-16 — Subagent model aliases move into the official delegation tool / 子代理模型别名迁入官方委派工具

- EN: The delegation surface's model-alias face moves from the out-of-tree `dsh-subagent-dispatch` plugin into the official `packages/subagent/tool-subagent`, registered as a Tier C row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`. Two fork-owned modules land with it — `src/fork/model-alias.ts` (178 lines: the alias table's load-time validation, the model-facing `model` parameter and its wording, and the route merge over the instance's `agentOptions`) and `src/fork/delegation-scope.ts` (108 lines: the per-child `cwd`/`skillFilter` load-time validation and request mapping) — and `src/index.ts` grows +126/-14, every line a marked injection: four upstream expressions are replaced in place (the choice description, the parameter-face arm, `requiresRoutePreflight`, and the `requestedChildAgentOptions` call), and the rest is additive. Four config keys, all omission-preserving: `models` (the instance's alias table; when present it REPLACES upstream's `provider`/`model`/`reasoning_effort` model face with one optional `model` alias parameter, and the table IS that instance's authorization surface — an alias outside it fails loud at call time naming the allowed ones, and the session-level `subagent-model-selection` policy plays no part for an alias instance); `defaultModel` (the alias a call without `model` uses; must be in the table, defaulting to its first entry); `cwd` (child workspace, validated at load as a non-empty existing absolute directory and carried on the start request, where the in-process driver folds it into the child's session header); and `skillFilter` (`allow` xor `deny`, validated in full at load: object, known keys, string arrays, never both directions).
- ZH: 委派面的模型别名面从包外插件 `dsh-subagent-dispatch` 迁入官方 `packages/subagent/tool-subagent`，并登记为 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 的 Tier C 行。随迁两个 fork 自有模块——`src/fork/model-alias.ts`（178 行：别名表的加载期校验、面向模型的 `model` 参数与文案、以及在实例 `agentOptions` 之上的路由合并）与 `src/fork/delegation-scope.ts`（108 行：每子代理 `cwd`/`skillFilter` 的加载期校验与请求映射）——`src/index.ts` 增 +126/-14，每一行都是标记注入：4 处上游表达式就地替换（choice 描述、参数面分支、`requiresRoutePreflight`、`requestedChildAgentOptions` 调用），其余为纯新增。四个配置键，全部保持「省略即缺省」：`models`（实例的别名表；存在时**替换**上游面向模型的 `provider`/`model`/`reasoning_effort` 参数为单一可选 `model` 别名，而这张表就是该实例的授权面——面外别名在调用期 fail loud 并枚举允许项，session 级 `subagent-model-selection` 策略对别名实例不参与）；`defaultModel`（调用省略 `model` 时使用的别名；必须在表内，缺省为表首项）；`cwd`（子代理工作区，加载期校验为非空且已存在的绝对目录，随 start request 透传，由进程内驱动落到子会话 header）；`skillFilter`（`allow` 异或 `deny`，加载期全量校验：对象、已知键、字符串数组、不得两者同给）。
- EN: Why: the plugin was the only carrier of a per-instance alias face, so the deployment ran a second `subagent` implementation beside the official tool and its role faces duplicated what the official seam already reaches. The face belongs on the official tool as a `Config` field — the fork's preferred extension point, a `src/fork/` module over an upstream-file copy — so one delegation implementation serves both. The contract holds at both ends: `models` omitted keeps the upstream face, route resolution, and session policy byte-for-byte, so the package's 126 pre-existing upstream tests are unmodified and green beside the 38 new ones (`npx vitest run packages/subagent/tool-subagent/tests` is 6 files / 164 tests passed); `models` with `modelSelectionSettings: true` fails loud at mount, because two model-authorization paths with different semantics cannot coexist; and the alias route's `{provider, model, reasoningEffort}` is preflighted against the live `llm` adapter at call time only, so a mount needs no `llm`.
- ZH: 为什么：插件此前是每实例别名面的唯一载体，于是本部署在官方工具之外还跑着第二份 `subagent` 实现，其角色面重复了官方接缝本已触达的能力。别名面属于官方工具的 `Config` 字段——fork 首选的扩展点，且以 `src/fork/` 模块承载而非复制上游文件——于是一份委派实现同时服务两边。两端契约都成立：省略 `models` 时上游工具面、路由解析与会话策略逐字节不变，因此该包 126 个既有上游用例零改动，与 38 个新用例一同全绿（`npx vitest run packages/subagent/tool-subagent/tests` 为 6 文件 / 164 测试通过）；`models` 与 `modelSelectionSettings: true` 同在时挂载即 fail loud，因为两条语义不同的模型授权路径不能共存；别名路由的 `{provider, model, reasoningEffort}` 只在调用期对 live `llm` 适配器 preflight，故挂载期不需要 `llm`。
- EN: Machine-local activation (deployment state, not repo surface): the nine tool faces — the generic `subagent` tool plus the eight role tools (`roundtable`, `researcher`, `scout`, `tdd-tester`, `implementer`, `reviewer`, `code-quality-reviewer`, `lark`) — are now official `tool-subagent` rows in the `eng` and `research` presets, each carrying its own `models` table, so the presets no longer rely on the plugin; its `$DSH_HOME` profile row still mounts pending removal.
- ZH: 机本地激活（部署态，非仓库面）：9 个工具面——通用 `subagent` 工具加 8 个角色工具（`roundtable`、`researcher`、`scout`、`tdd-tester`、`implementer`、`reviewer`、`code-quality-reviewer`、`lark`）——现为 `eng` 与 `research` preset 里的官方 `tool-subagent` 行，各自携带自己的 `models` 表，故 preset 不再依赖该插件；其 `$DSH_HOME` profile 行仍处于挂载状态，待移除。
- EN: The alias face's config surface gains its fail-loud guard, in a third fork-owned module wired into the same file. `packages/subagent/tool-subagent/src/fork/config-surface.ts` (65 lines: `allowedConfigKeys(dict)` + `assertKnownConfigKeys(config, allowed)`) sits at `src/index.ts:44` (import), `:200` (`allowedConfigKeys(Config.dict)`), and the first statement of `apply()` (`:388`, ahead of the `models`×`modelSelectionSettings` mutual-exclusion check). Why: Cordis resolves the plugin config through Schemastery's NON-strict object schema (`vendor/schemastery/src/index.ts`: `Schema.resolve(data, schema, options, strict = false)` → `Schema.extend('object', ...)`'s `if (!strict) merge(result, data)`), so an undeclared top-level key is merged straight into the resolved config; the out-of-tree plugin carried its own `assertKnownKeys` and the migration dropped that guarantee, so a preset row spelling `models:` as `modelz:` mounted the upstream face — no `model` parameter, an inert `defaultModel`, children inheriting the parent's route — with no error anywhere. The guard names the unknown keys and lists the allowed set. That set is derived rather than restated: a Schemastery object schema hangs its declared-key map on the schema object, so `Config.dict` is reachable at runtime and the set is the 13 declared keys — upstream's nine (`provider`, `toolName`, `modelSelectionSettings`, `enableRunInBackground`, `backgroundMode`, `agentOptions`, `persona`, `toolFilter`, `maxDepth`) plus the fork's four (`models`, `defaultModel`, `cwd`, `skillFilter`) — with the module's literal list only the `dict === undefined` fallback, so an upstream key added to `Config` widens the accepted set with no second list to edit. The typo's other half is fixed beside it: a lone `defaultModel` — alias face absent while the key stays inert — now throws from `src/fork/model-alias.ts:76-86` with the message "tool-subagent: `defaultModel` is configured without `models` — add the alias table or remove `defaultModel`". Evidence: the guard's five-case block in `tests/fork-delegation-alias.spec.ts` (mount rejects the unknown key naming the allowed set, the same rejection when `apply()` bypasses Schemastery, the derived set proven equal to `Config.dict`'s keys beside a complete literal fallback, an upstream-only config still mounting with no fork field materialized, and `defaultModel` without `models`). The counts in this entry's first pair are superseded: 38→44 cases in that file and 164→170 in 6 files, and 248 across the three packages this session touched (170 here, 66 in `subagent-in-process-driver`, 12 in `tool-agent-team`) — the fourth case beyond the guard block covers the `cwd` type/emptiness branch in `src/fork/delegation-scope.ts`, bringing the three `src/fork/` modules to the repository's per-file 100% coverage gate (392/392 lines, 431/431 branches); `npx tsc -b tsconfig.host.json` exits 0. The Tier C row in `FORK_SURFACE.md` carries the module and the three wiring points.
- ZH: 别名面的配置面补上 fail-loud 守卫，作为第三个 fork 自有模块接入同一文件。`packages/subagent/tool-subagent/src/fork/config-surface.ts`（65 行：`allowedConfigKeys(dict)` + `assertKnownConfigKeys(config, allowed)`）落在 `src/index.ts:44`（import）、`:200`（`allowedConfigKeys(Config.dict)`）与 `apply()` 的首条语句（`:388`，在 `models`×`modelSelectionSettings` 互斥检查之前）。原因：Cordis 经 Schemastery 的**非严格** object schema 解析插件配置（`vendor/schemastery/src/index.ts`：`Schema.resolve(data, schema, options, strict = false)` → `Schema.extend('object', ...)` 的 `if (!strict) merge(result, data)`），未声明的顶层键被直接并入解析结果；包外插件自带 `assertKnownKeys`，迁移把这条保证丢了，于是 preset 行把 `models:` 拼成 `modelz:` 时挂载了上游面——没有 `model` 参数、`defaultModel` 成为死键、子代理继承父路由——且毫无报错。守卫点名未知键并列出允许集。允许集是**派生**而非重述：Schemastery 的 object schema 把成员表挂在 schema 对象上，故 `Config.dict` 运行时可达，键集即 13 个已声明键——上游 9 个（`provider`、`toolName`、`modelSelectionSettings`、`enableRunInBackground`、`backgroundMode`、`agentOptions`、`persona`、`toolFilter`、`maxDepth`）加 fork 4 个（`models`、`defaultModel`、`cwd`、`skillFilter`）——模块内的字面量列表仅作 `dict === undefined` 的兜底，因此上游往 `Config` 加键即自动放宽接受集，无需第二份清单。同一拼写错误的另一半在同处修好：孤立 `defaultModel`（别名面不存在而该键为死键）现在由 `src/fork/model-alias.ts:76-86` 抛出消息 "tool-subagent: `defaultModel` is configured without `models` — add the alias table or remove `defaultModel`"。证据：`tests/fork-delegation-alias.spec.ts` 内守卫的 5 个用例（挂载期拒绝未知键并点名允许集、`apply()` 绕过 Schemastery 时同样拒绝、派生集被证明等于 `Config.dict` 的键且字面量兜底完整、仅含上游键的配置照常挂载且不物化任何 fork 字段、`defaultModel` 无 `models`）。本条目第一对的计数随之作废：该文件 38→44 用例、6 文件 164→170 测试，本次触及的三个包合计 248（此处 170，`subagent-in-process-driver` 66，`tool-agent-team` 12）——守卫用例之外多出的这一条覆盖 `src/fork/delegation-scope.ts` 的 `cwd` 类型/空值分支，使三个 `src/fork/` 模块达到仓库的每文件 100% 覆盖率门禁（392/392 行、431/431 分支）；`npx tsc -b tsconfig.host.json` exit 0。`FORK_SURFACE.md` 的 Tier C 行登记该模块与三处接线点。
- EN: The migration's one unrecorded behavior change, in the prompt layer. An independent side-by-side run over the nine tool faces found the authorization surface, routing, defaults, `cwd`, `skillFilter`, `backgroundMode`, and `maxDepth` identical item by item (0 mismatches); the only substantive difference is what each face contributes to the system prompt. The out-of-tree plugin registered ONE `tool:subagent` section (796 characters) carrying the alias-whitelist sentence, the "the subagent does not see this conversation" sentence, and the `Role tools (…)` role-list sentence. The official design registers one `tool:<toolName>` section PER INSTANCE (`src/index.ts:722-728`), so the nine instances now contribute 9 sections / 3237 characters and only the background sentence survives — about 2.4 KB more per request. Consequence: the alias-whitelist sentence and the role-list sentence no longer appear in the system prompt (both survive in each tool's description and in the global `~/.dsh/AGENTS.md`), and the missing role self-description sentence has no downstream consumer (confirmed: `dsh-apollo` matches on tool name plus call arguments only).
- ZH: 本次迁移唯一未记录的行为变化在提示层。对 9 个工具面的独立对拍实测：授权面、路由、默认值、`cwd`、`skillFilter`、`backgroundMode`、`maxDepth` 逐项一致（0 mismatch）；唯一实质差异在各面向系统提示的贡献。包外插件注册**一个** `tool:subagent` 段（796 字符），携带别名白名单句、「子代理看不到本对话」句与 `Role tools (…)` 角色清单句。官方设计是**每实例一个** `tool:<toolName>` 段（`src/index.ts:722-728`），于是 9 个实例贡献 9 段 / 3237 字符，只剩背景句——每请求多约 2.4 KB。后果：别名白名单句与角色清单句不再出现在系统提示里（两者仍存活于各工具描述与全局 `~/.dsh/AGENTS.md` 中），而缺失的角色自述句没有下游消费者（已确认：`dsh-apollo` 只按工具名加调用参数匹配）。

### 2026-09-16 — Fork-surface verifier / fork 差异面校验器

- EN: Motivation: the 0.1.5-rc.2 sync dropped the in-process driver's `request.cwd`/`request.skillFilter` wiring silently, the re-apply commit missed it a second time, and nothing mechanical answered "did the sync replay every fork change?". `scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` (registered as a Tier A row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`) is a read-only checker that proves every fork marker under `packages/`, `apps/`, `vendor/` is reachable from an inventory row, every file a tier C row names still carries its marker, no row names a path no tracked file matches, and the English and Chinese surfaces keep the same heading structure and row count; a finding outside `scripts/fork-surface-baseline.txt` fails the run, and the baseline may freeze only accepted matcher limits (rows that register a surface by role, package or context instead of by a path the checker recognises), never a real marker or registration gap. It runs as runbook step 3, before walking the inventory, and as the `fork surface` lefthook pre-push job. Coverage and blind spots: it reads markers and inventory rows only, so it cannot tell whether a marked patch still does what its row claims (that stays the runbook's parity review), it scans nothing outside `packages/`, `apps/`, `vendor/` and skips `lib/`/`dist/`, and the generic file stems of a Tier A whole-package row stay baseline entries by design. This round also cleared the debt its first run reported: 25 upstream-owned files gained the marker their tier C row already named (the four `src/fork/` modules missing their `Fork-owned` header, the driver pair plus its `child-isolation.spec.ts`, the rewrite wire fields and the composer/facade/service threading, the `ui-tool` body-content trio, the mobile store fields, the two client brand defaults, and three test-file patches whose marker named `FORK_CHANGES.md` instead of the surface), 11 marked files were named in the rows that register them (`session-history-journal.host.spec.ts`, the two locale dictionaries, `ui-chat/src/client/apply.ts` and its spec, the two client web modules, `ui-layout`'s store spec, `ui-settings-general`'s portal spec, `ui-workspace/stores.ts`, `continuation-activation.ts`), `apps/web/tests/shipped-composition.e2e.ts`'s marker was repaired to the exact `Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md)` form it had drifted from, and the frozen baseline fell from 44 findings to 7 (2 Tier A whole-package entries and 5 tier C rows that cite a file as evidence rather than patch it).
- ZH: 动机：0.1.5-rc.2 同步静默丢掉了进程内驱动的 `request.cwd`/`request.skillFilter` 接线，「重新施加」那个提交又漏了第二次，而「同步到底有没有重放每一处 fork 改动」此前没有任何机械答案。`scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts`（已作为 Tier A 行登记进 `FORK_SURFACE.md`）是一个只读校验器：证明 `packages/`、`apps/`、`vendor/` 下每个 fork 标记都能从某条清单行到达、每个 tier C 行点名的文件仍带标记、没有行指向不存在的路径，且中英两份差异面保持相同的标题结构与行数；基线 `scripts/fork-surface-baseline.txt` 之外的发现会让运行失败，而基线只允许冻结被接受的**匹配器局限**（按角色、整包或上下文登记、而非按校验器可识别的路径登记的行），绝不冻结真实的标记或登记缺口。它在 runbook 第 3 步（走清单之前）与 lefthook pre-push 的 `fork surface` job 中运行。覆盖范围与已知盲区：它只读标记与清单行，因此无法判断某处已标记的补丁是否仍与其行描述相符（那仍是 runbook 的对等复核）；不扫描 `packages/`、`apps/`、`vendor/` 之外的任何路径，也跳过 `lib/`/`dist/`；Tier A 整包行内的通用文件名按设计保留在基线中。本轮同时清掉了首跑报出的欠账：25 个上游自有文件补上了其 tier C 行早已点名的标记（4 个缺 `Fork-owned` 头的 `src/fork/` 模块、驱动那对调用点及其 `child-isolation.spec.ts`、重写 wire 字段与 composer/facade/service 的透传、`ui-tool` 的 body-content 三件套、移动端 store 字段、两处客户端品牌默认值，以及 3 个标记写成 `FORK_CHANGES.md` 而非差异面名的测试文件），11 个带标记文件被写进登记它们的行（`session-history-journal.host.spec.ts`、两份语言字典、`ui-chat/src/client/apply.ts` 及其规格、两个 client web 模块、`ui-layout` 的 store 规格、`ui-settings-general` 的 portal 规格、`ui-workspace/stores.ts`、`continuation-activation.ts`），`apps/web/tests/shipped-composition.e2e.ts` 的标记修回它已漂移掉的 `Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md)` 精确形式，冻结基线从 44 条发现降到 7 条（2 条 Tier A 整包条目 + 5 条把文件作为证据引用而非补丁的 tier C 行）。

### 2026-09-19 — Fork-surface consumer audit / fork 差异面消费者审计

- EN: First consumer audit of the whole fork surface, requested to cut maintenance cost and coupling. Enumeration: fork master against its base tag dsh-v0.1.5-rc.2 is 630 files (+27,726/−1,290), 99 of the 101 marker-carrying files are registered by inventory rows (the two gaps are frozen as accepted Tier A whole-package matcher entries), and `verify-fork-surface` is green on the 7-entry matcher baseline; no newer rc/stable tag exists upstream (0.1.6-alpha churn is skipped by policy), so no sync was due. Every active row was verified against a live consumer: the deployment's user-layer and web-profile runtime patches mount the SQLite persistence package, the four web-search providers, `browserAuth: false`, `websocketPerMessageDeflate`, and the deferred boot batches, and the preset files carry the tool-subagent alias tables; the installed `dsh-apollo`/`dsh-rewind`/`dsh-better-sidebar` plugins pin the ui-tool `bodyContent`, message-actions-hook, and open-file-routing rows; every retained dual path keeps its measured-defect note. No active row lost its consumer, so no code surface was deleted. Retired: (1) the top-level `deliberations/` crate (19 files, adopted 2026-09-05 to carry the masked-tool-payload roundtable and the 0.1.2-alpha.1 absorb's worklog) — zero references anywhere in the repository and the private knowledge store keeps the original, so the copy was pure maintenance weight; `FORK_SURFACE.md` gains its retired row in both languages. (2) The duplicated "Application launch" paragraph in AGENTS.md (a sync-merge artifact; upstream carries it once). (3) AGENTS.md's "Sync before every change" bullet now states the practiced rule — fetch upstream every session, absorb rc/stable tags on cadence, never raw `upstream/master` — and a new "Every row names a live consumer" bullet records the standing audit policy and points here for this round's outcome. (4) The stale `## Current inventory` heading still said "0.1.2-alpha.1 sync" while the rows carry 0.1.5-rc.2 statuses; both language twins now name the base tag. Housekeeping: the landed `card-summary` worktree was removed, and `dsh-subagent-dispatch` (removal pending since 2026-09-16) is confirmed gone from the machine — its alias face lives on the official tool-subagent rows. Evidence: `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` OK (7 baselined, 0 stale) after the edits; `pnpm run test:docs` green; AGENTS.md stays under its word ceiling (2,666 → 2,719 words, ceiling 2,760; the dedupe and the trimmed outcome clause offset the new bullet).
- ZH: 首次对整个 fork 差异面做消费者审计，目标是压缩维护成本与耦合。枚举：fork master 相对其基线 tag dsh-v0.1.5-rc.2 共 630 个文件（+27,726/−1,290），101 个带标记文件中 99 个由清单行登记（两处缺口冻结为已接受的 Tier A 整包匹配器条目），`verify-fork-surface` 在 7 条匹配器基线上全绿；上游没有更新的 rc/stable tag（0.1.6-alpha 按政策跳过），本次无需同步。每条活跃行都核对了活消费者：部署的用户层与 web-profile 运行时 patch 挂载 SQLite 持久化包、四个 web-search 提供方、`browserAuth: false`、`websocketPerMessageDeflate` 与 deferred 启动批次，preset 文件携带 tool-subagent 别名表；已安装的 `dsh-apollo`/`dsh-rewind`/`dsh-better-sidebar` 插件钉住 ui-tool `bodyContent`、message-actions 钩子与 open-file-routing 三行；每条保留的双路径都有实测缺陷记录。没有活跃行失去消费者，因此没有删除任何代码面。退役：(1) 顶层 `deliberations/` crate（19 个文件，2026-09-05 收编，承载 masked-tool-payload 圆桌与 0.1.2-alpha.1 吸收的工作日志）——仓库内零引用、私有知识库保留原件，副本纯属维护负重；`FORK_SURFACE.md` 两份语言孪生均增加其退役行。(2) AGENTS.md 中重复的 "Application launch" 段（同步合并产物；上游只出现一次）。(3) AGENTS.md 的 "Sync before every change" 条目改为实际执行的规则——每个会话 fetch upstream、按节奏吸收 rc/stable tag、绝不合并裸 `upstream/master`——并新增 "Every row names a live consumer" 条目记录常设审计政策并指向本文件的本轮结果。(4) `## Current inventory` 标题仍写 "0.1.2-alpha.1 sync" 而各行已是 0.1.5-rc.2 状态；两份孪生现均指向基线 tag。清理：已落地的 `card-summary` worktree 移除；`dsh-subagent-dispatch`（2026-09-16 起待移除）确认已从机器上消失——其别名面存活于官方 tool-subagent 行。证据：改动后 `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` OK（7 条基线、0 陈旧）；`pnpm run test:docs` 全绿；AGENTS.md 词数保持在天花板之下（2,666 → 2,719 词，天花板 2,760；去重与收窄的结果从句抵消新增条目）。
### 2026-09-19 — Composer submits only on the Cmd/Ctrl chord / 输入框改为仅 Cmd/Ctrl 组合键提交

- EN: The Web composer no longer submits on plain Enter — plain Enter and Shift+Enter insert native line breaks, and only the Cmd/Ctrl chord submits, resolving to the Send button's exact delivery mode (the busyEnter preference, never upstream's inverted `accelerated` gesture). The empty-draft whole-queue steer branch is deleted with its placeholder hint and the `canSteerQueue` derivation: with the chord as the sole submit gesture, a double press would steer the just-queued message into the running turn — the same accident class the change removes. Five marked files (Tier C row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`): the keymap guard after the IME guard and menu arbitration (null synthetic Enters still submit), the three InputBar edits, the `settings.enter.description` rewrite plus the deleted `placeholder.steerQueue` keys in both dictionaries, three `describe.skip`s in `steering.e2e.ts` (two chord-inversion scenarios, one whole-queue steer scenario, each with its restore path), and the default-placeholder wait in `subagent-interrupt-ui.e2e.ts`; the `queued-image` and settings-dialog goldens carry the new copy. The submission policy, wire contract, input facade/hub, and machine stay upstream — `steerQueue` keeps its `service-orchestration` coverage and QueueDock per-row Steer remains the queue-into-turn path. The 2026-08-28 note that owned the retired first attempt is archived; a new note owns the amended decision and the 2026-09-04 busy-Send note is updated in place. Why: upstream has no setting that demotes Enter to a newline and the owner wants accidental sends impossible (the "two behaviors differ" report was the fork's own pre-0.1.5 surface, retired at the 0.1.5-rc.2 sync when upstream's BusyEnterBehavior superseded it). Evidence: `pnpm run test:gui` 5,412 passed with only the two `ui-tool` failures proven pre-existing at HEAD; `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; `pnpm run test:docs` 16/16; `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` OK (7 baselined) with the new row registered; `verify-archived-agent-notes` sealed 3 artifacts; `DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay pnpm run test:web` ran at 44 failed files / 58 failed tests — below the lane's documented 48/66 pre-existing set (2026-09-12 entry), every failure attributed to the known classes (persisted-replay composition drift with `danger-full-access` and missing permission events, llm-replay fixture cascades, Access-chip/TurnProcess-duration ARIA drift, harbour-palette assertions, environment timeouts), while the converted scenarios (`steering`, `composer-placeholder`, `document-preview`) pass and the composer textbox line in the failing ARIA diffs matches byte-for-byte; the `subagent-interrupt-ui` cascade is caused by its offline-composer golden's Access-chip line failing first. Two independent cross-model reviews plus one fresh re-review passed the final diff.
- ZH: Web composer 不再按普通 Enter 提交——普通 Enter 与 Shift+Enter 插入原生换行，仅 Cmd/Ctrl 组合键提交，并解析为与 Send 按钮完全相同的投递模式（busyEnter 偏好，绝不经上游取反的 `accelerated` 手势）。空草稿整队列 steer 分支连同其 placeholder 提示与 `canSteerQueue` 派生一并删除：组合键成为唯一提交手势后，连按两次会把刚排队的消息 steer 进运行中的回合——正是本次要消除的事故类别。五个带标记文件（`FORK_SURFACE.md` 的 Tier C 行）：IME 守卫与菜单仲裁之后的 keymap 守卫（null 合成 Enter 仍提交）、InputBar 的三处编辑、两份字典里 `settings.enter.description` 的改写与 `placeholder.steerQueue` 键的删除、`steering.e2e.ts` 的三个 `describe.skip`（两个取反组合键场景与一个整队列 steer 场景，各带恢复路径）、`subagent-interrupt-ui.e2e.ts` 的默认 placeholder 等待；`queued-image` 与设置对话框金句携带新文案。提交策略、wire contract、输入 facade/hub 与 machine 保持上游——`steerQueue` 保留 `service-orchestration` 覆盖，QueueDock 逐行 Steer 仍是从队列进入运行中回合的路径。持有已退役首次尝试的 2026-08-28 note 归档；新 note 拥有修订后的决策，2026-09-04 busy-Send note 就地更新。原因：上游没有任何能把 Enter 降级为换行的设置，而所有者要求杜绝误发送（"两个行为不一样"的来源正是 fork 在 0.1.5 前的定制面，0.1.5-rc.2 同步时因上游 BusyEnterBehavior 取代而退役）。证据：`pnpm run test:gui` 5,412 通过、仅剩两例在 HEAD 上已证明预先存在的 `ui-tool` 失败；`pnpm run typecheck` exit 0；`pnpm run test:docs` 16/16；`npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` OK（7 条基线）且新行已登记；`verify-archived-agent-notes` 封存 3 个工件；`DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay pnpm run test:web` 为 44 失败文件 / 58 失败测试——低于该泳道有档可查的 48/66 既有集（2026-09-12 条目），每个失败都归入已知类别（`danger-full-access` 与缺失 permission 事件的持久化回放组合漂移、llm-replay fixture 级联、Access-chip/TurnProcess 时长 ARIA 漂移、harbour 调色板断言、环境超时），而被转换的场景（`steering`、`composer-placeholder`、`document-preview`）通过，失败 ARIA diff 中的 composer textbox 行逐字节一致；`subagent-interrupt-ui` 的级联由其离线 composer 金句的 Access-chip 行先行失败所致。两票跨模型独立审查加一票 fresh 复审通过最终 diff。

### 2026-09-20 — Session-list identity cleanup is linear in the Session count / 会话列表身份缓存清扫改为线性

- EN: `SessionSnapshotIdentity.stableEntries` dropped departed rows by scanning the freshly flattened row list once per cached id (`items.some(e => e.sessionId === id)`), which is quadratic in the Session count — and the method runs on every list rebuild, which the deployed client drives from every Host projection frame. A CPU profile of the deployed Web client attributed 60.5% of all sampled JS self time to this method at 2,458 Sessions. The cleanup now builds one `Set` of live ids and tests membership; on the string `SessionId`s the contract admits the two implementations decide the same removal set (they differ only for a value where `===` and SameValueZero disagree, which a branded string id cannot take), and the entry-reuse rule plus the `itemsCache` reference-stability contract are unchanged, so every memo and subscriber miss and hit stays as before. New spec `packages/api/session-controller/tests/snapshot-identity.client.spec.ts` pins entry-object reuse, eviction and re-mint after a row leaves and returns, an emptied list, and a deterministic read-count guard on the cleanup (3,000 rows, warmed cache): the replaced sweep reads a row id `n + n(n+1)/2` = 4,504,500 times, the committed code exactly `2n` = 6,000, so the guard fails red on the old implementation with no wall-clock assertion. Wall-clock numbers are author-local observations (node v25.9.0, this worktree; not a CI budget and not reproducible from this commit alone): a same-process A/B at n = 2,458 measured 38.0 ms → 0.33 ms median per call, and the four-spec file 176–831 ms → 8 ms. Why: the list holds one row per Session in the whole store, so the old sweep grew with the user's accumulated history while running tens of times per second; the fork keeps this module because upstream mints fresh entries/projections per rebuild and has no equivalent identity pass. Evidence: `vitest run packages/api/session-controller/tests` 43 files / 819 tests green; `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; oxlint clean on both files.
- ZH: `SessionSnapshotIdentity.stableEntries` 原先通过"为每个缓存 id 扫一遍新行表"（`items.some(e => e.sessionId === id)`）剔除已离开的行，代价是会话数的二次方——而该方法在每次列表重建时都会跑，线上客户端由每个 Host projection 帧驱动。对线上 Web 客户端的 CPU 采样 profile 显示，2,458 个会话时该方法占全部 JS 自耗时间的 60.5%。现在改为先构建一次 live id 的 `Set` 再做成员判断；在契约允许的字符串 `SessionId` 上两者删除集合完全一致（唯一分歧是 `===` 与 SameValueZero 不一致的那个取值，branded string id 取不到），条目复用规则与 `itemsCache` 引用稳定性契约一字未改，所有 memo 与订阅者的命中/失效模式与之前相同。新增 `packages/api/session-controller/tests/snapshot-identity.client.spec.ts` 钉住条目对象复用、行离开再返回后的重铸、清空列表、以及一个**确定性读次数守卫**（3,000 行、缓存已预热）：被替换的清扫读行 id `n + n(n+1)/2` = 4,504,500 次，改动后恰好 `2n` = 6,000 次——守卫不依赖墙钟即可在旧实现上先红。墙钟数字为作者本机观测（node v25.9.0、本 worktree；不是 CI 预算，也无法仅凭本 commit 重放）：同进程 A/B 在 n = 2,458 下单次中位数 38.0 ms → 0.33 ms，四项 spec 文件 176~831 ms → 8 ms。原因：列表持有全部会话各一行，旧的清扫随用户累积的历史增长，却每秒要跑几十次；fork 保留该模块是因为上游每次重建都铸造全新条目/投影、没有等价的身份传递。证据：`vitest run packages/api/session-controller/tests` 43 文件 / 819 测试全绿；`pnpm run typecheck` exit 0；两个文件 oxlint 干净。

### 2026-09-20 — SQLite page cache becomes a validated Config field / SQLite 页缓存成为受校验的 Config 字段

- EN: The SQLite session store gains an optional `cacheSizeKib` Config field, applied on every connection as `PRAGMA cache_size = -<cacheSizeKib>` (SQLite's KiB form) and read back for verification. Omitted — the default — executes no pragma at all, so every existing composition keeps SQLite's default suggestion of 2,000 KiB (`-2000`, about 1.95 MiB); `0` applies `-0`, which SQLite reads as a zero-page suggestion floored to its fixed 10-page minimum — 0 pages is SQLite's TEMP-database default, not this database's. Validation is `z.number().step(1).min(0).max(MAX_CACHE_SIZE_KIB)`, with `MAX_CACHE_SIZE_KIB = 2_147_483_647` being the largest page-cache magnitude inside SQLite's signed 32-bit KiB range (INT32_MAX; SQLite does accept the literal `-2147483648` and reads it back, but the field carries a positive magnitude, and 2,147,483,648 has no positive signed 32-bit representation) — the same interface-bound rationale the neighbouring `MAX_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS` states. Two resources join `resources/sql/`: `cache-size.sql` (`PRAGMA cache_size = -?;`) and `select-cache-size.sql`; `sql()` gains an overload for `cache-size` alone whose argument is substituted for that resource's `?` token, while every other resource name stays callable with the name alone (15 of the 42 resources declare `?` tokens, but those are ordinary bound parameters), because SQLite's pragma grammar refuses a bound value (`PRAGMA cache_size = ?` is a syntax error on the bundled SQLite 3.51.3) and the package's SQL-resource boundary test admits only `sql(...)`/`testSql(...)` calls as statement sources, so the value cannot be assembled at the call site. Wiring is `Config.cacheSizeKib` → `SqliteStoreOptions.cacheSizeKib` → `openDatabase(..., cacheSizeKib)` → `configurePageCache`, which returns before the pragma when the option is absent. Why: the deployment wants a 1 GiB page cache for the session store (`cacheSizeKib: 1048576`), and a validated `Config` field is the fork's preferred extension point; the field is off unless configured, so the default path stays identical and a regression is bisectable to one key. Where: `src/index.ts`, `src/schema.ts`, `src/store.ts`, `src/sql.ts`, `resources/sql/cache-size.sql`, `resources/sql/select-cache-size.sql`, `tests/page-cache.spec.ts`, `tests/sql-resource-boundary.spec.ts`, `tests/resources/sql/select-cache-size.sql`, `tests/test-sql.ts`, plus the package README pair (EN/ZH config row, the "values are bound, never assembled" claim narrowed to its one exception, and the per-connection settings sentence) and its re-recorded `README.i18n.yaml` hashes. The change lands inside the already-registered Tier A `session-persistence-sqlite` whole-package row, so `FORK_SURFACE.md` needs no new row. Evidence (node v25.9.0, this worktree): the new spec ran red before the implementation — 3 failed | 1 passed, `expected -2000 to be -1048576` for a configured mount and `promise resolved "'mounted'" instead of rejecting` for `-1`/`1.5`/`2_147_483_648` — and green after it; the omitted-field case passes in both runs because no pragma is executed. `tests/sql-resource-boundary.spec.ts` gains a third case that scans every `sql(...)`/`testSql(...)` call under `src/` and `tests/` and rejects a substituted argument outside `cache-size`; the overloads were checked with a scoped `tsc` probe whose three `@ts-expect-error` directives are unused (TS2578) against the previous `sql(name, argument?)` signature and consumed against the overloads. Default and read-back facts were re-measured on the bundled SQLite 3.51.3: a fresh connection reads back `-2000`, `PRAGMA temp.cache_size` reads 0, `0` sets 0, and `-2147483648` is accepted and read back unchanged. `vitest run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` is 7 passed files and 130 passed | 1 skipped tests in this run, where a `lib/` build makes `built-package.spec.ts` run instead of skip (without it: 6 files, 129 passed | 2 skipped, against the 124 passed | 2 skipped baseline). `oxlint` reports 0 warnings / 0 errors on every changed TypeScript file, and the README pair is consistent under `verify-translation-pairing`. Not run by design: repo-wide typecheck, build, e2e, and benchmarks. Still open outside this change's write set: the generated `docs/config-catalog.md` entry for the new field, which its gate will demand at the next doc-sync.
- ZH: SQLite 会话存储新增可选 `cacheSizeKib` Config 字段，在每个连接上以 `PRAGMA cache_size = -<cacheSizeKib>`（SQLite 的 KiB 形式）应用并读回校验。省略该字段即默认，不执行任何 pragma，因此所有既有组合保持 SQLite 的默认建议值 2,000 KiB（`-2000`，约 1.95 MiB）；`0` 应用 `-0`，SQLite 将其读作零页建议并下限钳制为固定的 10 页——0 页是 SQLite 的 TEMP 库默认值，不是本库的。校验为 `z.number().step(1).min(0).max(MAX_CACHE_SIZE_KIB)`，`MAX_CACHE_SIZE_KIB = 2_147_483_647`（INT32_MAX）是落在 SQLite 有符号 32 位 KiB 范围内的最大页缓存量级（SQLite 确实接受字面量 `-2147483648` 并原样读回，但本字段承载的是正量级，而 2,147,483,648 没有正的 signed 32-bit 表示）——与相邻 `MAX_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS` 相同的「接口边界」理由。`resources/sql/` 新增两个资源：`cache-size.sql`（`PRAGMA cache_size = -?;`）与 `select-cache-size.sql`；`sql()` 改为只为 `cache-size` 提供重载，其实参替换该资源的 `?` 占位符，其余资源名只能仅带资源名调用（42 个资源中有 15 个声明了 `?`，但那些都是普通绑定参数），因为 SQLite 的 pragma 语法拒绝绑定值（随包 SQLite 3.51.3 上 `PRAGMA cache_size = ?` 是语法错误），而本包的 SQL 资源边界测试只接受 `sql(...)`/`testSql(...)` 作为语句来源，故该值无法在调用点拼装。接线为 `Config.cacheSizeKib` → `SqliteStoreOptions.cacheSizeKib` → `openDatabase(..., cacheSizeKib)` → `configurePageCache`，后者在选项缺省时直接返回、不执行 pragma。原因：部署希望会话存储使用 1 GiB 页缓存（`cacheSizeKib: 1048576`），而受校验的 `Config` 字段是 fork 首选的扩展点；该字段不配置即关闭，默认路径完全不变，回归也可二分定位到单个键。改动位置：`src/index.ts`、`src/schema.ts`、`src/store.ts`、`src/sql.ts`、`resources/sql/cache-size.sql`、`resources/sql/select-cache-size.sql`、`tests/page-cache.spec.ts`、`tests/sql-resource-boundary.spec.ts`、`tests/resources/sql/select-cache-size.sql`、`tests/test-sql.ts`，以及本包 README 双语对（配置表新增行、「值以绑定传入、从不拼装」这句话收窄到唯一例外、每连接设置的句子）与其重录的 `README.i18n.yaml` 哈希。改动落在已登记的 Tier A `session-persistence-sqlite` 整包行内，故 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 无需新行。证据（node v25.9.0、本 worktree）：新 spec 在实现前先红——3 失败 | 1 通过，配置挂载报 `expected -2000 to be -1048576`，`-1`/`1.5`/`2_147_483_648` 报 `promise resolved "'mounted'" instead of rejecting`；实现后全绿；省略字段的用例两次都通过，因为不执行 pragma。`tests/sql-resource-boundary.spec.ts` 新增第三个用例：扫描 `src/` 与 `tests/` 下每一处 `sql(...)`/`testSql(...)` 调用，拒绝 `cache-size` 之外的实参替换；重载本身用一份限定范围的 `tsc` 探针验证——三个 `@ts-expect-error` 指令在旧的 `sql(name, argument?)` 签名下未被使用（TS2578），在重载下被消耗。默认值与读回事实在随包 SQLite 3.51.3 上重新实测：新连接读回 `-2000`，`PRAGMA temp.cache_size` 读回 0，`0` 设为 0，`-2147483648` 被接受并原样读回。`vitest run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` 本次为 7 通过文件、130 通过 | 1 跳过测试（本 worktree 存在 `lib/` 构建，`built-package.spec.ts` 因此执行而非跳过；无该构建时为 6 文件、129 通过 | 2 跳过，基线 124 通过 | 2 跳过）。`oxlint` 对每个改动的 TypeScript 文件报 0 warning / 0 error，README 双语对在 `verify-translation-pairing` 下一致。按要求未跑：全仓 typecheck、build、e2e 与 benchmark。本次改动写集之外仍待办：新字段在生成的 `docs/config-catalog.md` 中的条目——下次 doc-sync 时其门禁会要求补上。

### 2026-09-20 — Session-log decompression moves to the libuv thread pool / 会话日志解压改走 libuv 线程池

- EN: `session-persistence-sqlite` gains one validated `Config` field and the thread-pool decompression arm behind it. `src/index.ts` declares `asyncCodec?: boolean` (`z.boolean().default(false)`, JSDoc naming why the switch exists) and threads it into `SqliteStoreOptions`; `src/store.ts` resolves its stored-row scan once (`scanRows` versus `scanRowsOnThreadPool`) and the two cold-read scans — `loadStoredLog` and `loadStoredFrom`, the only scans that read outside a transaction — await it; `src/compression.ts` gains the `ScannedRows` and `StoredRowScan` types plus `scanRowsOnThreadPool`, which hands each compressed data column to `zlib.zstdDecompress` in a bounded window (`HYDRATION_WINDOW = 8`, twice libuv's default pool size of 4) and then runs the very same `scanRows` over the hydrated rows. A column the pool cannot decode keeps its compressed value, so the scan's own per-row decode classifies the malformed row exactly where the synchronous codec classifies it. All touched sources and new specs carry the `Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md)` marker; `README.md` gains the config row, a read-path paragraph, and a corrected Known Limitations bullet.
- ZH: `session-persistence-sqlite` 增加一个受校验的 `Config` 字段及背后的线程池解压通道。`src/index.ts` 声明 `asyncCodec?: boolean`（`z.boolean().default(false)`，JSDoc 说明该开关为何存在）并透传进 `SqliteStoreOptions`；`src/store.ts` 把存储行扫描解析一次（`scanRows` 或 `scanRowsOnThreadPool`），两处冷读扫描——`loadStoredLog` 与 `loadStoredFrom`，即唯一在事务之外读的两处——await 它；`src/compression.ts` 新增 `ScannedRows` 与 `StoredRowScan` 类型及 `scanRowsOnThreadPool`：把每个压缩数据列交给 `zlib.zstdDecompress`，按有界窗口（`HYDRATION_WINDOW = 8`，即 libuv 默认池大小 4 的两倍）处理，随后对解压后的行跑**同一个** `scanRows`。线程池解不开的列保留压缩原值，于是扫描自己的逐行解码会把该畸形行归类在与同步编解码完全相同的判定点上。改动的源码与新增规格均带 `Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md)` 标记；`README.md` 增加配置行、读路径段落，并修正 Known Limitations 条目。
- EN: Why: a production session of 61k events costs 36.4 CPU·s to open, dominated by the synchronous `zstdCompressSync`/`zstdDecompressSync` calls in this module, all of it blocking the event loop. libuv pool threads share the V8 heap, so the pool adds no second heap; libuv's default pool size is 4 and this deployment sets no `UV_THREADPOOL_SIZE`, so the window bounds in-flight decodes rather than memory — the thread-pool scan peaks above the synchronous one because it retains every decoded column beside the caller's compressed rows. The switch defaults off, so today's behavior is one field away and the change is bisectable and rollbackable on its own.
- ZH: 原因：生产环境 61k 事件的大会话打开要耗 36.4 CPU·秒，其中大头是本模块里同步的 `zstdCompressSync`/`zstdDecompressSync`，全程阻塞事件循环。libuv 线程池线程共享同一个 V8 堆，故线程池不额外开一个堆；libuv 默认池大小为 4，而本部署未设置 `UV_THREADPOOL_SIZE`，因此该窗口限制的是在飞解码数而非内存——线程池扫描的峰值高于同步扫描，因为它会把每个解压后的列与调用方的压缩行一并保留。开关默认关闭：今天的行为只隔一个字段，改动本身可二分、可回滚。
- EN: Falsified assumption, recorded because the acceptance criterion asked for it: Node's asynchronous zstd compressor cannot reproduce `zstdCompressSync` bytes. `zstdCompressSync` is one-shot (`ZSTD_compress2`): it knows the source size, so it writes a single-segment frame carrying Frame_Content_Size. `zstdCompress` streams (`ZSTD_compressStream2`): it writes a window descriptor instead. Same input, same dictionary, same `params` — `28b52ffd609402fd…` (41 B) versus `28b52ffd0058fd…` (40 B); 5/5 probe inputs differ, and an explicit `ZSTD_c_contentSizeFlag`, a 1 MiB `chunkSize`, or an explicit `windowLog` does not close the gap (with `windowLog` pinned and the content-size flag off the two paths do agree — but only by rewriting the synchronous compressor's options, which would change today's stored bytes). Async decompression is unaffected: sync and pool decodes return identical bytes, including the `maxOutputLength` bound (`ERR_BUFFER_TOO_LARGE`) and the `ZSTD_error_prefix_unknown` failure for non-zstd input. Consequence: compression stays `zstdCompressSync` in both states and `asyncCodec` covers decompression only, so on and off still write byte-identical rows; moving compression off-thread needs an accepted frame difference or a `worker_thread` (own isolate), neither of which this change takes.
- ZH: 被证伪的假设，记录在案是因为验收判据如此要求：Node 的异步 zstd 压缩器无法复现 `zstdCompressSync` 的字节。`zstdCompressSync` 是一次性调用（`ZSTD_compress2`）：它知道源长度，因此写出带 Frame_Content_Size 的 single-segment 帧；`zstdCompress` 走流式（`ZSTD_compressStream2`），改写出 window descriptor。同一输入、同一字典、同一 `params`——`28b52ffd609402fd…`（41 B）对 `28b52ffd0058fd…`（40 B）；5/5 个探针输入都不一致，显式 `ZSTD_c_contentSizeFlag`、1 MiB `chunkSize`、显式 `windowLog` 都无法弥合（把 `windowLog` 钉死并关掉 content-size 标志后两条路径确实一致——但那要改写同步压缩器的选项，等于改变今天已写入的字节）。异步解压不受影响：同步与线程池解码返回完全相同的字节，包括 `maxOutputLength` 上界（`ERR_BUFFER_TOO_LARGE`）与非 zstd 输入的 `ZSTD_error_prefix_unknown` 失败。结论：压缩在两种状态下都保持 `zstdCompressSync`，`asyncCodec` 只覆盖解压，因此开与关写出的行仍逐字节相同；要把压缩也移出主线程，需要接受帧差异或改用 `worker_thread`（独立 isolate），本次都不采用。
- EN: Transaction boundary, the reason the async arm is scoped to the two cold-read scans: a decode that runs while a write transaction is open (the append tail window behind `physicalSpanFrom`, `commitRepair`, `truncateLog`) keeps the synchronous decoder. An await between `begin` and `commit` on the store's single `DatabaseSync` connection would let another store operation open a nested transaction on it, and that operation's `rollback` would then end the writer's transaction — so keeping those decodes synchronous preserves the existing begin/commit/rollback semantics literally, and it also lets concurrent cold reads decompress in parallel instead of queueing behind a store-wide mutex.
- ZH: 事务边界，也就是异步通道只落在两处冷读扫描上的原因：凡是在写事务打开期间执行的解码（`physicalSpanFrom` 背后的追加尾部窗口、`commitRepair`、`truncateLog`）保留同步解码器。在该 store 唯一的 `DatabaseSync` 连接上，`begin` 与 `commit` 之间出现 await，会让另一个 store 操作在同一连接上开启嵌套事务，而它随后的 `rollback` 会终止写方的事务——因此让这些解码保持同步，等于逐字保留既有的 begin/commit/rollback 语义；同时这也让并发的冷读能在池上并行解压，而不是排在 store 级互斥锁后面。
- EN: Evidence: `node node_modules/vitest/vitest.mjs run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` → 7 files passed / 1 skipped, 134 passed / 2 skipped (136), exit 0; the two new specs are `tests/compression-async.spec.ts` (6 cases: sync-versus-pool equivalence over packed, compressed-scalar and raw-text rows, a scan base, torn tail, committed corruption, malformed and oversized frames; the zstd entry point each arm actually uses, observed through a `node:zlib` mock; the `maxOutputLength` bound crossing into the pool call) and `tests/async-codec.spec.ts` (4 cases: the default and the schema rejection of a non-boolean value, byte-identical raw `events` columns for a DB written with the switch off and on, cross-reads in all four write/read pairings, and pool-versus-sync entry-point selection through a mounted provider). Differential digests from a one-off probe over one compressible 5-event log: raw stored columns `sha256[:16]` `5030cfae1f7d6776` on both settings, 4 of 5 rows stored as compressed blobs with identical blob hex, decoded events `b9657f813f7957b2` for `scanRows` and `scanRowsOnThreadPool` alike, no torn tail. No pre-existing spec file was edited (only `tests/test-sql.ts` gains one resource name, `select-event-columns`, with its fixture); oxlint reports 0 warnings / 0 errors on all six files. Worktree note: the JSONL differential mounts a native binding, and this worktree had no built `native/system/packages/linux-x64/bin/glibc/system.node`, so the prebuilt one was copied from the main checkout into that git-ignored `bin/` path (read-only use of the main checkout; no tracked file changed).
- ZH: 证据：`node node_modules/vitest/vitest.mjs run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` → 7 文件通过 / 1 跳过，134 通过 / 2 跳过（136），exit 0；两个新规格为 `tests/compression-async.spec.ts`（6 例：packed、压缩标量、未压缩文本行、扫描 base、撕裂尾、已提交损坏、畸形与超限帧上的同步对线程池等价性；各通道实际选用的 zstd 入口，经 `node:zlib` mock 观测；`maxOutputLength` 上界进入池调用的断言）与 `tests/async-codec.spec.ts`（4 例：默认值与 schema 对非布尔值的拒绝、开关两态写出的原始 `events` 列逐字节相同、四种写读配对的交叉读取、挂载后的 provider 上按配置选择池/同步入口）。一次性探针对同一份可压缩的 5 事件日志给出差分摘要：原始存储列 `sha256[:16]` 两态同为 `5030cfae1f7d6776`，5 行中 4 行以压缩 blob 存储且 blob 十六进制一致，解码事件对 `scanRows` 与 `scanRowsOnThreadPool` 同为 `b9657f813f7957b2`，无撕裂尾。未改动任何既有规格文件（仅 `tests/test-sql.ts` 增加一个资源名 `select-event-columns` 及其 fixture）；oxlint 对全部六个文件报 0 warnings / 0 errors。worktree 备注：JSONL 差分规格会挂载原生 binding，而本 worktree 没有构建产物 `native/system/packages/linux-x64/bin/glibc/system.node`，故从主 checkout 只读复制预构建的该文件到其 git-ignored 的 `bin/` 路径（未改动任何受跟踪文件）。
