### 2026-09-20 — Turn-continuation guard: a turn that ended truncated opens one more turn / 回合续跑 guard：被截断的回合自动再开一轮

- EN: New opt-in package `packages/guard/turn-continuation` (`@deepseek-ai/dsh-turn-continuation`) — the fork's first ADDED package rather than a modified upstream surface. Why: a session stops whenever a turn ends, including when the model's answer was cut off at the output-token ceiling (`turn/end` reason `max-tokens`). That reason is deliberately terminal and sticky (`packages/core/agent-loop/src/agent.ts:305-310`, `:484`); the one existing continuation driver gives up on it (`goal-round-driver/src/index.ts:329-333` disarms), and the only other turn-end seam belongs to user-authored Stop hooks, which force continuation with no cap (`hooks-claude-code/src/index.ts:266-276` carries the `TODO(stop-loop-guard)`). So a truncated answer ended the session until a human typed "continue".
- ZH: 新增 opt-in 包 `packages/guard/turn-continuation`（`@deepseek-ai/dsh-turn-continuation`）——本 fork 首次**新增**包（此前各条目均为修改上游面）。原因：会话在每个回合结束时就停，包括模型回答因触顶输出 token 上限而被截断时（`turn/end` 的 reason 为 `max-tokens`）。该 reason 被设计为终态且粘性（`packages/core/agent-loop/src/agent.ts:305-310`、`:484`）；唯一现存的续跑驱动在它上面直接放弃（`goal-round-driver/src/index.ts:329-333` 执行 disarm），另一处回合末接缝属于用户自写的 Stop hook，其强制续跑没有任何上限（`hooks/hooks-claude-code/src/index.ts:266-276` 留着 `TODO(stop-loop-guard)`）。于是被截断的回答会让会话停住，直到人手动输入「继续」。
- EN: What it does: it only RECORDS the reason from a `session/event` `turn/end` observer — never enqueues there, because `Session.append` rejects reentry while another append is being published (`packages/core/session/src/index.ts:721-724`) and the session's contained-observer wrapper logs the throw at warn level and contains it (`:412-420`), so the wake would be lost. It acts from the `agent/status` idle edge, claiming the idle phase with `agent.runMaintenance()` and calling `agent.followup()`, which opens a NEW turn: the truncated turn keeps its `max-tokens` reason and the continuation is its own ordinary turn that can end `completed`. Continuable reasons are exactly `max-tokens` and `error`; an `aborted`, `blocked`, `completed`, or `interrupted` entry throws at plugin load rather than silently continuing nothing. The budget is a count of consecutive continuations per human turn in a `WeakMap<Agent, number>`, refilled only when `agent/inbox/claimed` reports a message whose `source.kind === 'user'` — the shipped `tool-jobs` wake-budget rule (`jobs/tool-jobs/src/index.ts:213,224-228`), so a continuation this plugin queued (source `{kind:'plugin'}`) cannot refill the budget it just spent. Defaults: `continueOn: ['max-tokens']`, `maxConsecutive: 2`, and `0` disables continuation without unmounting. Authority is process-local by construction: a resumed session has observed no turn-end in the new process, so it never continues on its own — which satisfies the recorded rule that opening a session is observation, not authority to spend resources.
- ZH: 做什么：它只从 `session/event` 的 `turn/end` 观察者**记录** reason——绝不在那里入队，因为 `Session.append` 在另一次 append 发布期间拒绝重入（`packages/core/session/src/index.ts:721-724`），而 session 自带的受控观察者包装会把该异常按 warn 记录并吞掉（`:412-420`），于是唤醒会丢失。它在 `agent/status` 的 idle 边沿行动：用 `agent.runMaintenance()` 占用 idle 相位，再 `agent.followup()` 开启**新的一轮**——被截断的回合保留其 `max-tokens` reason，续跑是它自己的一轮普通回合，可以以 `completed` 结束。可续跑的 reason 恰为 `max-tokens` 与 `error`；配置里写 `aborted`、`blocked`、`completed` 或 `interrupted` 会在插件加载期抛错，而不是静默地什么也不续。预算是「每个真人回合允许的连续续跑次数」，存于 `WeakMap<Agent, number>`，仅当 `agent/inbox/claimed` 报出一条 `source.kind === 'user'` 的消息时回填——沿用已发布的 `tool-jobs` 唤醒预算规则（`jobs/tool-jobs/src/index.ts:213,224-228`），因此本插件自己排队的续跑（source `{kind:'plugin'}`）无法回填它刚花掉的预算。默认值：`continueOn: ['max-tokens']`、`maxConsecutive: 2`，`0` 表示不卸载即停用。授权在构造上就是进程内的：被恢复的会话在新进程中没有观察到任何 turn-end，因此不会自行续跑——这满足「打开会话是观察、不是花费资源的授权」这条已记录规则。
- EN: Why a plugin and not the loop: the recorded design rejects adding a generic loop abstraction to `dsh-agent-loop` (`.agents/notes/implemented/feature/2026-07-19-persisted-same-session-goal-domain.md:47`), and the recorded rule for a same-session objective is that continuation policy belongs to a consumer package. No `agent-loop` change was needed, no `SessionEventMap` member was added, both SDK projections are untouched, and no invariant companion is published (the package README records why: the plugin owns no independent event sequence — it reads `turn/end` and writes ordinary `user/message` events through the loop). The plugin is OPT-IN: it is deliberately absent from `packages/bundle/base/cordis.patch.yml`, so a deployment enables it with a profile patch row naming `@deepseek-ai/dsh-turn-continuation`.
- ZH: 为什么做成插件而非改循环：已记录的决策拒绝把通用循环抽象塞进 `dsh-agent-loop`（`.agents/notes/implemented/feature/2026-07-19-persisted-same-session-goal-domain.md:47`），而同会话目标的已记录规则是续跑策略归属消费者包。本次无需改动 `agent-loop`，未新增 `SessionEventMap` 成员，两个 SDK 投影未受影响，也未发布 invariant 伴随（包 README 记录了原因：该插件不拥有独立事件序列——它读 `turn/end`，并通过循环写入普通 `user/message`）。插件为 **opt-in**：刻意不进入 `packages/bundle/base/cordis.patch.yml`，由部署方以 profile patch 行命名 `@deepseek-ai/dsh-turn-continuation` 启用。
- EN: Evidence: `npx vitest run packages/guard/turn-continuation/tests` — 2 files / 19 tests passed. The behaviour suite covers the reason gate, the budget cap, human-input refill, the no-self-refill case, five fail-loud config rejections, the zero-budget disable, and fiber-disposal; the REQUIRED real-composition suite boots the whole spine plus this plugin through the real Loader from a temporary `cordis.yml` (only the LLM adapter is mocked) and asserts model requests and the durable session log: the default config continues a truncated turn, a `maxConsecutive: 1` from the yml issues exactly one continuation where the default would issue two, and a `continueOn: [error]` from the yml leaves the truncated turn alone — so both Config fields are proven live rather than constant. `npx vitest run packages/guard packages/core/agent-loop` — 27 files / 464 tests passed, so the new package regresses neither guard nor agent-loop; `npx tsc -b packages/guard/turn-continuation` exit 0. The keyless recorded-session snapshot `snapshots/session/turn-continuation/` owns its composition and mounts the opt-in plugin by RELATIVE SOURCE PATH (`../../../packages/guard/turn-continuation/src/index.ts`), so no bundle row was added; the committed fixture shows turn 1 closing `max-tokens`, turn 2 closing `completed`, and the continuation admitted as a durable `user/message` sourced `{kind:'plugin',plugin:'turn-continuation'}` — i.e. the plugin fires inside a real `dsh --profile headless` process, not a mock. `npx vitest run --config vitest.snapshot.config.ts -t turn-continuation` passes keyless and the corpus gate accepts the scenario. Documentation in the same change: the package README triplet, the `guard` group README pair (now three packages, recording which two ship enabled and which one is opt-in), an Agent Note triplet, and the regenerated `docs/config-catalog`, `docs/module-graph`, and `docs/event-producer-consumer` pairs. Note for the next sync: regenerating `docs/event-producer-consumer.md` also corrected five pre-existing `api-session/*` source line references that had drifted on master, so that file's diff is wider than this change.
- ZH: 证据：`npx vitest run packages/guard/turn-continuation/tests` —— 2 文件 / 19 测试通过。行为套件覆盖 reason 门禁、预算上限、真人输入回填、不可自回填、五条 fail-loud 配置拒绝、零预算停用、fiber 卸载；**必需的真实组合套件**从临时 `cordis.yml` 经真实 Loader 启动整条脊柱加本插件（只 mock LLM 适配器），并对模型请求与会话日志断言：缺省配置会续跑被截断的回合，yml 里的 `maxConsecutive: 1` 只发出一轮续跑（缺省会发两轮），yml 里的 `continueOn: [error]` 则完全不碰被截断的回合——两个 Config 字段都被证明是活的而非常量。`npx vitest run packages/guard packages/core/agent-loop` —— 27 文件 / 464 测试通过，故新包对 guard 与 agent-loop 均无回归；`npx tsc -b packages/guard/turn-continuation` exit 0。keyless 录制会话快照 `snapshots/session/turn-continuation/` 自带 composition，并以**相对源码路径**挂载这个 opt-in 插件（`../../../packages/guard/turn-continuation/src/index.ts`），因此没有新增任何 bundle 行；提交的录制件显示第 1 轮以 `max-tokens` 收尾、第 2 轮以 `completed` 收尾，且续跑以 `{kind:'plugin',plugin:'turn-continuation'}` 来源的持久 `user/message` 入账——即插件是在真实的 `dsh --profile headless` 进程里触发的，不是 mock。`npx vitest run --config vitest.snapshot.config.ts -t turn-continuation` 在 keyless 下通过，corpus 门禁也接受该场景。同批文档：包 README 三件套、`guard` 组 README 两份（现在描述三个包，并记录哪两个默认启用、哪个是 opt-in）、Agent Note 三件套，以及再生成的 `docs/config-catalog`、`docs/module-graph`、`docs/event-producer-consumer` 配对。给下次同步的提示：再生成 `docs/event-producer-consumer.md` 时顺带修正了 5 处 master 上已漂移的 `api-session/*` 源码行号引用，因此该文件的 diff 比本次改动本身更宽。

### 2026-09-21 — A catalog listing bounds its cold Session reads / catalog listing 的冷读有了上限

- EN: `packages/subagent/subagent` gains two validated `Config` fields and threads them into the listing core. Measured production evidence: on the production-isomorphic isolated instance, the first GUI load of a 422-subagent workspace with a cold projection cache decoded every non-live child's complete stored Session log (`loadStoredLog`) — 423 such decodes per pass across 4–5 passes — and pinned one core at 100% for about 8 minutes while every RPC and stream stalled and clients timed out and reconnected; a hot cache performs zero reads, so any invalidation (a new `DSH_HOME`, one projection unit's stateVersion change, a rewrite discard, lost storages) restores the cold cost. `coldReadConcurrency` (default 4, the ported hardcoded constant) and `coldReadBudget` (default 64) now ride `listChildren`/`listDescendants` into `packages/subagent/subagent/src/list-children.ts`, where one listing starts only real cold reads inside the budget: the projection-cache rung is resolved before selection, so a cache-served candidate spends neither the budget nor a concurrency slot — repeated listings advance monotonically, and a newly created child is never permanently `unavailable` behind a cache-served head — while candidates past the budget keep their corpus position and report the existing retryable `{kind:'diagnostic',reason:'unavailable'}`, so no wire vocabulary or cancellation semantics change. The same commit adds the vendored `@deepseek-ai/schemastery` dependency (130 packages in the tree already depend on it) and the regenerated catalogs and subsystem docs. Evidence: the new `SubagentRuntime cold-read bounds` block in `packages/subagent/subagent/tests/list-children.spec.ts` (the shipped defaults and the rejection of a bound below one, budget selection over a synthetic cold corpus, the concurrency peak held open, cache advance on the next listing, and the defaults on a directly constructed plugin). Marked files: `packages/subagent/subagent/src/index.ts`, `packages/subagent/subagent/src/list-children.ts`, and that spec; registered as a new Tier C row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`. Agent Note: [subagent-listing-cold-read-bound](.agents/notes/implemented/bug-fix/2026-09-21-subagent-listing-cold-read-bound.md).
- ZH: `packages/subagent/subagent` 新增两个经校验的 `Config` 字段，并把它们透传进 listing 核心。实测的线上证据：在与生产同构的隔离实例上，投影缓存为冷时首次 GUI 加载一个有 422 个子会话的 workspace，会为每个非 live 子会话解码一份完整存储会话日志（`loadStoredLog`）——每一遍 423 次这样的解码，共 4–5 遍——把单核钉在 100% 约 8 分钟，期间所有 RPC 与流挂起、客户端超时并反复重连；缓存热时该路径零读取，因此任何一次失效（新的 `DSH_HOME`、某个投影单元 stateVersion 变化、rewrite discard、storages 丢失）都会让冷态成本回来。`coldReadConcurrency`（默认 4，即移植过来的硬编码常量）与 `coldReadBudget`（默认 64）现经 `listChildren`/`listDescendants` 传入 `packages/subagent/subagent/src/list-children.ts`：单次 listing 只在预算内发起**真实**冷读——投影缓存那一档在选择之前就已解析，因此被缓存服务的候选既不花预算也不占并发槽，于是重复 listing 单调推进、新建子会话不会因队首被缓存服务而永久 `unavailable`——超出预算的候选保留其 corpus 位置并产出既有的可重试 `{kind:'diagnostic',reason:'unavailable'}`，wire 词汇与取消语义均无变化。同一提交还加入 vendored 的 `@deepseek-ai/schemastery` 依赖（仓库内已有 130 个包依赖它）以及重新生成的 catalog 与子系统文档。证据：`packages/subagent/subagent/tests/list-children.spec.ts` 新增的 `SubagentRuntime cold-read bounds` 块（随产品交付的默认值与小于 1 的拒绝、合成冷 corpus 上的预算选择、被挂起读取钉住的并发峰值、下一次 listing 的缓存推进、以及直接构造插件时的默认值）。带标记文件：`packages/subagent/subagent/src/index.ts`、`packages/subagent/subagent/src/list-children.ts` 与该规格；在 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 登记为新的 Tier C 行。Agent Note：[subagent-listing-cold-read-bound](.agents/notes/implemented/bug-fix/2026-09-21-subagent-listing-cold-read-bound.md)。

### 2026-09-21 — Remote stream carrier lifecycle diagnostics / Remote stream 载波生命周期诊断

- EN: `packages/api/gateway` reports why a Remote stream carrier lived and died. Measured production evidence: the browser-side mux WebSocket rebuilt every ~15 seconds for hours with no line anywhere on that path, so a heartbeat terminate, a delayed ready/first frame (the event loop occupied elsewhere), and a peer or proxy close were indistinguishable; traefik recorded 468 connections on the same route in 24 hours with a median lifetime of 14.5 s and peaks of 90–135 connections per hour. `RemoteStreamMuxServer` takes an optional `RemoteStreamDiagnostics` (injectable sink + slow threshold) and emits four single-line diagnostics: a first item slower than the threshold (endpoint + elapsedMs), a heartbeat tick the event loop delayed past its interval (driftMs, at most one line per 5 seconds), a heartbeat terminate (missed-Pong count), and one line per socket close (code, reason clipped to 120 characters, socket lifetime, logical streams opened, heartbeat-caused flag). The new `TypertGatewayService.Config.diagnosticsSlowMs` (default 3000) sets the threshold. The sink writes `process.stderr`: this tree registers no console exporter (cordis's built-in exporter only appends to a memory ring buffer), so `ctx.logger` lines never reach an operator log, while the deployment's supervisor folds stderr into the service log; the sink stays injectable for a composition that does serve an exporter. Healthy traffic emits only the per-close line, and no line carries stream payloads or Session values. Evidence: the new `Remote stream mux diagnostics` block in `packages/api/gateway/tests/stream-server.host.spec.ts` (every line, the throttle, the reason clip, the heartbeat-caused close) plus the `diagnosticsSlowMs` bounds and the stderr routing through the composed Host in `packages/api/gateway/tests/gateway-stream.host.spec.ts`. Marked files: `packages/api/gateway/src/index.ts`, `packages/api/gateway/src/stream-server.ts`, and both specs; registered as a new Tier C row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`. Agent Note: [remote-stream-carrier-diagnostics](.agents/notes/implemented/feature/2026-09-21-remote-stream-carrier-diagnostics.md).
- ZH: `packages/api/gateway` 现在会说明一条 Remote stream 载波为何存活、为何断开。实测的线上证据：浏览器侧 mux WebSocket 每约 15 秒重建一次并持续数小时，而这条路径此前一行日志都没有，于是心跳 terminate、ready/首帧迟发（事件循环被别处占用）与对端或代理关闭三者无法区分；traefik 记录同一 route 24 小时内 468 条连接、寿命中位 14.5 秒、峰值每小时 90–135 条。`RemoteStreamMuxServer` 新增可选的 `RemoteStreamDiagnostics`（可注入 sink + 慢阈值），输出四类单行诊断：首帧慢于阈值（endpoint + elapsedMs）、事件循环令心跳 tick 迟到（driftMs，最多每 5 秒一条）、心跳 terminate（missed 计数）、以及每次 socket 关闭一行（code、截断到 120 字符的 reason、socket 存活时长、已开逻辑流数、是否心跳所致）。新增的 `TypertGatewayService.Config.diagnosticsSlowMs`（默认 3000）设定该阈值。sink 写 `process.stderr`：本仓库没有注册任何 console exporter（cordis 内置 exporter 只写内存环形缓冲），`ctx.logger` 的行到不了运维日志，而本部署由 supervisor 把 stderr 折进服务日志；sink 保持可注入，供确有 exporter 的组合改投 logger。健康流量只产出每次关闭那一行，任何一行都不携带 stream payload 或 Session 值。证据：`packages/api/gateway/tests/stream-server.host.spec.ts` 新增的 `Remote stream mux diagnostics` 块（每一类行、限流、reason 截断、心跳致死的关闭行），以及 `packages/api/gateway/tests/gateway-stream.host.spec.ts` 中 `diagnosticsSlowMs` 的边界与组合 Host 上的 stderr 路由。带标记文件：`packages/api/gateway/src/index.ts`、`packages/api/gateway/src/stream-server.ts` 与两个规格；在 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 登记为新的 Tier C 行。Agent Note：[remote-stream-carrier-diagnostics](.agents/notes/implemented/feature/2026-09-21-remote-stream-carrier-diagnostics.md)。

### 2026-09-21 — A connection retains whole decoded session logs by revision / 连接按 revision 保留整份已解码会话日志

- EN: `packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite` gains `decodedLogCacheBytes` (`z.natural().default(0)`; `0` = off), a bounded per-connection LRU of whole decoded logs keyed by session id. Why: every whole-log read decompresses, parses, validates, and freezes the stored rows, so resuming a Session, reconnecting a client, or re-listing a catalog rebuilds an identical object graph on data that has not changed. The cold catalog listing measured on a 422-subagent workspace — 423 whole-log decodes per pass across 4–5 passes, one core pinned at 100% for about 8 minutes (the listing-bound entry above) — is the same cost seen from the other side: that bound caps how many reads one pass starts, while each pass decoded essentially the whole corpus again because nothing in the store remembered a log it had already produced. Measured A/B on the isolated production-isomorphic instance against this deployment's 66,736-event Session: repeat whole read 3.5 s → 0.30 s (about 11×), both hits the same frozen object (`identitySame: true`), that Session's retained log charged 252.2 MB of decoded JSON text, and process heap about 0.69 GB with the cache on against 1.96 GB with it off (retention replaced an accumulation of unreferenced decoded graphs with the one graph it keeps). Correctness: a hit requires the revision read from the session row in the same `readTransaction` as the event rows — no extra statement, no await before the reuse — so a write by any connection or process misses; every local write path bumps that revision inside its transaction and drops the session's entry after commit, header materialization included (it now bumps too, closing the reviewed hole where a cached log masked another connection's header write and bypassed the inherited-cut validation with it); `close()` clears. The ceiling charges each log the UTF-8 `Buffer.byteLength` of its decoded JSON text, evicts least-recently-used entries, and never retains a log larger than the whole ceiling. Default off keeps the released behavior unchanged; a deployment sizes it from its profile row beside `cacheSizeKib` and `asyncCodec`. Evidence: `packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests/decoded-log-cache.spec.ts` (16 cases pinning I1–I7 plus the schema default and the rejection of `-1`/`1.5`), the package suite (`npx vitest run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` → 10 files passed / 1 skipped, 162 passed / 2 skipped of 164, exit 0), and a mutation round in which removing the revision check, charging UTF-16 code units instead of UTF-8 bytes, or removing the ceiling each fails exactly its owning case; that round also found the cross-connection `materializeHeader` path has no failing-case coverage, which the note records as a named gap. Registered as a new Tier A row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`, which clears the `unregistered` finding the new spec's fork marker raised. Agent Note: [decoded-log-cache](.agents/notes/implemented/feature/2026-09-21-decoded-log-cache.md).
- ZH: `packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite` 新增 `decodedLogCacheBytes`（`z.natural().default(0)`；`0` = 关闭），一个按会话 id 索引、有界、按连接持有的整份已解码日志 LRU。原因：每一次整日志读取都要解压、解析、校验并冻结存储行，因此恢复会话、重连客户端或重新列 catalog 都会在未变化的数据上重建一份完全相同的对象图。在有 422 个子会话的 workspace 上实测到的冷 catalog listing——每遍 423 次整日志解码、共 4–5 遍，单核被钉在 100% 约 8 分钟（即上一条 listing 上界记录）——正是同一笔成本的另一半：那道上界限制单遍发起多少读取，而每一遍几乎都重新解码了整个 corpus，因为存储里没有任何东西记得它已经产出过的日志。在与生产同构的隔离实例上、针对本部署那条 66,736 事件的会话做的 A/B 实测：重复整读 3.5 秒 → 0.30 秒（约 11×），两次命中返回同一个冻结对象（`identitySame: true`），该会话的保留日志被计费 252.2 MB 解码后 JSON 文本，进程 heap 在开启缓存时约 0.69 GB、关闭时约 1.96 GB（保留把不断堆积却无人引用的解码图换成了它唯一持有的那一份）。正确性：命中要求会话行的 revision 与事件行来自同一个 `readTransaction`——不额外发语句，复用之前也没有 await——因此任何连接或进程的写入都会未命中；每条本地写路径都在自己的事务内推动该 revision，并在提交后删除该会话的条目，header 物化也在其中（它现在同样推动 revision，补上了审查指出的漏洞：缓存日志曾掩盖另一个连接的 header 写入，并连带绕过 inherited-cut 校验）；`close()` 清空。上限按每条日志解码后 JSON 文本的 UTF-8 `Buffer.byteLength` 计费，按最久未使用淘汰，且永不保留比整体上限还大的日志。默认关闭使已发布行为保持不变；部署在自己的 profile 行里与 `cacheSizeKib`、`asyncCodec` 并列设定它。证据：`packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests/decoded-log-cache.spec.ts`（16 用例，钉住 I1–I7、schema 默认值与对 `-1`/`1.5` 的拒绝）、包套件（`npx vitest run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` → 10 文件通过 / 1 跳过，164 中 162 通过 / 2 跳过，exit 0），以及一轮变异验证：去掉 revision 校验、把 UTF-8 字节换成 UTF-16 码元、或去掉上限，各自恰好让对应用例失败；同一轮还发现跨连接的 `materializeHeader` 路径没有失败态覆盖，该缺口已在记录中点名。在 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 登记为新的 Tier A 行，同时清掉了该规格的 fork 标记引发的 `unregistered` 发现。Agent Note：[decoded-log-cache](.agents/notes/implemented/feature/2026-09-21-decoded-log-cache.md)。

### 2026-09-21 — Decoded-log cache correctness hardening / 解码日志缓存正确性加固

- EN: a fleet review of the entry above (five read-only lines plus the Lead's own mutation/execution battery) found one real defect and several places where the tests could not have caught its return. The defect: `SqliteStoredLog.meta` was unfrozen on the current-format path — `restoreStoredLog` deep-froze the events but not `artifact.header`, and the v3 codec builds that header by spreading the decoded one (`{ ...decoder.header, version: 3 }`) — so a cache hit handed one writable header object to every later read at that revision; reproduced by execution (`first.meta.cwd = '/poisoned'` succeeded and the next cached read observed it, `frozenMeta=false` against `frozenEvents=true`). `restoreStoredLog` now freezes the header graph beside the events through the same `freezeGraph` walk, which gives the miss path and the hit path the immutable header both interfaces declare; the header's fields are primitives, so the graph walk is the whole header. Coverage closed: `tests/decoded-log-cache.spec.ts` grows from 17 to 23 cases — content differentials against an uncached reader for another connection's append, header materialization, publish, truncate and repair (a negative assertion alone is satisfied by the local post-commit drop and cannot tell a revision bump from a forgetting store), an unfit read that must not evict a resident entry, a hit that must refresh LRU recency, the frozen header plus its rejected write, and the mounted plugin actually passing the configured ceiling to the store it constructs (a wiring mutation had left the whole suite green). The packed-row charge now has its own coverage: `tests/packed-charge.spec.ts` reads a schema-19 fixture whose migrated packed row carries multibyte text back through the store under two ceilings that separate its byte count from its code-unit count, and `tests/compression.spec.ts` asserts the accumulator directly on a packed row (its local byte helper measured code units before and now measures bytes). Independent mutation run: every new case kills the mutant it was written for — revision comparison, header-materialization/append/publish/repair bumps, the oversize refusal, the LRU refresh, the plugin wiring, the header freeze, and each charge site separately — which closes the cross-connection `materializeHeader` gap the entry above named; two mutants still fail nothing (the post-commit `forgetDecodedLog` and the `close()` clear), because each only changes when bytes are released, which no public observation distinguishes. The Agent Note gains the header-freeze decision and I8 and carries the corrected mutation record; the README's out-of-band-damage limitation now tells operators to stop the instance before restoring a backup over the file. Package suite: `npx vitest run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` → 12 files passed, 172 passed / 1 skipped of 173, exit 0. Updated files: `packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/src/restore.ts`, `tests/decoded-log-cache.spec.ts`, `tests/packed-charge.spec.ts` (new), `tests/resources/sql/insert-schema-19-multibyte-events.sql` (new), `tests/compression.spec.ts`, `tests/test-sql.ts`, both READMEs, the Agent Note pair, and the retained-logs row in `FORK_SURFACE.md`.
- ZH: 对上面那条改动做的一次舰队 review（五条只读线索加上主线程自己的变异/执行验证）发现一个真实缺陷，以及若干「缺陷回归时测不出来」的覆盖空档。缺陷：当前格式路径上 `SqliteStoredLog.meta` 未被冻结——`restoreStoredLog` 深冻结了事件却没冻结 `artifact.header`，而 v3 codec 是用展开解码结果的方式构造该 header 的（`{ ...decoder.header, version: 3 }`）——因此缓存命中会把同一个可写 header 对象交给同一 revision 上的每一次后续读取；已实跑复现（`first.meta.cwd = '/poisoned'` 写入成功，且下一次命中读到了被改的值，`frozenMeta=false` 而 `frozenEvents=true`）。现在 `restoreStoredLog` 通过同一条 `freezeGraph` 遍历把 header 图与事件一并冻结，使未命中路径与命中路径交出的都是两处接口所声明的不可变 header；header 的字段全是原始值，因此图遍历覆盖的就是整个 header。补齐的覆盖：`tests/decoded-log-cache.spec.ts` 从 17 条增至 23 条——对另一连接的 append、header 物化、publish、truncate 与 repair 各自做「与无缓存读取者的内容差分」（单独的否定断言会被本地提交后删除满足，区分不了 revision 推动与「删除条目」）、装不下的读取不得淘汰在位条目、命中必须刷新 LRU 新旧序、header 已冻结且写入抛错、以及挂载出的插件确实把配置上限传给了它构造的 store（此前的接线变异会让整个套件保持全绿）。packed 行计费也有了专属覆盖：`tests/packed-charge.spec.ts` 把一份 schema-19 fixture 经迁移链读回带多字节文本的 packed 行，用两个把字节数与码元数分开的上限断言；`tests/compression.spec.ts` 另有一条直接断言累加器的 packed 行用例（其本地字节 helper 此前按码元计量，现按字节）。独立变异轮次：每条新用例都杀死了它为之而写的变异——revision 比较、header 物化/append/publish/repair 的推动、超限拒绝、LRU 刷新、插件接线、header 冻结，以及两个计费点各自——这也关掉了上一条记录点名的跨连接 `materializeHeader` 缺口；仍有两个变异什么都不失败（提交后的 `forgetDecodedLog` 与 `close()` 清空），因为它们只改变字节何时释放，没有任何公开观测能区分。Agent Note 增加了 header 冻结决策与 I8，并写入修正后的变异记录；README 的带外损坏限制新增一句：把备份恢复到文件之上前必须先停实例。包套件：`npx vitest run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests` → 12 文件通过，173 中 172 通过 / 1 跳过，exit 0。改动文件：`packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/src/restore.ts`、`tests/decoded-log-cache.spec.ts`、`tests/packed-charge.spec.ts`（新增）、`tests/resources/sql/insert-schema-19-multibyte-events.sql`（新增）、`tests/compression.spec.ts`、`tests/test-sql.ts`、两份 README、Agent Note 双语对，以及 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 的保留日志行。

### 2026-09-21 — Pin the packed-row charge to its exact byte count / 把 packed 行计费钉成精确字节数

- EN: `tests/packed-charge.spec.ts` gains a third ceiling one byte below the measured byte count, which must refuse the log, plus per-row unit assertions that keep the multibyte payload inside the packed row rather than in a scalar row. The two original ceilings only bracketed the total charge inside a 64-byte window, so any partial sum that dropped an earlier row's bytes stayed green: a packed-row charge that assigns instead of accumulating (`+=` mutated to `=`) kept every case in the package passing while the store retained logs past its ceiling. The third ceiling reproduces that mutant as a failure, verified by running the mutation with and without the added case. Test-only; no implementation change.
- ZH: `tests/packed-charge.spec.ts` 新增第三个上限——恰好比实测字节数少一字节，必须拒绝保留——以及逐行单位断言，确保多字节载荷位于 packed 行而不是某条 scalar 行。原来的两个上限只把总计费夹在一个 64 字节宽的窗口里，于是任何丢掉前面若干行字节的部分和都能保持全绿：把 packed 行的计费写成赋值而非累加（`+=` 变异为 `=`）时，整个包的用例仍然通过，而 store 会保留超出自身上限的日志。第三个上限把该变异复现为失败，已通过「加与不加这条用例」两次实跑变异验证。仅测试改动，未改实现。

### 2026-09-21 — A decoded-log cache hit stops reading the session log / 解码日志缓存命中不再读取会话日志

- EN: `SqliteStore.loadStoredLog` now decides the hit inside its one read transaction and before the event rows are queried, so a hit prepares `select-session` and no event-row statement, and no longer pays the integer session-key lookup the miss path needs. Measured on a synthetic 66,000-event Session (11,000 turns): hit median 80.6 ms → 0.025 ms, p95 0.342 ms, zero event-row statements across twenty hits, cold read unchanged at about 0.75 s. A revision-only probe in a second transaction was rejected: it would let the row that selects the codec come from one snapshot and the events from another, could raise a false inherited-cut corruption error, and breaks the registered invariant that the revision comes from the same `readTransaction` as the events. Evidence: new `tests/hit-no-event-read.spec.ts` — a `vi.mock('node:sqlite')` recorder requiring one event-row statement, one session-key lookup and one `BEGIN`/`COMMIT` pair on a cold read, and none of the first two plus one `select-session` and the same pair on a hit — the existing 24 cache cases, and mutation runs in which moving the decision back behind the query, answering without reading the session row, or fetching the session key on a hit each fail that case; that file also injects a committed foreign write before the event query and requires the read to answer the snapshot it read, and a warm entry whose session row is deleted out of band answers nothing. The README limitation that a hit still reads the event rows is retired with its test.
- ZH: `SqliteStore.loadStoredLog` 现在在**同一个读事务内、查询事件行之前**判定命中，因此命中只 prepare 一条 `select-session`、不 prepare 任何事件行语句，也不再付出未命中路径才需要的整数会话键查询。在合成的 66,000 事件会话（11,000 轮）上实测：命中中位 80.6 ms → 0.025 ms，p95 0.342 ms，二十次命中零条事件行语句，冷读不变（约 0.75 秒）。另开第二个事务只读 revision 的方案被否决：那会让选 codec 的那一行与事件行来自不同快照、可能抛出假的 inherited-cut 损坏错误，并且违反「revision 与事件行来自同一个 `readTransaction`」这条已登记不变量。证据：新增 `tests/hit-no-event-read.spec.ts`——用 `vi.mock('node:sqlite')` 记录器要求冷读恰有一条事件行语句、一次会话键查询和一对 `BEGIN`/`COMMIT`，命中则前两者为零、含一条 `select-session` 且事务对相同——加上既有 24 个缓存用例，以及变异实跑：把判定挪回事件行查询之后、不读会话行就回答命中、命中时顺手取会话键，三者各自让该用例失败；同一文件还在事件行查询之前注入一次已提交的外部写入，要求这次读取按它读到的快照作答，而一条已热条目对应的会话行被带外删除时必须什么都不返回。README 里「命中仍要读取事件行」的限制随其用例一并退场。

### 2026-09-22 — Phone drawer hosts the settings seat in its brand row / 手机抽屉把设置席位放进品牌行

- EN: In the phone drawer the Settings entry was a 42px full-width row pinned to the bottom of the 300px overlay, directly under the session list at the thumb's resting spot, so a scroll or a session tap there opened Settings. The frame now reports the drawer regime to the sidebar occupant: `SidebarOwnerProps` gains the required `mobile` field, which AppFrame sets in its drawer branch and clears for the desktop column. `SidebarRoot` renders the `sidebar.settings` seat in the brand row directly after the brand while `mobile` is true, with the compact owner share `{ wide: false }`, and the foot keeps only `sidebar.footer.action`; the brand row packs from the leading edge with the toggle pinned to the trailing edge, and the brand stops growing, so the blank beside it is no longer part of the New Session button. Desktop expanded and the collapsed rail are unchanged, and the committed sidebar DOM snapshots still match (they render `mobile: false`). Consequence: the compact seat carries no connection control, so the drawer no longer shows the connection pill or its manual reconnect action on phones; the wide desktop foot seat keeps both. Files: `packages/client/ui-layout/src/client/{index.ts,AppFrame.tsx}`, `packages/client/ui-sidebar/src/client/{SidebarRoot.tsx,SidebarRoot.module.css,contract/slots.ts}`, the ui-sidebar / ui-settings-general README pairs, the regenerated `packages/extensions/cordis-client-runner/src/client/slot-catalog.ts`, and the Agent Note `2026-09-22-phone-drawer-settings-seat`. Verified: `pnpm exec vitest run packages/client/ui-layout packages/client/ui-sidebar` → 61 files / 600 tests green; `pnpm run test:gui` green except two `ui-tool` failures that fail identically on unmodified master; `verify-client-catalog`, `verify-agent-note-format` (359 notes), `verify-fork-surface.ts` and the re-recorded translation pairs green; browser round at 412×915 on a second `dsh web` instance (port 3097, own `DSH_HOME`, single-ownership proven through `/proc/*/fd`) over the rebuilt bundles — drawer opened from the frame opener, closed through the scrim and Escape, Settings opened from the brand-row icon as the portalled full-screen phone dialog, and desktop 1280 plus the collapsed rail keep the foot placement.
- ZH: 在手机抽屉里，Settings 入口此前是一条 42px 通栏行，固定在 300px 覆盖层底部、紧贴会话列表并正落在拇指停留处，因此在该处滚动或点选会话会打开 Settings。frame 现在把抽屉形态报告给侧边栏占用方：`SidebarOwnerProps` 新增必填的 `mobile` 字段，由 AppFrame 在抽屉分支置真、桌面列置假。`mobile` 为真时，`SidebarRoot` 把 `sidebar.settings` 席位渲染在品牌行中品牌的正后方，并传入紧凑 owner 参数 `{ wide: false }`；页脚只保留 `sidebar.footer.action`。品牌行从行首排布、折叠按钮固定在行尾，且品牌不再伸展，因此品牌旁的空白不再是 New Session 按钮的一部分。桌面展开态与收起轨道不变，既有侧栏 DOM 快照仍然匹配（它们渲染的是 `mobile: false`）。后果：紧凑席位不携带连接状态控件，手机抽屉不再显示连接胶囊及其手动重连操作；桌面宽版页脚席位两者都保留。改动文件：`packages/client/ui-layout/src/client/{index.ts,AppFrame.tsx}`、`packages/client/ui-sidebar/src/client/{SidebarRoot.tsx,SidebarRoot.module.css,contract/slots.ts}`、ui-sidebar / ui-settings-general 的 README 双语对、重新生成的 `packages/extensions/cordis-client-runner/src/client/slot-catalog.ts`，以及 Agent Note `2026-09-22-phone-drawer-settings-seat`。已验证：`pnpm exec vitest run packages/client/ui-layout packages/client/ui-sidebar` → 61 文件 / 600 用例全绿；`pnpm run test:gui` 除两条在未改动 master 上同样失败的 `ui-tool` 用例外全绿；`verify-client-catalog`、`verify-agent-note-format`（359 条笔记）、`verify-fork-surface.ts` 与重录的翻译配对全绿；在第二个 `dsh web` 实例（3097 端口、独立 `DSH_HOME`、经 `/proc/*/fd` 证明单一所有权）上针对重新构建的产物做 412×915 浏览器验证——用 frame 浮动按钮打开抽屉、用遮罩与 Escape 关闭、从品牌行图标打开经 portal 的全屏手机设置弹窗，桌面 1280 与收起轨道的页脚位置保持不变。

### 2026-09-22 — Session hover card removal / 会话行悬浮卡片移除

- EN: The left sidebar's Session-row hover card is deleted in this deployment: the portaled popup upstream opens after a 500 ms dwell with the full display title, the ago-wrapped relative time ("1分钟前"), and one status line per live status ("空闲"/"已完成"/"进行中"/subagent count). The owner does not want that popup in the session list — the row itself carries the title, the compact relative time, and the status dot, so the card only duplicated the row while following the pointer. `SessionNodeItem` returns its row element directly instead of the `HoverCard` wrapper, and `SessionHoverContent`, `hoverTimeLabel`, the `.hoverStatus` CSS block, and both dictionaries' `time.ago` template are deleted with it. The Workspace-row card stays upstream's, so `HoverCard` and the `.hoverContent`/`.hoverTitle`/`.hoverPath`/`.hoverTime` rules, `WorkspaceHoverContent`, `createdLabel`, `abbreviateHomePath`, and the `hover.created`/`hover.copied`/`date.ymd` keys keep their consumer. Removing the wrapper also removes the `display: block` span around each session row: `.groupSection > * + *` keeps the 2 px row spacing and the row's drag markers were already positioned against the row itself. Registered as a Tier C row in `FORK_SURFACE.md` and `FORK_SURFACE.zh.md` with the re-apply procedure, and recorded in the Agent Note `2026-09-22-session-hover-card-removal`. Files: `packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/rows/Rows.tsx`, `packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/rows/Rows.module.css`, `packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/locales.ts`, `packages/client/ui-workspace/tests/rows.client.spec.tsx`, `apps/web/tests/workspace-management.e2e.ts`, the ui-workspace README pair, the two surface files, and the Agent Note triplet. Verified: `pnpm vitest run packages/client/ui-workspace` → 10 files / 159 tests green; `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` → 132/132 marked files registered, 68 inventory rows in both languages, no findings outside the baseline; `pnpm run test:docs` → 16/16 gates green; `pnpm run verify-client-ui-i18n` reports no violation in the touched packages — it fails on one pre-existing violation in `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/open-file-routing.ts` ("path open failed:"), reproduced unchanged with this change stashed.
- ZH: 本部署删除左侧栏的会话行悬浮卡片：上游在停留 500 ms 后打开的那张经 portal 的弹层——完整显示标题、套「…前」模板的相对时间（「1分钟前」），以及每个实时状态各一行状态文案（「空闲」／「已完成」／「进行中」／子代理数量）。所有者不需要会话列表里的这个弹层——行本身已呈现标题、紧凑相对时间与状态点，卡片只是重复行内容并随指针移动。`SessionNodeItem` 直接返回自己的行元素而不再包 `HoverCard`，同时删除 `SessionHoverContent`、`hoverTimeLabel`、`.hoverStatus` CSS 块以及两个字典中的 `time.ago` 模板。Workspace 行的卡片保持上游实现，因此 `HoverCard` 与 `.hoverContent`／`.hoverTitle`／`.hoverPath`／`.hoverTime` 规则、`WorkspaceHoverContent`、`createdLabel`、`abbreviateHomePath`，以及 `hover.created`／`hover.copied`／`date.ymd` 键仍有消费者。去掉包装同时去掉套在每个会话行外的 `display: block` span：`.groupSection > * + *` 继续提供 2 px 行距，行自身的拖拽标记本就相对行定位。已在 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 与 `FORK_SURFACE.zh.md` 登记为 Tier C 行并写明重放流程，同时记录于 Agent Note `2026-09-22-session-hover-card-removal`。改动文件：`packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/rows/Rows.tsx`、`packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/rows/Rows.module.css`、`packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/locales.ts`、`packages/client/ui-workspace/tests/rows.client.spec.tsx`、`apps/web/tests/workspace-management.e2e.ts`、ui-workspace README 双语对、两个 surface 文件，以及 Agent Note 三件套。已验证：`pnpm vitest run packages/client/ui-workspace` → 10 文件 / 159 用例全绿；`npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` → 132/132 带标记文件全部登记、两种语言各 68 条清单行、基线之外零发现；`pnpm run test:docs` → 16/16 门禁全绿；`pnpm run verify-client-ui-i18n` 在本次改动触及的包中零违规——它失败于一条既有违规 `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/open-file-routing.ts`（"path open failed:"），在把本次改动 stash 掉后原样复现。

### 2026-09-22 — Session-row `⋯` menu contribution point / 会话行 ⋯ 菜单扩展位

- EN: The sidebar's session row gains a contribution point for its `⋯` menu, so an out-of-tree plugin can add a row (with a hover submenu) beside Rename / Fork / Archive without a fork-side knowledge of that plugin. A new fork module `packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/fork/session-row-menu.ts` owns the registry — `register` (throws on a duplicate id, or on one the row cannot address — empty, carrying the leaf separator, or shadowing a built-in `rename`/`fork`/`archive`; the returned disposer removes the registration it still owns), `snapshot` (one array reference until a registration changes), `subscribe` — and its `SessionRowMenuContribution` shape `{ id, label, icon?, submenu(sessionId), onSelect(sessionId, leafId) }`. `packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/index.ts` provides it as the client service `sessionRowMenu` and publishes its source as the inject face's `hooks.sessionRowMenu`; `contract/slots.ts` types that hook on `WorkspaceBrowserInjected['hooks']`; `WorkspaceBrowser.tsx` reads it through `useSessionRowMenu` and threads `rowMenu` into both row call sites (grouped `SessionTree` and `FlatList`); `Rows.tsx` appends each contribution after the three built-in rows, hides one whose `submenu(sessionId)` returns no leaf, namespaces leaf ids as `<contributionId>\u0000<leafId>` when handing them to `Menu` (so two registrants cannot collide), and un-prefixes before calling the contributor's `onSelect` — the built-in `if (id === 'rename' | 'fork' | 'archive')` chain keeps its unknown-id-free form with no `else` fallback. `Menu`'s one-level hover submenu is upstream and unpatched. The consumer is the `dsh-session-snooze` plugin this deployment mounts: its footer bell and due queue were replaced by a `延后提醒` row whose submenu defers a session for 1h/8h/1d/3d or a custom amount and, while a record governs the session, offers `取消延后（剩余 …）`. Nothing lights up on its own: the plugin writes a `localStorage` record and the same menu is the only way back to it. Files: the new fork module, the four marked injections above, `packages/client/ui-workspace/tests/session-row-menu.client.spec.ts` (new) plus `tests/apply.client.spec.ts`, `tests/rows.client.spec.tsx`, and `tests/workspace-browser.client.spec.tsx`, `FORK_SURFACE.md`, `FORK_SURFACE.zh.md`, and the Agent Note `2026-09-22-session-row-menu-contributions`. Verified: `npx vitest run packages/client/ui-workspace` → 11 files / 168 tests green; `pnpm run test:gui` → 2 failed / 5427 passed (both failures are the pre-existing `packages/client/ui-tool` file-path-click cases, reproduced identically on a clean checkout); `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` → all marked files registered, inventory rows present in both languages, no findings outside the baseline; `pnpm run test:docs` → green; a second `dsh web` instance on port 3100 started from the deployment clone that carries this patch, with its own `DSH_HOME` and session database, where the menu row, the hover submenu, a preset deferral, the `取消延后（剩余 1 小时）` leaf, and the custom-duration dialog were exercised in the browser.
- ZH: 侧栏会话行的 `⋯` 菜单新增一个贡献点，使外部插件能在 重命名 / 分叉会话 / 归档会话 之外加一行（带悬停子菜单），而 fork 不需要知道那个插件。新 fork 模块 `packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/fork/session-row-menu.ts` 拥有注册表——`register`（重复 id，或行无法寻址的 id——空、携带叶子分隔符、影子化内置 `rename`/`fork`/`archive`——均抛错；返回的 disposer 只移除仍属于它的那次注册）、`snapshot`（在注册变化前返回同一数组引用）、`subscribe`——以及 `SessionRowMenuContribution` 形态 `{ id, label, icon?, submenu(sessionId), onSelect(sessionId, leafId) }`。`packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/index.ts` 把它作为客户端服务 `sessionRowMenu` 提供，并把它的源发布为 inject face 的 `hooks.sessionRowMenu`；`contract/slots.ts` 在 `WorkspaceBrowserInjected['hooks']` 上声明该 hook 类型；`WorkspaceBrowser.tsx` 用 `useSessionRowMenu` 读取并把 `rowMenu` 下传到两处行渲染（分组 `SessionTree` 与 `FlatList`）；`Rows.tsx` 把每条贡献追加在内置三行之后，`submenu(sessionId)` 返回空数组的贡献对该会话隐藏，穿过 `Menu` 时把叶子 id 命名空间化为 `<contributionId>\u0000<leafId>`（两个贡献者不会撞车），调用贡献者的 `onSelect` 前再剥掉前缀——内置的 `if (id === 'rename' | 'fork' | 'archive')` 链保持无未知 id 兜底、无 `else` 的形态。`Menu` 的单层悬停子菜单是上游能力，未打补丁。消费者是本部署挂载的 `dsh-session-snooze` 插件：它的底部铃铛与到期队列被一行 `延后提醒` 取代，子菜单可把会话延后 1h/8h/1d/3d 或自定义时长，并在记录仍生效时提供 `取消延后（剩余 …）`。它不再自己亮任何东西：插件只写一条 `localStorage` 记录，回到它的唯一入口就是这个菜单。改动文件：新 fork 模块、上面四处标记注入、新增 `packages/client/ui-workspace/tests/session-row-menu.client.spec.ts` 以及 `tests/apply.client.spec.ts`、`tests/rows.client.spec.tsx`、`tests/workspace-browser.client.spec.tsx`、`FORK_SURFACE.md`、`FORK_SURFACE.zh.md`，以及 Agent Note `2026-09-22-session-row-menu-contributions`。已验证：`npx vitest run packages/client/ui-workspace` → 11 文件 / 168 用例全绿；`pnpm run test:gui` → 2 失败 / 5427 通过（两条均为既有的 `packages/client/ui-tool` 文件路径点击用例，在干净 checkout 上原样复现）；`npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` → 带标记文件全部登记、两种语言清单行齐备、基线之外零发现；`pnpm run test:docs` → 全绿；另起一个端口 3100 的 `dsh web` 实例（独立 `DSH_HOME` 与会话库，启动自带本补丁的部署 clone），在浏览器里走通菜单行、悬停子菜单、预设延后、`取消延后（剩余 1 小时）` 与自定义时长弹窗。

### 2026-09-22 — Delivered prose mentions are native-gated / 交付文件的正文提及受原生门控

- EN: On this headless Host a `present` delivery's prose mention printed one `POST /api/present.open` 409 ("Host desktop unavailable") per click and opened nothing. That route carries upstream's own `workspaceDesktop().available` check, and the deliverables card already disables its menu in that state; the prose mention had no guard and bypassed the Chat `openFile` funnel that this fork routes into better-sidebar. `ui-chat`'s `fileMentions` now wraps the provided `chatFileMentions` vocabulary with the fork module's `nativeGatedMentions`: the gesture asks `ctx.remote.session.canOpenWorkspacePath()` (upstream's session-free native-opening probe), keeps the vocabulary's own native path when a desktop is reported, and otherwise routes the path through `openFile`; a rejected probe reads as unavailable so the click still opens the file. `openFile` is extracted to a local closure so the inject face and the wrapper share one implementation. Files: `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/{apply.ts,chat/fork/open-file-routing.ts}`, `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/apply-inject.client.spec.tsx`, the FORK_SURFACE pair, and the Agent Note `2026-08-29-headless-path-open-refusal` (updated in place: EN/ZH plus the re-recorded translation pair). Verified: `pnpm exec vitest run packages/client/ui-chat packages/client/ui-deliverables` → 37 files / 475 tests green; `pnpm run test:gui` → 5414 passed with the same two pre-existing `ui-tool` failures reproduced identically on unmodified master; `pnpm run typecheck` green; lint clean on the three touched files; `verify-agent-note-format` (360 notes), `verify-fork-surface.ts` and the re-recorded translation pair green; `verify-client-ui-i18n` keeps its single pre-existing violation in the same fork module (`nativeOpenFailureText`), unchanged by this work.
- ZH: 在本无桌面主机上，`present` 交付物的正文提及每次点击都打印一条 `POST /api/present.open` 的 409（"Host desktop unavailable"）且什么都没打开。那条路由带上游自有的 `workspaceDesktop().available` 检查，交付卡片在该状态已禁用菜单；正文提及没有门控，并且绕过了本 fork 路由进 better-sidebar 的 Chat `openFile` 漏斗。`ui-chat` 的 `fileMentions` 现在用 fork 模块的 `nativeGatedMentions` 包装所提供的 `chatFileMentions` 词汇：手势先问 `ctx.remote.session.canOpenWorkspacePath()`（上游无需指定 Session 的原生打开探测），探测报有桌面时保留词汇自身的原生路径，否则把路径交给 `openFile`；探测被拒绝视作不可用，因此点击仍能打开文件。`openFile` 提取为局部闭包，使 inject 面与包装共用同一实现。改动文件：`packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/{apply.ts,chat/fork/open-file-routing.ts}`、`packages/client/ui-chat/tests/apply-inject.client.spec.tsx`、FORK_SURFACE 双语、以及 Agent Note `2026-08-29-headless-path-open-refusal`（原地更新：中英双版加重录的翻译配对）。已验证：`pnpm exec vitest run packages/client/ui-chat packages/client/ui-deliverables` → 37 文件 / 475 用例全绿；`pnpm run test:gui` → 5414 通过，两个失败与未改动 master 上原样复现的既有 `ui-tool` 失败相同；`pnpm run typecheck` 全绿；三个改动文件 lint 干净；`verify-agent-note-format`（360 条笔记）、`verify-fork-surface.ts` 与重录的翻译配对全绿；`verify-client-ui-i18n` 仍只有同一 fork 模块（`nativeOpenFailureText`）里那条既有违规，本次改动未使其变化。
### 2026-09-22 — Phone header: safe-area alignment and count-only metadata chips / 手机头部：安全区对齐与纯计数元信息 chip

- EN: Two phone-header defects fixed in one pass. (1) The frame's fixed drawer opener carries `top: calc(8px + env(safe-area-inset-top))` while the conversation header's phone blocks reserved no top inset, so on an installed-app/fullscreen device (34px emulated status bar) the button sat 33px below the title row and the title painted under the system bar; both blocks now reserve the same inset (`padding-top: calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-top))` at ≤767.98px, folded into the 560px `padding` shorthand), and the same measurement after the fix reads opener y=42 against header content y=43. (2) In a busy session the phone row was over-subscribed — title 88px, lineage chip 108px, job badge 65px, three 28px controls — and every text item truncated ("分析 mint…", ":: 32 su…", "4 bac…"); at ≤560px the lineage chip and the job badge now render their count alone, the localized label hidden but kept as the trigger's accessible name, and the `.crumbs` floor rises 88 → 140px, with the same session measuring title 172px and chip 35px. Desktop and the rail are unchanged: the compact span is `display: none` outside the phone block (desktop chip 94px, full label). Files: `packages/client/ui-conversation/src/client/skeleton/ConversationRoot.module.css`, `packages/client/ui-jobs/src/client/{JobListAction.tsx,JobListAction.module.css}`, `packages/client/ui-subagent/src/client/{SubagentHeaderLineage.tsx,SubagentHeaderLineage.module.css}`, the three phone-styles specs, `FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh.md` (rows for the conversation header and ui-jobs, plus the new ui-subagent row), and the updated phone-header Agent Note triplet (both languages with its `.i18n.yaml`). Verified: 469 tests across the three packages green; `pnpm run test:gui` green except the two pre-existing `ui-tool` failures also red on unmodified master; client typecheck exit 0; documentation, fork-surface and client-catalog gates green; browser check on a second `dsh web` instance (port 3098, own `DSH_HOME` over a `VACUUM INTO` snapshot of the production session database, single ownership proven through `/proc/*/fd`) at 412×915 with a DevTools-emulated 34px top inset, plus a 1280px regression crop.
- ZH: 一轮修掉两个手机头部缺陷。(1) frame 固定的抽屉按钮带 `top: calc(8px + env(safe-area-inset-top))`，而会话 header 的手机块不预留顶部内边距，于是在装成 App/全屏的设备上（模拟 34px 状态栏）按钮比标题行低 33px、标题还绘制在系统栏之下；现在两个块预留同一内边距（≤767.98px 为 `padding-top: calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-top))`，560px 块并入 `padding` 简写），修后同一测量为按钮 y=42、header 内容 y=43。(2) 忙碌会话下手机行被过度占用——标题 88px、lineage chip 108px、job 徽标 65px、三个 28px 控件——每段文字都被截断（"分析 mint…"、":: 32 su…"、"4 bac…"）；现在 560px 以下 lineage chip 与 job 徽标只渲染计数，本地化文案被隐藏但仍是触发按钮的可访问名称，`.crumbs` 底线由 88px 提高到 140px，同一会话实测标题 172px、chip 35px。桌面与轨道不变：紧凑 span 在手机块之外为 `display: none`（桌面 chip 94px，完整文案）。改动文件：`packages/client/ui-conversation/src/client/skeleton/ConversationRoot.module.css`、`packages/client/ui-jobs/src/client/{JobListAction.tsx,JobListAction.module.css}`、`packages/client/ui-subagent/src/client/{SubagentHeaderLineage.tsx,SubagentHeaderLineage.module.css}`、三个 phone-styles 测试、`FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh.md`（会话 header 与 ui-jobs 行、新增的 ui-subagent 行），以及更新后的手机 header Agent Note 三件套（双语加 `.i18n.yaml`）。已验证：三个包 469 条用例全绿；`pnpm run test:gui` 除两条在未改动 master 上同样失败的 `ui-tool` 用例外全绿；客户端 typecheck 退出 0；文档、fork-surface 与 client-catalog 门禁全绿；在第二个 `dsh web` 实例（3098 端口、独立 `DSH_HOME`、其会话库为生产库的 `VACUUM INTO` 快照、经 `/proc/*/fd` 证明单一所有权）上以 412×915 + DevTools 模拟 34px 顶部安全区内边距实机验证，并补一张 1280px 回归截图。

### 2026-09-22 — Session-row menu spec reads the published hook source / 会话行菜单用例改读已发布的 hook 源

- EN: `packages/client/ui-workspace/tests/apply.client.spec.ts` asserted a rejected contribution id by calling `snapshot()` on `ctx.get('sessionRowMenu')`, but the Context face is the registrant's `SessionRowMenuService` — `register` only — while `snapshot`/`subscribe` belong to the `SessionRowMenuHandle` that `apply` provides and publishes to the browser as the inject face's `hooks.sessionRowMenu`. The case now reads that published source, like its two sibling cases, so `tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` typechecks again; the registry, the narrowed Context face, and every runtime path are unchanged. Verified: `pnpm exec tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` exit 0; `npx vitest run packages/client/ui-workspace` → 11 files / 168 tests green.
- ZH: `packages/client/ui-workspace/tests/apply.client.spec.ts` 原先在 `ctx.get('sessionRowMenu')` 上调 `snapshot()` 断言非法贡献 id 被拒，但 Context 面是贡献者的 `SessionRowMenuService`——只有 `register`——`snapshot`/`subscribe` 属于 `apply` 提供、并作为 inject face 的 `hooks.sessionRowMenu` 发布给浏览器的 `SessionRowMenuHandle`。该用例现在像同组另两条用例一样改读这个已发布的源，`tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` 因此重新通过；注册表、收窄的 Context 面与所有运行路径未变。已验证：`pnpm exec tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` 退出 0；`npx vitest run packages/client/ui-workspace` → 11 文件 / 168 用例全绿。
- EN: On this headless Host a `present` delivery's prose mention printed one `POST /api/present.open` 409 ("Host desktop unavailable") per click and opened nothing. That route carries upstream's own `workspaceDesktop().available` check, and the deliverables card already disables its menu in that state; the prose mention had no guard and bypassed the Chat `openFile` funnel that this fork routes into better-sidebar. `ui-chat`'s `fileMentions` now wraps the provided `chatFileMentions` vocabulary with the fork module's `nativeGatedMentions`: the gesture asks `ctx.remote.session.canOpenWorkspacePath()` (upstream's session-free native-opening probe), keeps the vocabulary's own native path when a desktop is reported, and otherwise routes the path through the owner's `openFile` (the Chat view's gesture path, which owns the busy state and the localized failure dialog); a rejected probe reads as unavailable so the click still opens the file. Review follow-ups in the same change deleted the dead `nativeOpenFailureText` export (no caller since the 2026-09-10 0.1.5 fork adaptation commit), corrected the Agent Note's stale claim that the Chat face throws a `fileOpen.desktopUnavailable` copy (the funnel falls back to the Sidebar resource instead), and dropped the same phantom from the FORK_SURFACE row. Files: `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/{apply.ts,chat/fork/open-file-routing.ts}`, `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/apply-inject.client.spec.tsx`, the FORK_SURFACE pair, and the Agent Note `2026-08-29-headless-path-open-refusal` (updated in place: EN/ZH plus the re-recorded translation pair). Verified: `pnpm exec vitest run packages/client/ui-chat packages/client/ui-deliverables` → 37 files / 475 tests green; `pnpm run test:gui` → 5414 passed with the same two pre-existing `ui-tool` failures reproduced identically on unmodified master; `pnpm run typecheck` green; lint clean on the touched files; `verify-agent-note-format`, `verify-fork-surface.ts` and the re-recorded translation pair green; **`verify-client-ui-i18n` now green** (605 files) — deleting the dead export cleared the one standing violation; browser re-verification on the isolated instance (port 3101, own DSH_HOME and session database) repeated the end-to-end result after the follow-ups.
- ZH: 在本无桌面主机上，`present` 交付物的正文提及每次点击都打印一条 `POST /api/present.open` 的 409（"Host desktop unavailable"）且什么都没打开。那条路由带上游自有的 `workspaceDesktop().available` 检查，交付卡片在该状态已禁用菜单；正文提及没有门控，并且绕过了本 fork 路由进 better-sidebar 的 Chat `openFile` 漏斗。`ui-chat` 的 `fileMentions` 现在用 fork 模块的 `nativeGatedMentions` 包装所提供的 `chatFileMentions` 词汇：手势先问 `ctx.remote.session.canOpenWorkspacePath()`（上游无需指定 Session 的原生打开探测），探测报有桌面时保留词汇自身的原生路径，否则把路径交给 owner 的 `openFile`（Chat 视图自己的手势路径，持有 busy 状态与本地化失败对话框）；探测被拒绝视作不可用，因此点击仍能打开文件。同一改动内的复查修复：删除死导出 `nativeOpenFailureText`（自 2026-09-10 的 0.1.5 fork 适配提交起无调用者）、修正 Agent Note 中「Chat 面抛出 `fileOpen.desktopUnavailable` 文案」的过时表述（该漏斗实际回落到 Sidebar 资源地址）、并从 FORK_SURFACE 行中去掉同一处幽灵描述。改动文件：`packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/{apply.ts,chat/fork/open-file-routing.ts}`、`packages/client/ui-chat/tests/apply-inject.client.spec.tsx`、FORK_SURFACE 双语、以及 Agent Note `2026-08-29-headless-path-open-refusal`（原地更新：中英双版加重录的翻译配对）。已验证：`pnpm exec vitest run packages/client/ui-chat packages/client/ui-deliverables` → 37 文件 / 475 用例全绿；`pnpm run test:gui` → 5414 通过，两个失败与未改动 master 上原样复现的既有 `ui-tool` 失败相同；`pnpm run typecheck` 全绿；改动文件 lint 干净；`verify-agent-note-format`、`verify-fork-surface.ts` 与重录的翻译配对全绿；**`verify-client-ui-i18n` 现已全绿**（605 文件）——删除死导出清掉了那条既有违规；复查修复后，在隔离实例（3101 端口、独立 `DSH_HOME` 与会话库）上重跑了端到端验证并复现同一结果。

### 2026-09-22 — Bounded settle hide for the conversation composer / 会话 composer 的 settle 隐藏加上限

- EN: The conversation composer disappeared on desktop with no visible cause, and only a page reload restored it. Reproduced on the deployment: restoring the GUI into a continuable subagent child while its parent-catalog read failed once (`POST /api/subagents/list` → `gateway/internal`) left `data-phase='settling'` and the 136px `[data-composer-seat]` at `visibility: hidden` (space kept, no reflow) with the 133-turn transcript painted; re-selecting the child issued no new read, and a reload with the read restored brought the composer back. Both `settling` inputs are asynchronous facts that need not settle: `parentAvailable` is written only by a **successful** `subagents.list` read (both failure branches keep at most the previous value and notify nobody, re-selecting the child refreshes the child's own catalog, and a stalled read holds the manager's single-flight entry), and `Session.doOpen` awaits the first journal frame with no timeout, rethrowing a non-remote rejection with `openState` left `loading`. The new fork module `packages/client/ui-conversation/src/client/skeleton/settle-hide.ts` adds `useSettleHide(pending, resetKey, limitMs)` with `SETTLE_HIDE_LIMIT_MS = 5000`, and `ConversationRoot` feeds its `settlePending` through it: one continuous pending period hides the seat for at most five seconds, after which the phase falls back to `hero`/`active` and the normal docked composer returns. Files: the new module, the marked call site in `ConversationRoot.tsx`, two fake-timer cases in `tests/skeleton.client.spec.tsx`, the ui-conversation README pair (the bounded-hide sentence plus the parent-catalog-retry limitation), the FORK_SURFACE pair, and the Agent Note triplet `2026-09-22-bounded-settle-hide`. Verified: `pnpm exec vitest run packages/client/ui-conversation` → 32 files / 408 tests green, both new cases failing on the pre-change tree; `pnpm exec tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` exit 0; live check on an isolated instance built from this revision (port 3099, own `DSH_HOME` whose profile points at a `VACUUM INTO` snapshot of the production session database, single ownership proven through `/proc/*/fd`) with the parent read still failing — `settling`/hidden at 1.5 s, 3.0 s and 4.8 s, `active`/visible at 5.6 s, 7.0 s and 9.0 s.
- ZH: 桌面端会话 composer 无故消失，只有刷新页面才恢复。在部署实例上复现：把 GUI 恢复到一个 continuable 子代理会话、同时让父目录读取失败一次（`POST /api/subagents/list` → `gateway/internal`），`data-phase` 停在 `settling`、136px 的 `[data-composer-seat]` 停在 `visibility: hidden`（占位保留、不重排），133 轮转写完整渲染；重新点选子会话不发起新读取，读取恢复后刷新即可让 composer 回来。`settling` 的两个输入都是不保证会落定的异步事实：`parentAvailable` 只由**成功**的 `subagents.list` 读取写入（两个失败分支最多保留旧值、不通知任何人；重新点选子会话刷新的是子会话自己的目录；挂起的读取占住 manager 的 single-flight 条目），而 `Session.doOpen` 等待日志首帧没有超时、对非 remote 异常直接 rethrow 并把 `openState` 留在 `loading`。新 fork 模块 `packages/client/ui-conversation/src/client/skeleton/settle-hide.ts` 引入 `useSettleHide(pending, resetKey, limitMs)` 与 `SETTLE_HIDE_LIMIT_MS = 5000`，`ConversationRoot` 把 `settlePending` 交给它：一段连续 pending 期最多隐藏座位 5 秒，之后 phase 回落 `hero`/`active`，正常停靠的 composer 回来。改动文件：新模块、`ConversationRoot.tsx` 里带标记的调用点、`tests/skeleton.client.spec.tsx` 两个假定时器用例、ui-conversation README 双语（有上限的隐藏说明 + 父目录无重试的限制条目）、FORK_SURFACE 双语，以及 Agent Note 三件套 `2026-09-22-bounded-settle-hide`。已验证：`pnpm exec vitest run packages/client/ui-conversation` → 32 文件 / 408 用例全绿，两个新用例在改动前的树上均失败；`pnpm exec tsc -b tsconfig.client.json` 退出 0；在本修订构建的隔离实例上实测（3099 端口、独立 `DSH_HOME`，其 profile 指向生产会话库的 `VACUUM INTO` 快照，经 `/proc/*/fd` 证明单一所有权）——父目录读取持续失败下，1.5 / 3.0 / 4.8 秒读到 `settling`/隐藏，5.6 / 7.0 / 9.0 秒读到 `active`/可见。

### 2026-09-23 — Session activation on a windowed open is deferred / 窗口化打开的会话激活改为延迟

- EN: Opening a stored Session for reading no longer activates it: the windowed opening snapshot is yielded and the request path ends there. `history.ts`'s `follow` lost the fork's second constructor callback and the `this.activate(target)` call that ran after the snapshot, and `index.ts` lost the `activate(sessionId)` method plus the constructor plumbing that fed it, so a read-only open performs the indexed suffix read alone — measured on an isolated instance against the 67,358-event Session, the opening read took 247 rows in 0.55 ms, and the eager activation that followed read the whole log (`select-events` 67,358 rows / 241 ms SQL) and decoded, validated, and froze it, blocking the shared Node event loop for 3,650.9 ms. The whole-log read and the Agent mount now happen at the first explicit resolution (a prompt, an append, a Typert lookup, or any other `resolveAgent` consumer), which is how every other consumer already activated. Nothing else changes: the follow snapshot still precedes any live frame, `snapshotCursor` and the continuity/`expectedSeq` checks are untouched, the observation fallback still promotes its prepared Session, and a Session that mounts later still replays its constructor suffix and later appends above the snapshot cursor. Contract cost: for a Session opened and never written, the GUI's live indicators (queue, jobs, status, title, checkpoint projection) now appear at first activation instead of immediately; a cold Session has no queue or jobs, and `session/control`'s baseline only covers mounted Sessions. Files: `packages/api/session-controller/src/{history.ts,index.ts}`, `tests/session-open-window.host.spec.ts` (four activation-pinning cases removed and five added — no whole-log read on a windowed open, the Session staying out of the live store, the deferred resolution mounting on demand, the deferred mount's seed suffix replaying to an open follower, and an append after the windowed opening through the real controller — plus two review-round cases: a failed deferred resolution leaving the open follower healthy, and a mount landing during the window read replaying from the constructor boundary), `tests/session-history-journal.host.spec.ts`, `tests/transport.host.spec.ts`, `tests/session-cold.host.spec.ts`, `benchmarks/session-open/session-open.worker.ts`, the FORK_SURFACE pair, and the Agent Note triplet `2026-09-23-lazy-windowed-open-activation`. Verified: `npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller` → 43 files / 822 tests green; `npx tsc -b packages/api/session-controller/tsconfig.host.json` exit 0 (the repo-wide `tsconfig.host.json` aggregate still fails on three pre-existing `BrowserAuth` TS2345 errors in untouched client test files); lint clean on the changed files; mutation checks — re-adding `this.activate(target)` fails `never reads the whole log for a windowed open`, replaying from seq 0 instead of the mount's `firstLiveSeq` fails `replays the constructor suffix when the Session is created during the window read`, and leaving a rejected resume in the facade's single-flight map fails `leaves the still-open follower healthy when the deferred resolution fails`, each reverted afterwards.
- ZH: 为阅读而打开一个已存会话不再激活它：窗口化打开快照交付后请求路径即结束。`history.ts` 的 `follow` 去掉了 fork 的第二个构造器回调与快照之后执行的 `this.activate(target)` 调用，`index.ts` 同时删除 `activate(sessionId)` 方法及其构造器接线，因此只读打开只做那次索引后缀读取——在隔离实例上对 67,358 事件的会话实测：打开读 247 行耗时 0.55 ms，随后的急切激活读取整段日志（`select-events` 67,358 行 / 241 ms SQL）并解码、校验、冻结，阻塞共享 Node 事件循环 3,650.9 ms。整段日志读取与 Agent 挂载现在都发生在第一次显式解析时（发送消息、追加、Typert 查找，或任何其他 `resolveAgent` 消费方），这也正是其他所有消费方此前的激活方式。其余行为不变：follow 快照仍先于任何实时帧，`snapshotCursor` 与连续性/`expectedSeq` 校验未动，观测回落仍然提升它准备好的会话，稍后挂载的会话仍在快照游标之上重放构造器种子后缀与后续追加。契约代价：对打开后从未写入的会话，GUI 的实时指示（队列、job、状态、标题、检查点投影）从立即出现改为在首次激活时出现；冷会话本就没有队列或 job，而 `session/control` 的基线只覆盖已挂载会话。改动文件：`packages/api/session-controller/src/{history.ts,index.ts}`、`tests/session-open-window.host.spec.ts`（删掉四个钉住激活的用例、新增五个——窗口化打开不读整段日志、会话不进入实时存储、延迟解析按需挂载、延迟挂载的种子后缀重放给已打开的 follower、窗口化打开之后的追加经真实控制器送达——另加两个复审用例：延迟解析失败后仍打开的 follower 保持健康、窗口读取进行中落地的挂载从构造器边界重放）、`tests/session-history-journal.host.spec.ts`、`tests/transport.host.spec.ts`、`tests/session-cold.host.spec.ts`、`benchmarks/session-open/session-open.worker.ts`、FORK_SURFACE 双语，以及 Agent Note 三件套 `2026-09-23-lazy-windowed-open-activation`。已验证：`npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller` → 43 文件 / 822 用例全绿；`npx tsc -b packages/api/session-controller/tsconfig.host.json` 退出 0（仓库级 `tsconfig.host.json` 聚合仍因未触及的客户端测试文件里既有的三个 `BrowserAuth` TS2345 错误而失败）；改动文件 lint 干净；变异检查——把 `this.activate(target)` 加回去会让 `never reads the whole log for a windowed open` 失败，用 seq 0 而非挂载的 `firstLiveSeq` 重放会让 `replays the constructor suffix when the Session is created during the window read` 失败，把被拒绝的 resume 留在 facade 的 single-flight 映射里会让 `leaves the still-open follower healthy when the deferred resolution fails` 失败，每处事后均已还原。

### 2026-09-22 — Bounded paged history reads / 分页历史读取的物理读取加上界

- EN: "Load earlier" in the Web GUI read from its page cut to the end of the log. Every older page issued the same `SELECT seq, type, time, data, source_event_seqs, surface_op, ignorable FROM events WHERE session_id = ? AND seq >= ? ORDER BY seq` through `SqliteStore.loadStoredFrom` → `SqliteSessionPersistence.readFrom` → the fork's `SeekablePersistence.readFrom`, and decoded every row from the cut upward: on this deployment's 67k-event Session the rows read grew 348 → 1345 with page depth while the page itself only needed a few dozen. The read plan already computed the page's exclusive end (`end = min(throughSeq + 1, beforeSeq)`) and passed it to `messageCut` alone; it now passes it to `readFrom` too. New `resources/sql/select-events-from-through.sql` (`… AND seq >= ? AND seq < ?`) and `resources/sql/select-max-seq.sql` (`COALESCE(MAX(seq), -1)`, index-only on the primary key), both registered in `src/sql.ts`; `loadStoredFrom(id, fromSeq, throughSeqExclusive?, signal?)` and `physicalSpanFrom` bound the physical select on a row's FIRST logical seq, keep the unbounded statement for unbounded reads, filter a packed row straddling the bound per logical event, and re-run the read unbounded when the bounded scan stops below its bound (`tornFrom < bound`) — a short suffix must never pass as the page, and the whole-log scan owns the corruption/torn-tail classification of the damage strictly below the bound (damage at or above it is never read: `loadStoredFrom(id, 0, 4)` answers `[0, 1, 2, 3]` where `loadStoredFrom(id, 0)` raises `invalid committed physical row at seq 4`, because an older page must not pay for a tail it never serves). The bounded scan runs the new `MAX(seq)` query inside the same read transaction that selects its span — never for an unbounded read, whose statement set is unchanged — and `readFrom` on the persistence faces and `page-boundary.SeekablePersistence` report the result as `storedEnd`, the highest stored logical seq this read observed (`-1` for an empty log, the validated prefix's tail for an unbounded read), with the optional bound threaded before `signal`; an absent bound is byte-identical to the released behavior. `readIndexedSuffix` answers an end at or below the window start as unsatisfiable without reading, accepts a bounded suffix only when it reaches its bound (a truncated read falls back to the observation path instead of serving a short page), and hands each read's bound and observed stored end to the caller's validator; `history.ts` runs the RELEASED cursor checks against that stored end for a page bounded below the request cursor — the bound keeps the read's own tail below the cursor — so a desynced cursor (`{throughSeq: 3000, beforeSeq: 901}` on a 1,200-event log, answered `ok: true` when this change first dropped the check) is `gateway/bad-request` on the fast path exactly as on the observation path, while a window that reached past its bound and a provider that reports no observed end fail loud `gateway/internal`; every unbounded read, and every bounded read whose end is the cursor plus one, keeps the released tail checks verbatim. An out-of-tree provider still implementing the released three-argument `readFrom(id, fromSeq, signal?)` receives the bound in its `signal` position: one that answers its released whole-tail read is refused loud (the window past its bound), one that reports no observed end is refused loud the same way, and one that dereferences the bound as an `AbortSignal` throws inside the fast path and the observation path answers — the released result without the bound win, never a page the bound did not cover. Evidence: new `packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests/bounded-suffix-read.spec.ts` (6 cases: dense `[fromSeq, bound)` equality over a 40-turn log, a bound at or past a torn tail identical to the unbounded read, a 1,030-delta packed run read whole and filtered per member, a damaged row below the bound re-running whole log and raising the same `invalid committed physical row` error, the historical arm's filter and stored end, and an empty legacy log's `-1`), new `tests/bounded-suffix-statements.spec.ts` (3 cases: the bounded read prepares `select-events-from-through` and touches exactly the rows whose first seq precedes the bound, the packed row straddling it included; the unbounded read keeps `select-events-from` and prepares no `MAX(seq)`, while the bounded read runs `select-max-seq` once, inside its own `begin`/`commit`; an empty log answers `-1`), new `packages/api/session-controller/tests/session-page-bound.host.spec.ts` (14 cases: bounded older-page and opening-page plans equal to the whole-log page, the opening-window plan still unbounded, an unsatisfiable end unread, a short suffix serving no page, two-argument validators still accepted, a controller case where the bounded fast path serves exactly the observation path's page, the reviewer's stale-cursor shape rejected `gateway/bad-request` on both paths, a 20-shape differential battery comparing the two paths' full results (`beforeSeq === throughSeq`, `throughSeq: -1`, `beforeSeq > throughSeq`, `{5000, 4000}`, `beforeSeq` 0/1/2/3, and the opening shapes included), and a released three-argument provider that reports no observed end failing loud `gateway/internal`), two cases in `session-open-window.host.spec.ts` over a persistence double that now applies the bound, and the two `session-cold.host.spec.ts` call-arity assertions updated for the new argument. `npx vitest run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite packages/api/session-controller` → 58 files passed / 1 skipped, 1018 passed / 2 skipped of 1020, the differential spec's unbounded equality for every `fromSeq` included. The stale-cursor case and the shape battery were RED against the pre-fix fast path (`ok: true` where the observation path answered `gateway/bad-request`). Mutation round: removing the per-event upper filter fails exactly the packed-straddle and historical-arm cases; removing the SQL bound fails exactly the statement-level case and the torn-below-bound fallback; forcing the observed stored end to `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` fails exactly the stale-cursor case and the shape battery, and forcing the same value inside the store's `select-max-seq` answer fails the store's stored-end cases. `npx tsc -b packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tsconfig.json packages/api/session-controller/tsconfig.host.json` exit 0; oxlint reports no error in any touched file. Registered in `FORK_SURFACE.md` (the seek-surface row and the paging-injection row) and recorded in Agent Note `2026-09-22-bounded-page-suffix`.
- ZH: Web GUI 的「加载更早」此前从页面切点一直读到日志末尾。每个旧页都会经 `SqliteStore.loadStoredFrom` → `SqliteSessionPersistence.readFrom` → fork 的 `SeekablePersistence.readFrom` 发出同一条 `SELECT seq, type, time, data, source_event_seqs, surface_op, ignorable FROM events WHERE session_id = ? AND seq >= ? ORDER BY seq`，并从切点向上解码每一行：在本部署那条 6.7 万事件的会话上，读取行数随翻页深度从 348 涨到 1345，而页面本身只需要几十行。读取计划本就算出该页的排他终点（`end = min(throughSeq + 1, beforeSeq)`）却只把它交给 `messageCut`；现在它同样交给 `readFrom`。新增 `resources/sql/select-events-from-through.sql`（`… AND seq >= ? AND seq < ?`）与 `resources/sql/select-max-seq.sql`（`COALESCE(MAX(seq), -1)`，走主键纯索引），两者都在 `src/sql.ts` 登记；`loadStoredFrom(id, fromSeq, throughSeqExclusive?, signal?)` 与 `physicalSpanFrom` 按行的首个逻辑 seq 限制物理查询，无界读取继续使用原语句，跨界的 packed 行按逻辑事件逐条过滤，而受限扫描未到达自己的上界（`tornFrom < bound`）时退回无界重读——短后缀绝不能被当成页面，严格位于上界之下的损坏/撕裂尾部分类由整日志扫描拥有（位于上界及其之上的损坏从不被读取：`loadStoredFrom(id, 0, 4)` 回答 `[0, 1, 2, 3]`，而 `loadStoredFrom(id, 0)` 抛出 `invalid committed physical row at seq 4`——旧页不该为一个它从不端出的尾部付出代价）。受限扫描在选取跨度的同一个读事务内执行这条新的 `MAX(seq)` 查询——无界读取从不执行，其语句集保持不变——`readFrom` 在持久化面与 `page-boundary.SeekablePersistence` 上把结果报告为 `storedEnd`，即本次读取观测到的最高已存逻辑 seq（空日志 `-1`，无界读取为校验前缀的尾部），可选上界串在 `signal` 之前；不传上界时与已发布行为逐字节一致。`readIndexedSuffix` 对不高于窗口起点的终点直接判为不可满足、不读取；受限后缀只有到达自己的上界才被接受（被截断的读取回落到观测路径，而不是端出短页），并把每次读取的上界与观测到的已存末端一并交给调用方校验；`history.ts` 对「上界低于请求游标」的读取改按该已存末端执行**已发布**的游标检查——上界使该读取自身的尾部落在游标之下——于是在本次改动最初丢掉该检查时被回答 `ok: true` 的失步游标（1,200 事件日志上的 `{throughSeq: 3000, beforeSeq: 901}`）在快路径上与观测路径一样是 `gateway/bad-request`，而越过自身上界的窗口与不上报已存末端的提供方都以 `gateway/internal` 响亮失败；全部无界读取、以及终点为游标加一的受限读取，其尾部检查逐字保留。仍在实现已发布三参数 `readFrom(id, fromSeq, signal?)` 的树外提供方会在 `signal` 位置收到上界：回答已发布整尾读取的那种因窗口越过上界被响亮拒绝，不上报已存末端的那种同样被响亮拒绝，而把上界当作 `AbortSignal` 解引用的那种在快路径内抛错、由观测路径作答——即已发布结果但没有上界收益，绝不会端出上界未覆盖的页面。证据：新增 `packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tests/bounded-suffix-read.spec.ts`（6 用例：40 轮日志上 `[fromSeq, bound)` 的稠密相等、上界位于或超过撕裂尾部时与无界读取完全相同、1,030 个 delta 的 packed 行整行读出后按成员过滤、上界之下的损坏行触发整日志重读并抛出同一条 `invalid committed physical row`、历史分支的过滤与已存末端、空 legacy 日志的 `-1`），新增 `tests/bounded-suffix-statements.spec.ts`（3 用例：受限读取 prepare `select-events-from-through`，且只触及首个 seq 位于上界之前的行——包括跨界的 packed 行；无界读取仍用 `select-events-from` 且不 prepare `MAX(seq)`，受限读取则在自己的 `begin`/`commit` 之间执行一次 `select-max-seq`；空日志回答 `-1`），新增 `packages/api/session-controller/tests/session-page-bound.host.spec.ts`（14 用例：旧页与首页计划都带界且与整日志页面相等、打开窗口计划仍无界、不可满足的终点不读取、短后缀不产出页面、仍接受两参数校验闭包、一条控制器用例证明受限快路径端出的页面与观测路径完全一致、审阅者给出的陈旧游标形态在两条路径上都被 `gateway/bad-request` 拒绝、一条 20 形态差分电池对比两条路径的完整结果（含 `beforeSeq === throughSeq`、`throughSeq: -1`、`beforeSeq > throughSeq`、`{5000, 4000}`、0/1/2/3 的 `beforeSeq` 以及首页形态），以及一条「上报不了已存末端的已发布三参数提供方」响亮失败 `gateway/internal` 的用例），`session-open-window.host.spec.ts` 两条用例（其持久化替身现在也应用上界），并更新 `session-cold.host.spec.ts` 两条调用参数断言。`npx vitest run packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite packages/api/session-controller` → 58 文件通过 / 1 跳过，1020 中 1018 通过 / 2 跳过，含 differential 规格对每个 `fromSeq` 的无界相等。陈旧游标用例与形态电池在修复前的快路径上是红的（回答 `ok: true`，而观测路径回答 `gateway/bad-request`）。变异轮次：去掉按事件的过滤只让 packed 跨界与历史分支两条用例失败；去掉 SQL 上界只让语句级用例与「上界之下撕裂」回退用例失败；把观测到的已存末端强制为 `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` 只让陈旧游标用例与形态电池失败，而在 store 的 `select-max-seq` 答案里强制同一值则让 store 自己的已存末端用例失败。`npx tsc -b packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/tsconfig.json packages/api/session-controller/tsconfig.host.json` 退出 0；oxlint 在本次触及的文件中零报错。已在 `FORK_SURFACE.md` 登记（寻址面行与分页注入行），并记录于 Agent Note `2026-09-22-bounded-page-suffix`。

### 2026-09-23 — Upstream dsh-v0.1.7-rc.1 merge / 上游 dsh-v0.1.7-rc.1 合并

- EN: Merged upstream tag `dsh-v0.1.7-rc.1` into the `sync/upstream-0.1.7-rc.1` branch at the `dsh-v0.1.7-rc.1` merge ("Merge tag 'dsh-v0.1.7-rc.1' into sync/upstream-0.1.7-rc.1"), whose fork side was `dsh-v0.1.5-rc.2` plus 177 commits: 7,877 files changed (+1,206,687 / −129,862) with 221 conflicts resolved. Inventory refresh (`FORK_SURFACE.md` and `.zh.md`, 73 rows each): nine fork surfaces retired or narrowed because upstream now ships the same outcome — the `ui-workspace` session-row `⋯` contribution point (upstream ships the `sidebar.workspaces.session.menu.item` and `sidebar.workspaces.session.row.action` list slots; the fork module, its four injections, and its spec are deleted), the `ui-workspace` order-stability patch (`src/client/fork/order-stability.ts` deleted; upstream derives the promoted order in `tree.ts` through `reconcileManualOrder`/`orderByRecency`), the StateDot `.cell` chase half of the ongoing-Session row (upstream ships spinner keyframes with its own reduced-motion arc; the palette token half stays), the `ui-agent-preset` phone rule (upstream ships `@container (max-width: 540px) { .label { display: none } }`), the `tool-agent-team` non-member request render (upstream renders the static `POLICY` and moved role and name into teammate system reminders), the session-controller client `snapshot-identity.ts` plus its spec (upstream builds entry identity inline in `manager.buildListSnapshot`; the client session-controller no longer declares `SubagentCatalogSnapshot`), `tests/fake-api.client.ts` (upstream deleted it), the `ui-chat` TurnProcess partial-history fold (upstream's `processWindowReady` carries no `historyIncomplete` gate), and the TodoPanel `glyphProgress` half of the reduced-motion row (upstream renders `StateDot`; the InputBar and MenuView guards stay). New and moved carriers are registered by path: the extracted `ui-chat` reader hooks `use-chat-viewport.ts` / `use-chat-reading.ts` / `use-chat-scroll.ts` and `AssistantMarkdown.tsx`, `ui-conversation`'s `input/editor/view-binding.ts` and the skeleton views `ConversationContent.tsx` / `ConversationMainPanel.tsx` / `DefaultConversationViews.tsx`, `packages/client/web/src/boot.ts` together with `packages/client/modules/src/client/entries.ts`, and `packages/guard/turn-continuation` — a fork-owned guard plugin that had no inventory row at all (tier A; opt-in, no shipped profile mounts it, the deployment profile enables it). A new fork patch in the session-format stack is recorded after the released-v0 compat row: `packages/session/session-format-v3-to-v4/src/fork/subagent-descriptor-compat.ts` owns the interpreted descriptor generations (the installed `@deepseek-ai/dsh-subagent` stamps version 4, which the frozen edge would otherwise degrade to unknown-mode membership), with a marked re-export of `childCatalogFact` in the package entry and a marked delegation in its fact module. Behavior deltas recorded beside their rows: `history.ts`'s two fast paths take the client turn window's `minMessages` as the page floor; the subagent listing cold-read bounds (`coldReadConcurrency`, `coldReadBudget`) now ride only `listDescendants`, because upstream's `listChildren` answers from the parent catalog projection and performs no cold read; and `session-projection-cache` keeps the fork's three-argument `hydratePrepared(session, events, durableEventCount)` write-back and `rowsWithinDurablePrefix`, while `cachedSnapshot` and `cachedPredecessorTitle` are upstream's two-argument and one-argument faces. V4 adaptation in the same sync: the fork's SQLite session backend gains a children-binding pass — `src/child-catalog.ts` plus `select-child-sessions.sql`, `select-child-descriptor.sql`, and `select-child-descriptor-count.sql` — that collects each parent's direct subagent children from its own session and event rows and validates one child's fact through `childCatalogFact`, so a parent with an unreadable child descriptor keeps unknown-mode membership instead of refusing the whole V3→V4 restore; the adaptation is staged with this sync, and HEAD is still the `dsh-v0.1.7-rc.1` merge at this entry's writing. Consumer migration still open: the out-of-tree `dsh-session-snooze` plugin still injects the deleted `sessionRowMenu` service and must re-register its row through `sidebar.workspaces.session.menu.item`. Verification: the authoritative `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` run belongs to the lead once `pnpm install` settles; a read-only port of its marker census, registration coverage, reverse check, and English/Chinese parity run against this tree reports 73 + 73 rows and cuts the findings outside `scripts/fork-surface-baseline.txt` from 25 to 3 — the two generated catalogs (`packages/extensions/tool-cordis/src/api-catalog.ts`, `packages/extensions/cordis-client-runner/src/client/slot-catalog.ts`) no longer carry the fork JSDoc prose the merge replaced and need `pnpm run gen-cordis-api` and `pnpm run gen-client-catalog`; both stay outside the frozen baseline until those runs land.
