### 2026-09-23 — History paging adopts upstream's cut rule / 历史分页采用上游切点规则

- EN: The merge left history paging with a dual path: the fork's indexed fast path cut pages on user messages plus turn alignment (`page-boundary.ts`'s own boundary walk), while upstream's `history.ts::paginate` cuts on user+assistant messages and, with `turnWindow`, widens to the `turn/start` carrying both minima — so the same request could return different page content depending on whether a cache record existed. It is now unified on upstream's rule, per the dual-path policy. `paginate` gains an optional `baseSeq` (the read window's first seq) and returns the absolute `cut` plus `exhausted`; it is exported, and the new private `SessionHistoryController.windowCut(beforeSeq, maxMessages, turnWindow)` binds it per request and hands the cut to both fork read plans, so one rule cuts the page whether the fast path answers or the observation path does. `follow` now also passes the request's `turnWindow` (it had been silently dropped). `src/fork/page-boundary.ts` loses its cut rule — `nthMessageCut`, `turnAlignedCut`, and `paginateSuffix` (~110 lines) — and gains the `WindowPage`/`WindowPageCut` seam: `IndexedPagePlan.maxMessages` becomes `seedMessages` (it seeds the indexed read only; the page cap lives in the injected rule), the accept test is proof-only (`!exhausted && cut >= fromSeq`, or `fromSeq === 0`) plus a non-empty page, and an answered window must be exactly the dense `[fromSeq, end)` range or the request falls back to the observation path. The indexed physical read stays the fork's: `readIndexedSuffix`/`readIndexedPage`/`SeekablePersistence` (the indexed `messageCut` seed and the bounded `readFrom`), the `windowFloor` projection restart, the 128/4096 margin ladder, and the windowed-open checkpoint and closed-tail gates. Files: `packages/api/session-controller/src/{history.ts,fork/page-boundary.ts,fork/open-window.ts}`; `tests/session-history-journal.host.spec.ts` no longer carries the fork's widened-cut assertions (it is byte-identical to upstream's again), and `tests/session-page-bound.host.spec.ts` plus `tests/session-open-window.host.spec.ts` carry the cross-path agreement, including the turn-window shapes. Inventory: `FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh.md` row 24 records the adoption and names the indexed physical read as what remains fork-owned, row 60 drops the assertion-file path and the fast paths' self-computed `minMessages` floor (the cut now comes from `windowCut()`; `IndexedPagePlan.seedMessages` only seeds the indexed read), row 61 replaces the deleted turn-truncation check with the proof-only accept test, and row 69 records that the AppFrame drawer's current-Session watch derives its id from the list rows' `retainedBy.mainView` binding after upstream removed `SessionListState.current`. Verified: `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` → parity OK (73 rows / 9 headings in both languages), 135/135 marked files registered, no findings outside `scripts/fork-surface-baseline.txt`.
- ZH: 合并给历史分页留下了双路径：fork 的索引快路径按用户消息加轮次对齐切页（`page-boundary.ts` 自有的边界游走），而上游 `history.ts::paginate` 按 user+assistant 消息切，并在带 `turnWindow` 时展宽到同时满足两个下限的 `turn/start`——于是同一个请求会不会端出不同的页内容，取决于是否存在缓存记录。现在按双路径政策统一到上游规则。`paginate` 新增可选参数 `baseSeq`（读取窗口的首个 seq），并返回绝对 `cut` 与 `exhausted`；它被导出，新增私有方法 `SessionHistoryController.windowCut(beforeSeq, maxMessages, turnWindow)` 按请求绑定它并把切点下发给两条 fork 读取计划，因此无论快路径作答还是观测路径作答，页面都由同一条规则切出。`follow` 现在也会传递请求的 `turnWindow`（此前被静默丢弃）。`src/fork/page-boundary.ts` 删掉自己的切点规则——`nthMessageCut`、`turnAlignedCut`、`paginateSuffix`（约 110 行）——并新增 `WindowPage`/`WindowPageCut` 接缝：`IndexedPagePlan.maxMessages` 改名 `seedMessages`（只用于给索引读取播种子；页上限属于注入的规则），接受判定改为只做证明（`!exhausted && cut >= fromSeq`，或 `fromSeq === 0`）加页面非空，且被作答的窗口必须恰好是稠密的 `[fromSeq, end)` 区间，否则请求回落到观测路径。索引物理读取仍属 fork：`readIndexedSuffix`/`readIndexedPage`/`SeekablePersistence`（索引 `messageCut` 种子与受限 `readFrom`）、`windowFloor` 投影重启、128/4096 余量梯子，以及窗口化打开的检查点门槛与闭合尾部证明。改动文件：`packages/api/session-controller/src/{history.ts,fork/page-boundary.ts,fork/open-window.ts}`；`tests/session-history-journal.host.spec.ts` 不再携带 fork 的加宽切点断言（已再次与上游逐字节一致），跨路径一致性（含回合窗口形态）由 `tests/session-page-bound.host.spec.ts` 与 `tests/session-open-window.host.spec.ts` 承载。清单：`FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh.md` 第 24 行记录这次采用，并写明仍属 fork 的是索引物理读取；第 60 行删去断言文件路径与快路径自算的 `minMessages` 页面下界（切点现在来自 `windowCut()`，`IndexedPagePlan.seedMessages` 只给索引读取播种子）；第 61 行把已删除的回合截断检查换成只做证明的接受判定；第 69 行记录 AppFrame 抽屉的「当前会话」监听在上游移除 `SessionListState.current` 后改从列表行的 `retainedBy.mainView` 绑定派生 id。已验证：`npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` → 中英结构一致（各 73 行 / 9 个标题）、135/135 带标记文件全部登记、`scripts/fork-surface-baseline.txt` 之外零发现。

### 2026-09-23 — Restored children keep their descriptor identity / 被恢复的子会话保住 descriptor identity

- EN: Opening a migrated historical subagent child answered `RemoteError: subagent descriptor is corrupt` (`packages/api/session-controller/src/history.ts` `validateAddress`, code `subagent/catalog-diagnostic`) — the remaining fork adaptation gap after the `dsh-v0.1.7-rc.1` merge. Root cause is the fork's own descriptor generation: this fork stamps version 4 for the per-child `cwd`/`skillFilter` inputs (FORK_SURFACE row `subagent` per-child cwd + skillFilter), while a restored released log keeps the generation its writer stamped — version 3 in the reproducing web fixture. The released-v3 catalog edge already interprets generations 1–4 for the parent's membership (`session-persistence-sqlite` child-catalog backfill and the JSONL catalog migration), so the parent listed the child while the child's own `subagent` identity projection folded to the `null` sentinel: `foldSubagentDescriptor` reads the installed version alone, and a projection fold must never throw, so an unknown generation becomes the sentinel the address fence reports as corrupt. The fix is read-side, at the projection producer. New fork module `packages/subagent/subagent/src/fork/released-descriptor-identity.ts` (`foldReleasedDescriptorIdentity`) validates a copy of a released payload stamped with the installed version through `foldSubagentDescriptor`, so the identity comes from the same schema the strict fold uses: an undeclared member or a damaged identity field still establishes nothing, a generation at or above the installed one is untouched, and version 1 (no mode field) stays unreadable. `packages/subagent/subagent/src/projection.ts` gains one marked delegation, and the `subagent` projection's `stateVersion` moved 2→3 so rows cached as the `null` sentinel refold instead of surviving the change. `foldSubagentDescriptor` keeps refusing a released generation for the resume composition, and nothing rewrites stored payloads: upstream's contract that a migrated child's body survives V3→V4 verbatim (its `preset-migration` spec) is preserved. A second, independent adaptation landed in the same spec: upstream rc.1 added post-activation `permission/preset`, `sandbox/mode`, and `approval/policy` expectations to `apps/web/tests/preset-migration.snapshot.ts`, which this deployment cannot produce because the `permission` row (`@deepseek-ai/dsh-permission-presets`) is disabled in `packages/bundle/base/cordis.patch.yml` over the local non-confining executor; the expected tail now ends at the activation seed marker with that reason named inline. Files: the new module, `packages/subagent/subagent/src/projection.ts`, `packages/subagent/subagent/tests/released-descriptor-identity.spec.ts`, `packages/api/session-controller/tests/session-cold.host.spec.ts` (a cold child whose own descriptor carries generation 3), `apps/web/tests/preset-migration.snapshot.ts`, and the FORK_SURFACE pair (a new tier C row plus the test-updates row entry). Verified: the reproduction first — `DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay pnpm exec vitest run --config vitest.web.config.ts apps/web/tests/preset-migration.snapshot.ts` failed both cases with the reported `RemoteError`, and an instrumented read showed the child's own event `{"version":3,…}` folding to `subagent: null` with `inheritedEventCount 0`; then `pnpm exec vitest run packages/api/session-controller/` → 46 files / 960 tests green and `pnpm exec vitest run packages/subagent/subagent/ packages/api/session-controller/` → 62 files, 1315 passed with one pre-existing environment failure (`out-of-process.spec.ts` "rejects a directory without search permission", which fails identically with this change toggled off because the lane runs as uid 0); the new cold-child case and the two released-generation identity cases are RED with the delegation commented out and green with it; `DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay … apps/web/tests/preset-migration.snapshot.ts` → 2 passed; `npx tsc -b packages/subagent/subagent/tsconfig.json packages/api/session-controller/tsconfig.host.json` exit 0; oxlint clean on the five changed source/test files; `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` → no findings outside the baseline (74 rows in each language). The web lane reads built bundles, so `@deepseek-ai/dsh-subagent`'s bundle was rebuilt in this worktree (`npx tsc -b` on its leaf plus a temporary tsdown focus config, deleted afterwards).

### 2026-09-23 — Keyless Web lane re-synced to the fork composition / 无密钥 Web 泳道按 fork 组装重新对齐

- EN: Re-synced the residual red cases of the keyless Web lane (`DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay`, `vitest.web.config.ts`) to the fork's composition, test-side only — no product source changed. `shipped-composition.e2e.ts` no longer asserts that the `sandboxPolicy` service is absent: the `sandbox-policy` row stays enabled at `danger-full-access` and only `permission` is disabled, so the case pins that mode instead. The four Auto producer cases moved to `apps/web/tests/shipped-composition-auto.e2e.ts`, which `vitest.web.config.ts` excludes, because each drives the host `permissionPresets` service and the disabled `permission` row leaves the `auto-review` entry pending; `github-ready-review.e2e.ts` is excluded for the same reason — its webhook ingress never registers, so a signed ping answers 404 where the spec expects 202, a pre-existing gap the lane now records instead of claiming the feature works. `sidebar-terminal.e2e.ts` and `thinking-markdown.e2e.ts` boot through the deployment theme's documented per-browser opt-out (shared helper `optOutDeploymentTheme` in `apps/web/tests/support.ts`, which stores the theme key the query owns because the host's token redirect strips the query before boot), because `packages/client/web/src/fork/themes.css` pins a dark-only palette that masks upstream's light/dark terminal colours and its Markdown typography. `trajectory-virtualization.e2e.ts` arms its in-flight request gate for the second older-page press and raises its paging loop guard from 12 to 32, because the fork's `PAGE_MESSAGES = 8` makes the first press a real page rather than a reveal of resident rows. `chat-long-interactions.e2e.ts` re-pins the transcript tail after its fold expansion, so the rail's current mark is the last turn again. `remote-welcome.e2e.ts` asserts the fork's durable behaviour: a host-published trusted authority is a serving authority, so the acknowledgement lands in the Host settings document and the notice stays dismissed across reload. `details-session-lifecycle.e2e.ts` keeps its 767 px scenario and asserts the mobile frame's single-column track — the fork's `MOBILE_VIEWPORT = 768` owns that width and upstream's auto-fullscreen rule is `viewportWidth < 768`, so no desktop width reaches the fullscreen branch. `present.e2e.ts` withdraws the bare native open while its prose mentions run — through the file's existing `Reflect.set` idiom, counting the refused calls so the window closes after the mention's asynchronous desktop probe — so the mention reaches the Sidebar preview while the delivery card keeps its reveal and application opens; the same case's `snapshots/web/present/ui.expected.md` drops the Access chip this composition does not render. `plan-control-row.e2e.ts` scans down to a 300 px viewport and records the composition's truth: without the permission/Access chip the content-driven collapse lands on a ~301 px row, below upstream's 360 px floor, and the trigger's `min(360px, 45cqw)` cap keeps even the shorter label compact there, so `layout.expected.md` was re-recorded and two upstream facts now read `false`. `declared-reasoning.e2e.ts` stays red on its WebKit half alone: this runner has no `/root/.cache/ms-playwright/webkit-2311/pw_run.sh`, and with no guarded browser-availability skip precedent in the repository the environmental failure stays documented rather than silently skipped. Inventory: `FORK_SURFACE.md`/`.zh.md` row 45 names every re-synced file; `tsconfig.host.json` and `apps/web/tsconfig.json` register the new spec file in the host program and exclude it from the client program. ### 2026-09-24 — Recorded sessions advanced to the current generation on the fork composition / 录制会话在 fork 组装下推进到当前世代

- EN: The keyless snapshot lane (`vitest.snapshot.config.ts`) replays committed Sessions and compares the replayed output with committed goldens, and rc.1's format bump left both sides behind: 99 of 118 scenarios already failed on the frozen branch because their goldens are upstream-shaped (they carry the `permission/preset`, `sandbox/mode`, and `approval/policy` rows this composition does not mount). The sanctioned refresh mode (`DSH_SNAPSHOT=refresh`, which replays committed inputs and rewrites the expected outputs — no live API) advanced the fixtures to the current generation and re-synced the goldens with the fork composition (93 tracked files, +679/−2359; the deletions are dominated by the sandbox tool-schema and permission-row blocks), and the borrowing manifests now name their owner's selected generation. Result: 149 passed / 7 failed / 2 skipped of 158, against 17 passed / 99 failed on the frozen branch. The seven remaining are recorded-composition or environment facts, not defects: four confinement/sandbox scenarios (`background-confinement-failure`, `foreground-confinement-timeout`, `ptc-node-workspace`, `session-query-spill`) need a confinement backend this host does not have; `tool-call-turn from retained V3 input` keeps its upstream golden because refresh deliberately does not rewrite retained-input scenarios; and `compaction-output-reserve` / `compaction-summary-headroom` declare one header change while their tool-schema sidecar carries two sets, a pair the fork's composition makes unequal. Also fixed in the same pass: the corpus invariant required borrowers to name the owner's newest generation, which the earlier web re-record had moved to `session.v4.jsonl` for fourteen owners.
- ZH: 无密钥快照泳道（`vitest.snapshot.config.ts`）回放已提交会话并把结果与金样比较，rc.1 的格式升级让两侧同时落后：冻结分支上 118 个场景已有 99 个失败，因为其金样是上游形态（带 `permission/preset`、`sandbox/mode`、`approval/policy` 行，而本组装不挂载这些）。按仓库认可的 refresh 模式（`DSH_SNAPSHOT=refresh`，只回放已提交输入并重写期望输出，不调用线上 API）把夹具推进到当前世代、并按 fork 组装重新对齐金样（93 个受跟踪文件，+679/−2359，删除内容以沙箱工具 schema 与权限行块为主），借用者 manifest 亦改为指向 owner 当前选中的世代。结果：158 项中 149 通过 / 7 失败 / 2 跳过，而冻结分支为 17 通过 / 99 失败。剩余七项是记录形态或环境事实、不是缺陷：四个隔离/沙箱场景（`background-confinement-failure`、`foreground-confinement-timeout`、`ptc-node-workspace`、`session-query-spill`）需要本机没有的隔离后端；`tool-call-turn from retained V3 input` 保留上游金样，因为 refresh 有意不重写保留输入的场景；`compaction-output-reserve` / `compaction-summary-headroom` 声明一次 header 变更而工具 schema 侧车带两组集合，这是 fork 组装下不再相等的一对。同一轮还修好了语料不变量：借用者必须指向 owner 最新世代，而先前的 Web 重录已把 14 个 owner 的最新世代变为 `session.v4.jsonl`。

### 2026-09-25 — Journal frame consumption yields to the event loop / 日志帧消费让出事件循环

- EN: The Web GUI saturated its main thread while an agent streamed. Measured on the running deployment with a phone-class client (412×915, DPR 2.625, `Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate(4)`, `long-animation-frame` observation): 228 s of live streaming produced 1352 long animation frames and 164.6 s of blocking — 72% of wall time — with a worst frame of 2927 ms, while style and layout together totalled 61 ms; function-level attribution put the work inside the api-gateway client bundle's WebSocket message task. Mechanism: `packages/api/gateway/src/client/stream-client.ts` pushes decoded frames into an unbounded `StreamInbox`, and while frames are queued its `next()` resolves on an already-resolved promise, so the consumer chain (`stream-client.ts`'s async generator → `remote-stream.ts` → `RemoteJournalStream.consume()` → session-controller fold → conversation assembler → store publish) drains the whole burst through microtasks inside one WebSocket task and never returns to the event loop; the renderer gets no turn. New fork module `packages/api/gateway/src/client/fork/stream-frame-pacing.ts` records one consumed frame and, once a bounded batch is spent, awaits one `MessageChannel` macrotask raced against the stream's `AbortSignal` (no timer fallback — every supported host defines `MessageChannel`): `FRAME_PACING_TIME_BUDGET_MS = 4` (`4 ms` of folding becomes about one 60 Hz frame under the measured 4× throttle) and `FRAME_PACING_FRAME_BUDGET = 32` (a cheap burst never reaches the time budget, and clock resolution can hide it). The budget is spent from the last yield, so it always applies. The first version's idle-gap restart read one expensive frame's own processing as idle time, which silently disabled the time budget in the production regime (about 122 ms per long frame) and left only the 32-frame cap; the restart is deleted, and a slow one-frame-per-message stream now pays one macrotask hop per frame instead. `packages/api/gateway/src/client/journal-stream.ts` gains one marked import and one marked call at the top of `consume()` — after the previous frame was published, before the next `takeNext` — so frame order, terminal and error frames, cursor state, `options.failed`, and dispose quiescence are unchanged; the pacer never delays a pending abort past the current frame. Tests: new `packages/api/gateway/tests/stream-frame-pacing.client.spec.ts` (10 cases: 7 unit, 3 integration through `RemoteJournalStream`); the three integration cases fail with the injected call removed, and the accounting case fails against the deleted idle-gap restart. Verified: `npx vitest run packages/api/gateway` → 10 files / 379 tests green, `npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller` → 46 files / 960 tests green, `tsc -b packages/api/gateway/tsconfig.client.json` exit 0, oxlint clean on the three touched files, both new/changed files at 100% statements/branches/functions/lines in the coverage run, and `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` clean after the new row. Not verified: the main-thread win is not re-measured in a browser — only the scheduling behaviour is proven by the new spec.
- ZH: Web GUI 在 agent 流式输出时把主线程占满。用手机档客户端（412×915、DPR 2.625、`Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate(4)`、`long-animation-frame` 观测）在生产部署上实测：连续流式 228 秒产生 1352 个长动画帧、阻塞 164.6 秒——占墙钟 72%——最长单帧 2927 ms，而样式与布局合计仅 61 ms；函数级归因把工作定位在 api-gateway 客户端 bundle 的 WebSocket 消息任务里。机制：`packages/api/gateway/src/client/stream-client.ts` 把解码后的帧推入无界 `StreamInbox`，队列非空时其 `next()` 在已 resolve 的 promise 上兑现，于是消费链（`stream-client.ts` 的 async generator → `remote-stream.ts` → `RemoteJournalStream.consume()` → session-controller 折叠 → conversation assembler → store 发布）在一个 WebSocket 任务里通过微任务抽干整段突发，从不回到事件循环，渲染器轮不到。新增 fork 模块 `packages/api/gateway/src/client/fork/stream-frame-pacing.ts`：记录已消费帧数，一批预算用尽后等待一个与流的 `AbortSignal` 竞速的 `MessageChannel` 宏任务（无定时器回退——所有受支持 host 都提供 `MessageChannel`）——`FRAME_PACING_TIME_BUDGET_MS = 4`（在实测的 4× 降速下，4 ms 折叠工作约等于一个 60 Hz 帧）与 `FRAME_PACING_FRAME_BUDGET = 32`（廉价的突发永远到不了时间预算，且时钟分辨率可能掩盖它）；预算自上次让出起计，因此始终生效。首版的空闲间隔重启把「一帧自身的处理耗时」误读为空闲，在生产形态（长帧约 122 ms）下静默废掉时间预算、只剩 32 帧上限；该重启已删除，代价是「一条消息一帧」的慢流现在每帧付一次宏任务跳转。`packages/api/gateway/src/client/journal-stream.ts` 增加一处标记 import 与 `consume()` 顶部一处标记调用——位于上一帧发布之后、下一次 `takeNext` 之前——因此帧序、终止帧与错误帧、游标状态、`options.failed` 与 dispose 静默均不变，且让出绝不会把待处理的取消推迟到当前帧之后。测试：新增 `packages/api/gateway/tests/stream-frame-pacing.client.spec.ts`（10 用例：7 单元 + 3 经 `RemoteJournalStream` 的集成）；去掉注入调用后 3 条集成用例变红。已验证：`npx vitest run packages/api/gateway` → 10 文件 / 379 用例全绿，`npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller` → 46 文件 / 960 用例全绿，`tsc -b packages/api/gateway/tsconfig.client.json` 退出 0，oxlint 在三个改动文件上零告警，新增与改动文件在覆盖率运行中语句/分支/函数/行均为 100%，新增清单行后 `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` 零发现。未验证：主线程收益未在浏览器中重测——新规格只证明了调度行为。

### 2026-09-25 — Session list scoped by row selection end to end / Session 列表端到端按行范围收敛

- EN: The sidebar's first list carried every stored Session — 6,015 rows and 2.58 MB on the wire on the production deployment — because `session/list` had one unpaginated, unfiltered form and the client pulled it whole. The list is now scoped by row selection end to end. Wire: `SessionListRequest` carries `scope: 'listed' | 'all'` and `parentSessionId`; an absent scope selects the sidebar's listed rows (every row whose origin is not `subagent`), and the `list` Remote translates the request through `listScopeOf` into a `SessionListScope`. Query: `listSessions(signal, scope)` hands the whole selection to persistence and applies the shared `matchesListSelection` predicate to the merged live and persisted records, so no listing mixes selections. Storage: `SessionPersistenceListOptions` extends the new `SessionPersistenceListSelection`; the fork-owned SQLite backend selects listed and parented rows in SQL (`select-listed-sessions.sql`, `select-parented-sessions.sql`), so an excluded row is never decoded, while the JSONL backend reads every artifact header and applies the same predicate to headers and to this process's created-but-unmaterialized rows. Client: `SessionManager.refreshList` pulls `{ scope: 'listed' }`; `loadChildren()` reads an opened Session's direct children, walks up to eight ancestors so a depth-3 breadcrumb keeps its rows, memoizes one read per parent per Host generation, and treats a successful children read as that parent's membership authority — a retained row the Host no longer returns is forgotten. Retained subagent rows survive later listed pulls carrying the mutations replayed while the response was outstanding, keep their status row through a removal frame, and are re-read by `handleConnected` so a child that finished while the connection was down stops running. `UiSession` keeps a scoped-out child's status until its row leaves the store, and the conversation breadcrumb renders the ancestry from retained rows. Files: `packages/api/session-controller/src/index.ts` (the `list` Remote and `listScopeOf`), `packages/api/session-controller/src/{list.ts,types.ts}`, `packages/api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/{manager.ts,service.ts}`, `packages/session-query/session-query/src/{corpus.ts,index.ts,types.ts}`, `packages/session/session-persistence/src/index.ts`, `packages/session/session-persistence-jsonl/src/index.ts`, `packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/src/{index.ts,store.ts,sql.ts}` with `resources/sql/select-listed-sessions.sql` and `resources/sql/select-parented-sessions.sql`, `packages/client/ui-session/src/client/index.ts`, `packages/client/ui-conversation/src/client/skeleton/ConversationSession.tsx`, and the regenerated `packages/extensions/tool-cordis/src/api-catalog.ts` with `docs/subsystems/{session,session-query,persistence}.*` and the `session-query`/`session-persistence` README pairs. Verified: `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; `npx tsx scripts/gen-cordis-catalog.ts --check` reports 115 generated files/regions up to date; the focused suites (`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller packages/session-query packages/session/session-persistence packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite packages/session/session-persistence-jsonl packages/settings/settings packages/client/ui-session packages/client/ui-workspace packages/client/ui-conversation`) report 156 files, 3212 passed and 2 skipped. Not verified: the production row count and payload are not re-measured in a browser.
- ZH: 侧栏首屏此前会列出每一个已存储会话——生产部署上 6,015 行、线上载荷 2.58 MB——因为 `session/list` 只有一种不分页、不过滤的形态，客户端整包拉取。现在列表端到端按行范围收敛。线协议：`SessionListRequest` 携带 `scope: 'listed' | 'all'` 与 `parentSessionId`；缺省 scope 选中侧栏的 listed 行（origin 不是 `subagent` 的每一行），`list` Remote 经 `listScopeOf` 翻译为 `SessionListScope`。查询：`listSessions(signal, scope)` 把整个选择下传给持久化，并对 live 与 persisted 合并后的记录套用共享谓词 `matchesListSelection`，因此任何一次列举都不会混用选择。存储：`SessionPersistenceListOptions` 继承新的 `SessionPersistenceListSelection`；fork 自有的 SQLite 后端在 SQL 里选出 listed 行与指定父会话的行（`select-listed-sessions.sql`、`select-parented-sessions.sql`），被排除的行永不解码；JSONL 后端读每个 artifact 的 header，并对 header 与本进程已创建未落盘的行套用同一谓词。客户端：`SessionManager.refreshList` 拉取 `{ scope: 'listed' }`；`loadChildren()` 读取被打开会话的直属子会话，最多向上走八层祖先（深度 3 的面包屑因此保留每一行），按父会话在每代 Host 内只读一次，并把一次成功的子会话读取作为该父会话的成员权威——Host 不再返回的保留行会被遗忘。保留的 subagent 行在后续 listed 拉取中存活，并带上响应在途期间重放的变更；收到删除帧时保留其状态行；`handleConnected` 重新读取已知父会话，使连接断开期间结束的子会话不再显示为运行中。`UiSession` 在被范围排除的子会话行离开 store 之前保留其状态，会话面包屑从保留行派生祖先链。文件：`packages/api/session-controller/src/index.ts`（`list` Remote 与 `listScopeOf`）、`packages/api/session-controller/src/{list.ts,types.ts}`、`packages/api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/{manager.ts,service.ts}`、`packages/session-query/session-query/src/{corpus.ts,index.ts,types.ts}`、`packages/session/session-persistence/src/index.ts`、`packages/session/session-persistence-jsonl/src/index.ts`、`packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite/src/{index.ts,store.ts,sql.ts}` 连同 `resources/sql/select-listed-sessions.sql` 与 `resources/sql/select-parented-sessions.sql`、`packages/client/ui-session/src/client/index.ts`、`packages/client/ui-conversation/src/client/skeleton/ConversationSession.tsx`，以及重新生成的 `packages/extensions/tool-cordis/src/api-catalog.ts`、`docs/subsystems/{session,session-query,persistence}.*` 与 `session-query`／`session-persistence` 两对 README。已验证：`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；`npx tsx scripts/gen-cordis-catalog.ts --check` 报告 115 个生成文件／区域均为最新；聚焦测试（`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller packages/session-query packages/session/session-persistence packages/session/session-persistence-sqlite packages/session/session-persistence-jsonl packages/settings/settings packages/client/ui-session packages/client/ui-workspace packages/client/ui-conversation`）报告 156 个文件、3212 通过、2 跳过。未验证：生产环境的行数与载荷未在浏览器中重测。

### 2026-09-25 — Legacy settings import resilient to rejected sections / 旧版设置导入可容忍被拒绝的 section

- EN: `importLegacyDocument` renamed the legacy `settings.yaml` before writing a single section, so one section the running composition rejected consumed the document: the stranded production document's `reasoningEfforts` rejected the literal `false` key the YAML parser produces for a bare `off`, and every later section — the whole model/provider list — stayed behind in the renamed copy, never retried. The import now writes every section while the document still occupies its path, and only the accepted sections leave it: a rejected section stays at the legacy path for the next boot, leaving a retry document that holds exactly the sections still to import. A completed import records its section names in a `.imported-sections` sidecar before the rename, so a rename that keeps failing never repeats the writes, and the consumed document moves to the first free `.imported[.N]` name instead of overwriting an earlier copy; the retry document and the sidecar are written atomically through a sibling temp file. A section the schema rejects is retried once with its literal `false`/`true` keys rewritten to the level names the entry's config schema declares, walking nested maps and arrays, leaving a key the schema does not declare alone, and reporting a key whose declared level name the object already holds. Files: `packages/settings/settings/src/index.ts`, `packages/settings/settings/tests/legacy-import.spec.ts`, `packages/settings/settings/tests/configuration.spec.ts`, `packages/settings/settings/tests/configuration-fixture.ts`. Verified: `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; the focused suites above (the settings package included) report 156 files, 3212 passed and 2 skipped. Not verified: the production document is not re-imported against a live deployment — the specs cover the failing rename, the sidecar, the retry document, the `.imported[.N]` target, and the boolean-key normalization in a temporary home.
- ZH: `importLegacyDocument` 此前在写入任何 section 之前就把旧版 `settings.yaml` 改名，于是一个被运行中组装拒绝的 section 会消耗掉整份文档：滞留的生产文档里 `reasoningEfforts` 拒绝了 YAML 解析器为裸 `off` 生成的布尔键 `false`，其后每一个 section——整份模型／provider 列表——都留在改名后的副本里，永不再试。现在文档仍在其原路径时就写入每个 section，只有被接受的 section 离开它：被拒绝的 section 留在旧路径等待下次启动，重试文档恰好只保留仍需导入的 section。导入完成时在改名之前把 section 名单记进 `.imported-sections` 边车文件，因此改名持续失败也不会重复写入；被消费的文档移向第一个空闲的 `.imported[.N]` 名，而不是覆盖既有副本；重试文档与边车都经同目录临时文件加改名原子写入。被 schema 拒绝的 section 会再试一次，把字面 `false`／`true` 键改写为该条目配置 schema 声明的等级名——遍历嵌套 map 与数组，schema 未声明的键保持原样，并报告对象里同时持有拼写键与等级名的冲突。文件：`packages/settings/settings/src/index.ts`、`packages/settings/settings/tests/legacy-import.spec.ts`、`packages/settings/settings/tests/configuration.spec.ts`、`packages/settings/settings/tests/configuration-fixture.ts`。已验证：`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；上述聚焦测试（含 settings 包）报告 156 个文件、3212 通过、2 跳过。未验证：生产文档未在真实部署上重新导入——规格在临时 home 中覆盖改名失败、边车、重试文档、`.imported[.N]` 目标与布尔键归一化。

### 2026-09-25 — Session-list rebuilds skip frames that change nothing / 会话列表只为真变化重建

- EN: The client rebuilt its whole session list on every control frame of every attached Session. Measured on the deployment under a phone-class emulation (412×915, DPR 2.625, `Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate(4)`, `long-animation-frame` observation) with **no session open**: about 300 s of JavaScript inside a 310 s window, 496 long animation frames, 280.9 s blocking, while style and layout together totalled 13 ms; self time sat in `projectList`, `buildListSnapshot`, `flattenLineage`'s `walk`, `publishStatus`, `reconcileStatus`, `SessionTree`, `orderByRecency`, `mainSessionId` and `visibleSessionIds`. Chain: `sessionProjections.onChanged` broadcasts one frame per changed wire view per attached Session (`packages/api/session-controller/src/control.ts:22`), the client applied it and marked the list dirty unconditionally (`client/sessions/manager.ts` `handleControlFrame`), the microtask flush rebuilt the list snapshot and rows, `service.projectList` minted a fresh row per session and always called `list.set`, and every subscriber re-rendered; the fork's activity coalescer only ever covered `api-session/activity`. Two changes: `client/sessions/projection-store.ts` now reports whether an accepted frame's published value actually changed — an equal value advances the sequence watermark, keeps the cached value and its reference, and notifies nobody — and `manager.handleControlFrame` marks the list dirty only on a real change; `service.projectList` hands its rebuilt state to `reconcileListState` (in the already-registered fork module `client/sessions/fork/coalesced-refresh.ts`), which keeps the published row object wherever all eleven published `SessionSummary` fields are `Object.is`-equal and returns the published state object itself when ids, rows, order, phase and the projection snapshots are unchanged, so `list.set` is skipped. The same change moves `refreshProjections`' in-flight probe from the values identity to the per-key watermark: a silent landing no longer moves that identity, and a missing answer would otherwise have cleared a row an equal-valued frame had just confirmed. Files: `packages/api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/{projection-store.ts,manager.ts,service.ts,fork/coalesced-refresh.ts}` and the three matching specs. Verified: `packages/api/session-controller` 46 files / 985 tests green; the three named client packages 20 files / 431 tests green; `tsc -b` client and host exit 0; `verify-export-jsdoc` exit 0; oxlint clean on touched files except `manager.client.spec.ts`, whose three `no-unsafe-*` errors reproduce on a pristine stashed tree in that worktree (missing generated remote artifacts) and are clean in the built main checkout; the gated fork module and `projection-store.ts` at 100% statements/branches/functions/lines, with zero uncovered locations inside any added line of the coverage-exempt `manager.ts`/`service.ts`. Not verified: no browser measurement after the change; the exit conditions (a fresh `getListSnapshot()` after mutations, the `recordMutation` no-op contract, status/blank/activity transitions) rest on the existing suites plus the new row/order/projection cases rather than a live GUI run.
- ZH: 客户端此前对每个已附加会话的每个控制帧都重建整份会话列表。手机档仿真（412×915、DPR 2.625、`Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate(4)`、`long-animation-frame` 观测）下**不打开任何会话**实测：310 秒窗口内约 300 秒 JavaScript、496 个长动画帧、阻塞 280.9 秒，而样式与布局合计仅 13 ms；自时间落在 `projectList`、`buildListSnapshot`、`flattenLineage` 的 `walk`、`publishStatus`、`reconcileStatus`、`SessionTree`、`orderByRecency`、`mainSessionId`、`visibleSessionIds`。链路：`sessionProjections.onChanged` 为每个已附加会话的每个变更线上视图广播一帧（`packages/api/session-controller/src/control.ts:22`），客户端无条件应用并标脏列表（`client/sessions/manager.ts` 的 `handleControlFrame`），微任务 flush 重建列表快照与行，`service.projectList` 为每个会话新建行并总是 `list.set`，随后所有订阅者重渲染；fork 的活动合并器只覆盖过 `api-session/activity`。两处改动：`client/sessions/projection-store.ts` 现在报告被接受的帧其已发布值是否真的变了——值相等时只推进序号水位、保留缓存值及其引用、不通知任何人——`manager.handleControlFrame` 仅在真变化时标脏；`service.projectList` 把重建结果交给已登记的 fork 模块 `client/sessions/fork/coalesced-refresh.ts` 里的 `reconcileListState`，后者在十一个已发布 `SessionSummary` 字段全部 `Object.is` 相等时沿用已发布的行对象，并在 ids、行、顺序、phase 与投影快照都未变时返回已发布的状态对象本身，从而跳过 `list.set`。同一改动把 `refreshProjections` 的在途探测从 values 身份改为按键水位：静默落地不再移动该身份，否则缺失的应答会把一个刚被等值帧确认过的行清掉。文件：`packages/api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/{projection-store.ts,manager.ts,service.ts,fork/coalesced-refresh.ts}` 及三个对应规格。已验证：`packages/api/session-controller` 46 文件 / 985 用例全绿；三个具名客户端包 20 文件 / 431 用例全绿；`tsc -b` 客户端与宿主面退出 0；`verify-export-jsdoc` 退出 0；oxlint 在改动文件上干净，唯 `manager.client.spec.ts` 的三条 `no-unsafe-*` 在该 worktree 的干净 stash 树上同样复现（缺生成的 remote 产物），而在已构建的主检出上干净；受门禁的 fork 模块与 `projection-store.ts` 语句/分支/函数/行均为 100%，覆盖率豁免的 `manager.ts`/`service.ts` 中新增行内零未覆盖点。未验证：改动后未做浏览器测量；退出条件（写后 `getListSnapshot()` 新鲜、`recordMutation` 空转契约、状态/空白/活动迁移）依赖既有套件加新增的行/顺序/投影用例，而非真机 GUI 运行。

### 2026-09-25 — The transcript mounts a bounded window around the reader / 转写面只挂载读者周围的窗口

- EN: The chat transcript mounted every loaded history row, so per-frame DOM and render work grew with the conversation (phone-class measurement: 228 s of streaming produced 1352 long animation frames and 164.6 s of blocking, 72% of wall time, while style and layout totalled 61 ms; opening a 231-row session cost 6.87 s of script inside an 8.7 s window). New fork module `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts` plans and holds a window over the resident order: `MOUNTED_ROW_LIMIT = 50` resident order keys, `REVEAL_ROW_STEP = 25` per reveal, a group mounts only its in-window members (so the bound counts rows, not entries), and a resident tail stub stays mounted while a Turn streams so live rows, the opening echo and the streaming assertions keep working. The reader's saved anchor is resolved to a resident row — plain node keys, `["key","part"]` pairs, and `group:<json>` through its first embedded member key — and an anchor the window cannot place (`call:<id>`) leaves the session's scroll memory intact instead of being erased by a tail landing; the landing to the bottom now waits for tail ownership to be handed back rather than for the window to reach the tail slice, a reveal with no reader row pages instead of unmounting the newest rows, and a jump re-arms the fork's reflow hold from its landing. Five marked injections carry it: `ChatView.tsx` (window plan, rail/Load-earlier/Back-to-bottom wrappers, the affordance gate, the opening-echo splice, `mountSignature` reflow), `ChatNodeSeat.tsx` (optional `mountedKeys`), `ChatGroupSeat.tsx` (member filter and empty guard), `use-chat-scroll.ts` (anchor resolution at session open, `mountSignature` reflow, `holdReader`), `use-chat-reading.ts` (`hold`, `setAnchorResolved`, reflow re-arm on a jump landing). The browser lanes were re-expressed around reachability and the bound (`chat-scroll-contract`, `chat-long-interactions`, `stats-paged-history`, `preview-boot`, `complex-history.perf.ts`, plus `benchmarks/long-session-browser/long-session.bench.ts` driven off the window head), and the shared `loadEarlierUntil` helper no longer mistakes the transient loading label for the end of history. Files: the new module, the five injected files, six specs, four browser lanes plus the perf lane and the long-session bench. Verified: `npx vitest run packages/client/ui-chat` 42 files / 621 tests green; the fork module at 100% statements/branches/functions/lines; oxlint clean on every touched file; the pre-existing `tsc` error set unchanged; `verify-fork-surface` green. Known residual: a frozen resolution is cached per entries identity plus head and revalidated against the 50-key slice, so an order rewrite that moves a resident key above the window while that slice stays identical can serve a resolution stored from a complete walk (probe: entries `[group g1, node x]`, order `[a0..a9, w0..w49, x]` stores the group, then `[a0..a7, x, a9, w0..w49]` returns it while a fresh identity yields none); closing it needs a bounded re-pairing walk or an O(order) cache key, both of which change the per-commit cost this change exists to bound. Not verified here: the browser lanes and the perf lane were not run in this worktree (the lead runs them against the built artifacts), so the gesture-level behaviour and the mount bound in a real browser rest on that run.
- ZH: 转写面此前挂载全部已加载历史行，每帧的 DOM 与渲染工作因此随会话增长（手机档实测：连续流式 228 秒产生 1352 个长动画帧、阻塞 164.6 秒，占墙钟 72%，而样式与布局合计仅 61 ms；打开 231 行会话在 8.7 秒窗口里花掉 6.87 秒脚本时间）。新增 fork 模块 `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts` 在常驻顺序之上规划并持有窗口：`MOUNTED_ROW_LIMIT = 50` 个常驻 order key、每次揭示上移 `REVEAL_ROW_STEP = 25`；分组只挂载落在窗口内的成员（因此上界数的是行而不是 entry），轮次流式期间保留常驻尾部短桩，使实时行、开场回显与流式断言继续成立。读者保存的锚点会被解析到常驻行——普通节点 key、`["key","part"]` 对，以及经首个内嵌成员 key 解析的 `group:<json>`——无法放置的锚点（`call:<id>`）保留会话滚动记忆，而不是被回到底部的落地抹掉；回到底部现在等待尾部归属被交还，而不是等窗口抵达尾部切片；没有读者行时「加载更早」改为翻页而不是卸载最新若干行；跳转落地后重新布防 fork 的回流保持。五处标记注入承载它：`ChatView.tsx`（窗口规划、轨道/加载更早/回到底部包装、控件门控、开场回显拼接、`mountSignature` 回流）、`ChatNodeSeat.tsx`（可选 `mountedKeys`）、`ChatGroupSeat.tsx`（成员过滤与空守卫）、`use-chat-scroll.ts`（会话打开时解析锚点、`mountSignature` 回流、`holdReader`）、`use-chat-reading.ts`（`hold`、`setAnchorResolved`、跳转落地重新布防回流）。浏览器泳道按可达性与上界重写（`chat-scroll-contract`、`chat-long-interactions`、`stats-paged-history`、`preview-boot`、`complex-history.perf.ts`，以及按窗口头驱动的 `benchmarks/long-session-browser/long-session.bench.ts`），共享的 `loadEarlierUntil` 辅助函数不再把瞬时 loading 标签当作历史尽头。文件：新模块、五个被注入文件、六个规格、四条浏览器泳道加性能泳道与长会话 bench。已验证：`npx vitest run packages/client/ui-chat` 42 文件 / 621 用例全绿；该 fork 模块语句/分支/函数/行 100%；所有改动文件 oxlint 干净；既有 `tsc` 错误集不变；`verify-fork-surface` 绿。已知残留：冻结窗口的解析结果按 entries 身份加 head 缓存、只对该 50 key 切片复验，因此若顺序重写把某个常驻 key 移到窗口之上而该切片逐字节不变，就可能复用一次完整游走存下的解析结果（探针：entries `[group g1, node x]`，顺序 `[a0..a9, w0..w49, x]` 存下该分组，随后 `[a0..a7, x, a9, w0..w49]` 复用它，而新身份解析为空）；要闭合它需要带上界的重配对游走或 O(order) 缓存键，两者都会改变本次改动正是要约束的每提交成本。本处未验证：浏览器泳道与性能泳道未在该 worktree 运行（由 lead 针对构建产物运行），因此真实浏览器里的手势级行为与挂载上界以那次运行为准。

### 2026-09-26 — Session-list rebuilds gate by projection key; children reads merge in one pass / 会话列表按键门控重建；子会话读取单趟合并

- EN: The client's list-rebuild gate now knows which projection keys a list consumer can read, and a children read merges its rows in one pass. `client/sessions/fork/coalesced-refresh.ts` gains `LIST_IRRELEVANT_PROJECTION_KEYS` — a deny list holding `sessionStats`, `contextPressure`, `contextBreakdown`, whose only readers in this repository are session-scoped `useProjection` bindings — and `affectsSessionList(key)`; an unlisted key still rebuilds, so a consumer of a key nobody has examined keeps working, and `tokenUsage` stays out of the set because the subagent lineage reads it from the row map. `projection-store.ts`'s `subscribeAny` delivers the changed key per change (the manager's own notifier still batches), the manager's store listener marks the list dirty only when `affectsSessionList(key)` is true and narrows the blank correction to `sessionListMetadata` changes, and `handleControlFrame` no longer self-marks — the store channel owns the decision. `readChildren` hands its whole response to `mergeChildren`, which upserts every row through `mergeSummaryRows` (one O(rows+children) pass, one mutation-log write, one dirty mark, one `updateParentAvailability`) instead of one full-array rewrite per child, keeping the retained-child, engagement, blank, and projection-block effects through the shared `applyAddedSession`. Measured (synthetic bench on a 2,965-row store): 200 frames carrying only `sessionStats` (20 sessions × 10 changed values) went from 22.1 ms / 10 rebuilds to 0.6 ms / 0 rebuilds; one 2,244-children read went from 89.0 ms to 8.9 ms (production reported 99 ms). Files: `packages/api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/{fork/coalesced-refresh.ts,manager.ts,projection-store.ts}` and the two matching specs. Verified at the merged tip: `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller packages/session-query packages/client/ui-workspace packages/client/ui-session` 77 files / 1729 passed. Not verified: no browser re-measurement — the win rests on the synthetic bench and the existing suites.
- ZH: 客户端的列表重建闸门现在知道哪些投影键是列表面消费者会读的，且一次子会话读取把全部行单趟合并。`client/sessions/fork/coalesced-refresh.ts` 新增 `LIST_IRRELEVANT_PROJECTION_KEYS`——一个 deny list，持有 `sessionStats`、`contextPressure`、`contextBreakdown`（本仓库内其唯一读者是会话级 `useProjection` 绑定）——与 `affectsSessionList(key)`；未列出的键仍然重建，因此尚未被审视过的键的消费者不受影响，而 `tokenUsage` 不进豁免集，因为 subagent lineage 从行 map 里读它。`projection-store.ts` 的 `subscribeAny` 改为按变更逐个投递变更键（manager 自己的 notifier 仍批处理），manager 的 store 监听器只在 `affectsSessionList(key)` 为真时标脏列表、并把 blank 校正收窄到 `sessionListMetadata` 变更，`handleControlFrame` 不再自行标脏——决策归 store 通道。`readChildren` 把整个响应交给 `mergeChildren`，经 `mergeSummaryRows` 一次 O(rows+children) 合并（一次 mutation log 写入、一次标脏、一次 `updateParentAvailability`）替代每个子行一次全数组重写，保留行、engagement、blank 与 projection-block 副作用经共享的 `applyAddedSession` 保持不变。实测（2,965 行 store 上的合成基准）：200 个只带 `sessionStats` 的帧（20 会话 × 10 次变化值）从 22.1 ms / 10 次重建降到 0.6 ms / 0 次重建；一次 2,244 子行读取从 89.0 ms 降到 8.9 ms（生产上报 99 ms）。文件：`packages/api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/{fork/coalesced-refresh.ts,manager.ts,projection-store.ts}` 及两个对应规格。合并 tip 上已验证：`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller packages/session-query packages/client/ui-workspace packages/client/ui-session` 77 文件 / 1729 用例通过。未验证：未做浏览器重测——收益依据是合成基准与既有套件。

### 2026-09-26 — session/list rows ship only list-plane projections / session/list 行只携带列表面投影

- EN: The default `session/list` response spent 92.4% of its bytes on projections whose consumers all sit on the Session-open path, because `hintsOf` shipped every registered wire key on every row. The list builder now projects each block down to the keys list surfaces read — `sessionListMetadata`, `title`, `subagentCatalog`, `schedule`, `subagentTiming`, `tokenUsage`, `subagent`, `agentPreset`, `agentTeam` — so heavy open-plane keys (`turnOutline`, `inbox`, `todos`, `imageLimits`, `modelSelection`, and the rest) no longer cross the wire for a listing, and a block carrying none of the kept keys omits its `projections` entirely. The trim sits in the list builder (`list.ts` `hintsOf`) because it is the one chokepoint every list row and `api-session/added` frame passes through, cold and live alike, so no future caller can bypass it; the Session-open path (follow snapshot, control baseline, `session/projections` RPC) keeps the complete projection set, and the persistence/cache layers stay full. The kept set is the audited reader set — each key names its list-plane consumer (the workspace tree, the subagent lineage, the sidebar chat tab, the preset chip, the Team panel), and `tokenUsage`/`subagentTiming` stay because cold children's lineage metrics read them from the row map. `types.ts`'s `SessionProjectionHints` contract JSDoc now states the list-plane selection. Measured: the largest single row saves ≥37,786 B (the whole `turnOutline` value disappears); each row also drops the repeated `imageLimits` constant (~200 B × 223 rows ≈ 44 KB) and the other open-plane keys wherever present. Files: `packages/api/session-controller/src/{list.ts,types.ts}` and the two specs. Verified at the merged tip: `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller packages/session-query packages/client/ui-workspace packages/client/ui-session` 77 files / 1729 passed; `npx tsx scripts/gen-cordis-catalog.ts --check` 115 up to date. Not verified: the exact new total byte count (needs the deployment store) and no browser sidebar/lineage/Team rendering check — the reader set is a static audit plus the contract tests.
- ZH: 默认 `session/list` 响应把 92.4% 的字节花在消费者全部位于 Session-open 路径的投影上，因为 `hintsOf` 把每个已注册 wire 键都放进每一行。列表构建器现在把每个 block 投影到列表面会读的键——`sessionListMetadata`、`title`、`subagentCatalog`、`schedule`、`subagentTiming`、`tokenUsage`、`subagent`、`agentPreset`、`agentTeam`——于是重型 open-plane 键（`turnOutline`、`inbox`、`todos`、`imageLimits`、`modelSelection` 及其余）不再为一次列举过线，且一个不携带任何保留键的 block 会整体省略其 `projections`。裁剪放在列表构建器（`list.ts` 的 `hintsOf`），因为它是每个列表行与 `api-session/added` 帧都要经过的唯一收窄点，冷热皆然，未来调用方无法绕过；Session-open 路径（follow 快照、control baseline、`session/projections` RPC）保持全量投影，持久化/缓存层保持全量。保留集是审计过的读者集——每个键都指名其列表面消费者（workspace tree、subagent lineage、sidebar chat 标签、preset 芯片、Team 面板），`tokenUsage`/`subagentTiming` 保留是因为冷子会话的 lineage 指标从行 map 读它们。`types.ts` 的 `SessionProjectionHints` 契约 JSDoc 现在写明列表面选择。实测：最大单行省 ≥37,786 B（整个 `turnOutline` 值消失）；每行还省去重复的 `imageLimits` 常量（约 200 B × 223 行 ≈ 44 KB）及其他 open-plane 键（凡出现处）。文件：`packages/api/session-controller/src/{list.ts,types.ts}` 及两个规格。合并 tip 上已验证：`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller packages/session-query packages/client/ui-workspace packages/client/ui-session` 77 文件 / 1729 用例通过；`npx tsx scripts/gen-cordis-catalog.ts --check` 115 项最新。未验证：精确的新总字节数（需要部署库）与浏览器侧 sidebar/lineage/Team 渲染检查——读者集是静态审计加契约测试。

### 2026-09-26 — The transcript mounts the head Turn's fold control with the rows it hides / 转写面把头部轮次的折叠控制与其隐藏的行一起挂载

- EN: A completed Turn whose last step ends on a Tool call has no finalized answer, so the Turn fold's only visible row is its `turn-process` control; when that Turn's process run outran `MOUNTED_ROW_LIMIT = 50`, the control sat above the mounted slice and the Chat transcript rendered zero visible rows — hidden member rows plus chrome, silently, with no reveal path (a reader holding no saved row cannot move the window head, and paging only prepends older history). The window planner now mounts the head Turn's control row beside the slice it governs, restoring the invariant the fold assumes: the control is mounted with the rows it hides. `MountWindowInput` carries the ascending resident `controls` indices of the Turn-process keys, `headControl` binary-searches the last one at or above the window head, and `planMountedWindow` adds that one key — bounded at one extra row, and only when the control is resident above the head. Files: `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts`, the `controls` injection in `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/ChatView.tsx`, and the two `ui-chat` specs. Recorded on the branch (second `dsh web` instance against a copy of the production session store): the reported Session's Chat pane went from 0 mounted controls and 94 characters of transcript to the Turn control and its process rows on expand (76/76 rows visible, 1,946 characters).
- ZH: 末步停在工具调用、没有最终回答的已完成轮次，其折叠后唯一可见的行就是 `turn-process` 控制行；当该轮次的过程行数超过 `MOUNTED_ROW_LIMIT = 50` 时，控制行落在已挂载切片之上，Chat 转写面因此渲染零个可见行——只有隐藏的成员行与外壳，静默发生且没有揭示路径（没有已保存行的读者无法移动窗口头部，而分页只会前插更早的历史）。窗口规划器现在把头部轮次的控制行与它所管辖的切片一起挂载，恢复了折叠所假设的不变量：控制行与其隐藏的行同处挂载。`MountWindowInput` 携带 Turn-process 键的升序常驻 `controls` 索引，`headControl` 二分查找位于窗口头部或其之上的最后一个控制行，`planMountedWindow` 只增加这一个键——上限为多一行，且仅当该控制行常驻于头部之上时。文件：`packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts`、`packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/ChatView.tsx` 中的 `controls` 注入，以及 `ui-chat` 的两个规格。分支上记录（第二个 `dsh web` 实例，针对生产会话库的副本）：所报告 Session 的 Chat 面板从 0 个已挂载控制行、94 字符转写文本，变为出现轮次控制行、展开后出现其过程行（76/76 行可见，1946 字符）。

### 2026-09-25 — Reader gestures step the mounted window, and a rail jump lands again / 读者手势推进挂载窗口，轨道跳转重新落地

- EN: The bounded transcript window left back-scrolling dependent on the explicit earlier-rows control: with the reader pinned at the mounted head, a wheel or touch gesture scrolls nothing, so no scroll event fires and the window never steps — the reader hits an invisible wall every 50 resident rows. Three changes. First, `use-chat-viewport.ts` now delivers the reader's own gesture as its own event (`intent`, marked injection), because a gesture against the mounted head produces no scroll; `use-chat-scroll.ts` reads the scroll geometry, settles the pending reading sample first (a pending sample makes the fork's `reflow()` a no-op, and the settle arms the hold on the row the reader has reached), and the fork module decides with `willRevealAtHead`/`revealAtHead`, stepping the head one `REVEAL_ROW_STEP` (25 rows) while keeping the head row the reader reached, and the saved row while the step can still move with it. No timer paces the steps: the reveal makes the scrollport absorb the rows it added, which moves the reader more than a viewport from the new head, so the next step waits for the next viewport of reader travel. Second, a rail jump to a tail-side turn was a silent no-op from a reader pinned at the mounted head: `requested` used `{kind:'tail'}` both for "the reader owns the tail window" and "the reader owns no window of their own", and the adoption memo derived the effective window from the reader's row, so a jump that normalized to the live tail stored the value the state already held, the window signature never moved, and the landing scroll was never issued. `requested` is now `MountWindow | null`, with `null` meaning the reader owns no window; `hold` stores the resolved window as an explicit request and `release()`/tail ownership set `null`. Third, the lanes were re-expressed onto the product's own reachability: `chat-long-interactions` reaches the last turn's tool rows and the branch rows through the outline rail instead of a wheel search that only worked while every loaded row was mounted (its `wheelUntilMounted` helper is deleted; the `Back to bottom` click the lane first needed before that jump was removed again once the landing fix was verified in the browser), and the `stats-paged-history` aria golden was refreshed for the intentional end state (every rail mark reads `Jump to turn N` once all history is loaded). Files: `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts`, `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/{ChatView.tsx,use-chat-scroll.ts,use-chat-viewport.ts}`, `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/{mounted-window.client.spec.ts,chat-view.client.spec.tsx}`, `apps/web/tests/chat-long-interactions.e2e.ts`, `apps/web/tests/expected/stats-paged-history/ui.expected.md`. Verified: `pnpm --config.verify-deps-before-run=false exec vitest run packages/client/ui-chat` 42 files / 633 tests green, with the rail-jump landing case red before the fix (`expected null not to be null`) and green after; oxlint clean on every touched file; `tsc -p packages/client/ui-chat/tsconfig.client.json --noEmit` exit 0; in a real browser under `DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay`, `chat-long-interactions` (1 test), `chat-scroll-contract` (9 tests), `stats-paged-history` (3 tests) and `preview-boot` (1 test) all pass on the rebuilt artifacts, and the long-interactions landing passes with the `Back to bottom` workaround removed, which is what verifies the jump fix rather than the workaround. Not verified: `complex-history.perf.ts` cannot run at all on current master — its seed generator produces a log the V4 validation landed by another change rejects (`session snapshot line 1: system/message requires a protected first surface head`, from `session-format-v3-to-v4`'s `Relationships.foldSurface`), so the bounded-window perf assertions carry no measurement; that failure reproduces before any UI work and the two validation commits predate this change. The head-step mechanism itself was measured in the browser only as the window holding 51 mounted rows while the reader traverses.
- ZH: 有界转写窗口让"往回滚"依赖显式的「加载更早」控件：读者顶在挂载窗口头部时，滚轮或触摸手势滚不动任何东西，因此不会发出 scroll 事件、窗口永不推进——读者每 50 个常驻行就撞上一堵看不见的墙。三处改动。其一，`use-chat-viewport.ts` 现在把读者自己的手势作为独立事件投递（`intent`，标记注入），因为顶在挂载头部的手势不产生滚动；`use-chat-scroll.ts` 读取滚动几何，先结算在途的阅读采样（在途采样会让 fork 的 `reflow()` 变成空操作，结算则把重排保持武装在读者已到达的那一行），再由 fork 模块用 `willRevealAtHead`/`revealAtHead` 决策：把头推进一个 `REVEAL_ROW_STEP`（25 行），同时保住读者到达的头部行，以及在该步仍能与其一同下移时的已保存行。没有计时器节流：一次揭示让滚动口吸收它新增的行，使读者距新头部超过一个视口，于是下一步要等下一个视口的读者位移。其二，从顶在挂载头部的读者状态出发，向尾部一侧轮次的轨道跳转此前是静默空操作：`requested` 用 `{kind:'tail'}` 同时表示"读者拥有尾部窗口"与"读者没有自己的窗口"，采纳 memo 又从读者行推导生效窗口，于是一次归一化为尾部窗口的跳转存下的正是状态里已有的值，窗口签名不变，落地滚动从未发出。现在 `requested` 为 `MountWindow | null`，`null` 表示读者没有自己的窗口；`hold` 把解析后的窗口存为显式请求，`release()` 与尾部归属置 `null`。其三，泳道改用产品自身的可达路径：`chat-long-interactions` 通过大纲轨道抵达末轮的工具行与分支行，而不再用只在"全部已加载行都挂载"时才成立的滚轮搜索（其 `wheelUntilMounted` 辅助函数已删除；该跳转先前需要的「回到底部」点击在浏览器中验证落地修复后也被移除），`stats-paged-history` 的 aria 金样按有意变化刷新（全部历史加载完后每个轨道标记读作 `Jump to turn N`）。文件：`packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts`、`packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/{ChatView.tsx,use-chat-scroll.ts,use-chat-viewport.ts}`、`packages/client/ui-chat/tests/{mounted-window.client.spec.ts,chat-view.client.spec.tsx}`、`apps/web/tests/chat-long-interactions.e2e.ts`、`apps/web/tests/expected/stats-paged-history/ui.expected.md`。已验证：`pnpm --config.verify-deps-before-run=false exec vitest run packages/client/ui-chat` 42 文件 / 633 用例全绿，其中轨道跳转落地用例在修复前为红（`expected null not to be null`）、修复后为绿；所有改动文件 oxlint 干净；`tsc -p packages/client/ui-chat/tsconfig.client.json --noEmit` 退出 0；`DSH_SNAPSHOT=replay` 真机浏览器下 `chat-long-interactions`（1 用例）、`chat-scroll-contract`（9 用例）、`stats-paged-history`（3 用例）、`preview-boot`（1 用例）在重新构建的产物上全部通过，且 long-interactions 在移除「回到底部」兜底后仍通过——这才是对落地修复的验证而不是对兜底的验证。未验证：`complex-history.perf.ts` 在当前 master 上根本无法运行——它的种子生成器产出的日志被另一处改动落地的 V4 校验拒绝（`session snapshot line 1: system/message requires a protected first surface head`，来自 `session-format-v3-to-v4` 的 `Relationships.foldSurface`），因此有界窗口的性能断言没有任何测量；该失败在任何 UI 工作之前即复现，且那两个校验提交早于本次改动。头部推进机制本身在浏览器中的测量仅为：读者穿行期间挂载窗口保持 51 行。

### 2026-09-26 — History pages cut at a Turn start, not at the message budget / 历史页在轮次开头切分，而非消息预算处

- EN: History paging cut a page at the max-th append-origin message (widened to that message's origin group head), so a request whose message floor crossed a long Turn opened the page at an `assistant/message` with no `turn/start` above it and the transcript rendered that Turn without its prompt, its process control, and every earlier step — reproduced on both `session/page` and the `follow` opening snapshot with the production request shapes (`maxMessages: 500`, `turnWindow: { minMessages: 8, minTurns: 2 }`). `paginate` in `packages/api/session-controller/src/history.ts` now treats its own stop as an anchor and walks on to the `turn/start` owning it, so every page head is a Turn start, or the log head when the walk reaches it before any Turn start (`exhausted`); `maxMessages` becomes a floor rather than a hard cap, and requests without `turnWindow` are Turn-aligned by the same rule. The indexed fast path reads `exhausted` as an unproven window, retries at the deep margin, and otherwise falls back to the whole-log observation read; that fallback's measured cost is recorded at `PAGE_CUT_DEEP_MARGIN` in `src/fork/page-boundary.ts` — a single Turn carrying more events than the deep margin can never prove its cut from either window, so every request for that Session pays a whole-log read (measured: one 6,000-message Turn in a 6,011-event log; the page served afterward matched the observation path's). The branch's own regression tests drive the production request shapes through `session/page`, the `follow` opening, and the indexed/observation differential, and fail on the previous rule with an `assistant/message` head. Files: `packages/api/session-controller/src/{history.ts,types.ts,index.ts,fork/page-boundary.ts,fork/open-window.ts}`, the five host specs, the README pair with its re-recorded pairing file, the regenerated `docs/subsystems/session.{md,zh.md}` + `session.i18n.yaml` + `packages/extensions/tool-cordis/src/api-catalog.ts`, and `apps/web/tests/seeded-history.e2e.ts`, whose opening assertions are rewritten for the Turn-aligned single-Turn page (the file stays excluded from the web lane by the disabled `permission` row; multi-Turn paging is covered by `stats-paged-history.e2e.ts`). Verified: `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller` 47 files / 1004 passed; `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; oxlint clean on the changed files; `npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` OK (5 baselined findings, none new); `doc-quick` 19 passed / 1 failed, the failure the pre-existing `docs/config-catalog.md` pairing record. Not verified: the browser lane (the file is excluded), real SQLite persistence, and real-model e2e.
- ZH: 历史分页在第 max 条 append-origin 消息处切页（并展宽到该消息的来源组头），因此消息下限落在长轮次内部的请求会让页首成为上方没有 `turn/start` 的 `assistant/message`，转写面渲染出的该轮次缺少提示词、自身的过程控制行与更早的每个步骤——`session/page` 与 `follow` 首屏均以生产请求形态（`maxMessages: 500`、`turnWindow: { minMessages: 8, minTurns: 2 }`）复现。`packages/api/session-controller/src/history.ts` 的 `paginate` 现在把自身停止点当作锚点，继续回溯到拥有它的 `turn/start`，因此每个页首要么是轮次开头，要么（回溯在任何轮次开头之前先到日志开头时）是日志开头（`exhausted`）；`maxMessages` 由硬上限变为下限，未带 `turnWindow` 的请求同样按此规则对齐。索引快路径把 `exhausted` 读作未证明窗口，先在深余量上重试，否则回退整日志观测读；该回退的实测代价记录在 `src/fork/page-boundary.ts` 的 `PAGE_CUT_DEEP_MARGIN` 处——事件数超过深余量的单个轮次在两个窗口里都证明不了切点，于是该会话的每次请求都付一次整日志读取（实测：6,011 事件日志里一个 6,000 消息的轮次；随后端出的页与观测路径一致）。本分支自带的回归测试用生产请求形态穿过 `session/page`、`follow` 首屏与索引/观测差分，并在旧规则下以 `assistant/message` 页首失败。文件：`packages/api/session-controller/src/{history.ts,types.ts,index.ts,fork/page-boundary.ts,fork/open-window.ts}`、五个 host 规格、README 对及其重新记录的配对文件、重新生成的 `docs/subsystems/session.{md,zh.md}` + `session.i18n.yaml` + `packages/extensions/tool-cordis/src/api-catalog.ts`，以及 `apps/web/tests/seeded-history.e2e.ts`——其首屏断言按 Turn 对齐的单轮次页重写（该文件仍因 `permission` 行被禁用而排除在 web 泳道之外；多轮次分页由 `stats-paged-history.e2e.ts` 覆盖）。已验证：`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller` 47 文件 / 1004 通过；`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；oxlint 在改动文件上干净；`npx tsx scripts/verify-fork-surface.ts` 通过（5 条基线内发现，无新增）；`doc-quick` 19 通过 / 1 失败，失败项为既存的 `docs/config-catalog.md` 配对记录。未验证：浏览器泳道（该文件被排除）、真实 SQLite 持久化、真实模型 e2e。

### 2026-09-26 — The Team panel's `modelSelection` returns to the list-plane whitelist / Team 面板的 `modelSelection` 回到列表面白名单

- EN: The list-plane projection whitelist that landed with the 92.4%-byte trim dropped `modelSelection` from `session/list` rows, but the client's own list-rebuild gate already classifies that key as list-relevant (`client/sessions/fork/coalesced-refresh.ts` names it beside `agentTeam`), and the Team panel's member rows read it off the list plane (`packages/experimental/client-ui-agent-team/src/client/TeamAction.tsx`: `projectionsBySession[member.id]?.values.modelSelection?.next?.model`), so the member model label could never render while the manager kept rebuilding for every change of a value no row shipped. The whitelist is the wrong side: a Team member's child is usually not an opened Session, so the list plane is that value's only source, and the key costs tens of bytes per row against the trim's megabyte-scale savings. `modelSelection` is restored to `LIST_PROJECTION_KEYS`, `SessionProjectionHints`' contract JSDoc names it, and `tests/session-list-projection-selection.host.spec.ts` gains a case that derives the reader key set from this repository's own list-plane accessors (`projectionValues.<key>` and `projectionsBySession[...]?.values.<key>`, test files excluded) and asserts every such key crosses the wire, so dropping a consumer key again fails it; the case's own guards (walk reach, the Team reader file, `modelSelection`) fail a broken scan instead of passing an empty set. Verified: `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller` 47 files / 1004 passed, and with the key removed from the whitelist the new case fails with `expected [ 'agentPreset', 'agentTeam', …(7) ] to deeply equal ArrayContaining{…}`; `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; oxlint clean on the three changed TypeScript files; `verify-fork-surface` OK. Not verified: the browser Team panel is not re-measured — the reader set is a static derivation plus the contract tests.
- ZH: 与 92.4% 字节裁剪一同落地的列表面投影白名单把 `modelSelection` 从 `session/list` 行里丢掉了，但客户端自己的列表重建闸门早已把该键归为列表相关（`client/sessions/fork/coalesced-refresh.ts` 把它与 `agentTeam` 并列），Team 面板的成员行也从列表面读它（`packages/experimental/client-ui-agent-team/src/client/TeamAction.tsx`：`projectionsBySession[member.id]?.values.modelSelection?.next?.model`），于是成员模型标签永远无法渲染，而 manager 却会为一个从未过线的值的每次变化继续重建列表。错的是白名单这一侧：Team 成员的子会话通常不是已打开会话，列表面是该值唯一的来源，而该键每行只值几十字节，抵不过裁剪的百万字节级节省。`modelSelection` 恢复到 `LIST_PROJECTION_KEYS`，`SessionProjectionHints` 契约 JSDoc 列入该键，`tests/session-list-projection-selection.host.spec.ts` 新增一例：从本仓库自身的列表面访问器（`projectionValues.<key>` 与 `projectionsBySession[...]?.values.<key>`，排除测试文件）派生读者键集，并断言每个这样的键都过线，因此再次丢掉某个消费者键即失败；该例自身的守卫（遍历到达量、Team 读者文件、`modelSelection` 的存在）在扫描损坏时失败，而不是空集通过。已验证：`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/api/session-controller` 47 文件 / 1004 通过，且把该键从白名单移除后新用例失败，报 `expected [ 'agentPreset', 'agentTeam', …(7) ] to deeply equal ArrayContaining{…}`；`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；oxlint 在三个改动的 TypeScript 文件上干净；`verify-fork-surface` 通过。未验证：未重测浏览器中的 Team 面板——读者集是静态派生加契约测试。

### 2026-09-26 — The fold-anchor invariant gate and the stub Turn control / 折叠锚点不变量门禁与短桩轮次控制行

- EN: The mounted transcript window's completed-Turn fold hides a closed Turn's member rows behind its `turn-process` control, so a plan that mounts those rows without that control renders none of them. The shipped head-control mount covered the window slice, but the frozen window's stub is a second region of the plan: the stub holds the newest 50 keys, and a folded Turn longer than that slice crosses the stub's start, so its member rows mount while the control that sits above the slice does not — an unreachable plan with no reveal path (a reader holding no saved row cannot move the head, and paging only prepends older history). `planMountedWindow` now also mounts the tail slice's own Turn control while the stub is mounted; the conditional extra bound is two keys (the head control and the stub control), still O(1). The same change states the invariant as a gate: the new `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/mounted-window-anchor-invariant.client.spec.ts` (19 cases) fixes the property — every hidden row a plan can render has its Turn control mounted in that plan — and sweeps order lengths `[0,1,2,7,49,50,51,60,121]`, every frozen head plus the tail, six control layouts (including none), both stub states, and a reader row at every index of a 130-key order; it fails on the pre-fix planner (17 of 19) and on the shipped planner's stub states (4 of 19). `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/chat-view.client.spec.tsx` gains the reported production shape: a completed tool-only Turn whose process run outruns the window mounts its control and reveals its tool rows. Files: `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts` (the planner and its JSDoc), the new gate spec, and the ChatView case. Verified: `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/client/ui-chat` — 43 files / 646 tests at the branch tip; at the merged tip the gate reports 19 cases and the Turn-aligned spec 10 inside `ui-chat`'s 44 files / 663 tests; `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; oxlint clean on the three changed files. Not verified: no browser run — the stub control's visual behaviour in the frozen+streaming state is planner- and jsdom-proven only.
- ZH: 转写面挂载窗口的已完成轮次折叠把闭合轮次的成员行藏在它的 `turn-process` 控制行之后，因此一个挂载了这些行却没有该控制行的计划等于什么都不渲染。已落地的头部控制行挂载覆盖了窗口切片，但冻结窗口的短桩是计划的第二个区域：短桩持有最新的 50 个 key，而一个长于该切片的折叠轮次会跨过短桩起点，于是它的成员行被挂载、位于切片之上的控制行却没有——一个没有揭示路径的不可达计划（没有已保存行的读者无法移动窗口头部，分页只会前插更早的历史）。`planMountedWindow` 现在在短桩挂载期间也挂载尾部切片自身的轮次控制行；条件性额外上限为两个 key（头部控制行与短桩控制行），仍为 O(1)。同一改动把该不变量写成门禁：新增 `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/mounted-window-anchor-invariant.client.spec.ts`（19 用例）固定性质——计划能渲染的每个隐藏行，其轮次控制行都在同一计划中挂载——并遍历 order 长度 `[0,1,2,7,49,50,51,60,121]`、每个冻结头部加尾部、六种控制行布局（含无控制行）、两种短桩状态，以及 130 key order 上每一个位置的读者行；它在修复前的规划器上失败（17/19），在已发布规划器的短桩状态上失败（4/19）。`packages/client/ui-chat/tests/chat-view.client.spec.tsx` 补上所报告的生产形态：过程行数超过窗口的、末步停在工具调用的已完成轮次会挂载其控制行并揭示其工具行。文件：`packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts`（规划器及其 JSDoc）、新增门禁规格与该 ChatView 用例。已验证：`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/client/ui-chat`——分支 tip 为 43 文件 / 646 用例；合并 tip 上该门禁 19 用例、Turn 对齐规格 10 用例，含于 `ui-chat` 的 44 文件 / 663 用例内；`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；oxlint 在三个改动文件上干净。未验证：未做浏览器运行——冻结+流式状态下短桩控制行的视觉行为只有规划器与 jsdom 证明。

### 2026-09-26 — Partial-observation fences in the session client / 会话客户端的部分观测围栏

- EN: An adversarial sweep of the fork's partial-observation surfaces (client `ui-session`/`ui-subagent` and the session-controller client store) fixed five defects, each with a test that fails on the previous code. `SessionManager.readChildren` had no Host-generation fence while `refreshList` and `refreshProjections` did, so a pre-reconnect response merged its stale rows over the fresh read (a `running`-bit regression) and a superseded failure cleared the fresh read's memo: `childReadGeneration`, advanced by `handleConnected`, is now checked before the merge and on the failure path. `mergeChildren` could resurrect a child whose removal frame arrived after the response was computed: `removedChildIds` (marked by `handleSessionRemoved`, cleared by an add or a fresh generation) keeps that row out of the merge. `UiSession` treated any row leaving `byId` as a removal, so a catalog-derived child lost its observed running/`completionUnread` status whenever a generation reset cleared the projection stores; a `listBefore` snapshot separates a Host-list member that left from a stored-but-never-listed projection row, whose absence only reports catalog availability. The `ui-subagent` lineage switcher left an absent parent catalog loading forever for a restored subagent address: opening the tree now starts the lazy parent-catalog read. Files: `packages/api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/manager.ts`, `packages/client/ui-session/src/client/index.ts`, `packages/client/ui-subagent/src/client/SubagentHeaderLineage.tsx`, and the three matching specs. Verified: `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/client/ui-session packages/client/ui-subagent packages/api/session-controller` — 53 files / 1094 tests at the branch tip; `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; oxlint 0 warnings / 0 errors on the six changed files. Not verified: the `ui-session` and switcher fixes rest on client-runtime and component tests, not a rendered GUI run. A sixth finding — the list-plane projection whitelist dropping `modelSelection` — was fixed separately by its own entry above.
- ZH: 对 fork 部分观测面（客户端 `ui-session`/`ui-subagent` 与 session-controller 客户端 store）的一轮对抗性扫描修掉五个缺陷，每个都带有在改动前代码上失败的测试。`SessionManager.readChildren` 没有 Host 世代围栏，而 `refreshList` 与 `refreshProjections` 都有，于是重连前的响应把陈旧行合并到新读之上（`running` 位回退），被取代的失败还会清掉新读的 memo：现在由 `handleConnected` 推进的 `childReadGeneration` 在合并前与失败路径上都做检查。`mergeChildren` 可能复活一个删除帧晚于该响应计算的子会话：`removedChildIds`（由 `handleSessionRemoved` 标记，遇新增或新世代清除）把该行挡在合并之外。`UiSession` 把任何离开 `byId` 的行都当作删除，于是每当世代重置清空投影 store，目录派生的子会话就会丢掉已观测到的 running/`completionUnread` 状态；`listBefore` 快照把离开的 Host-list 成员与已存储但从未列入 Host 列表的投影行区分开，后者的缺席只报告目录可用性。`ui-subagent` lineage 切换器对恢复的 subagent 地址会让缺失的父目录永久停在加载态：现在打开该树会启动惰性的父目录读取。文件：`packages/api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/manager.ts`、`packages/client/ui-session/src/client/index.ts`、`packages/client/ui-subagent/src/client/SubagentHeaderLineage.tsx` 及三个对应规格。已验证：`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/client/ui-session packages/client/ui-subagent packages/api/session-controller`——分支 tip 为 53 文件 / 1094 用例；`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；oxlint 在六个改动文件上 0 告警 / 0 错误。未验证：`ui-session` 与切换器的修复依据是客户端运行时与组件测试，不是真机 GUI 运行。第六个发现——列表面投影白名单丢掉 `modelSelection`——已由上方各自条目单独修复。

### 2026-09-26 — The workflow run panel resolves members through one catalog index / 工作流运行面板经单一目录索引解析成员

- EN: Every Session-store notification re-ran the workflow run panel's member selector, and each member resolved with its own scan of the parent subagent catalog, so one invocation cost O(members × catalog size): a bench with a 200-entry catalog and 30 members counted 11,301 catalog element reads over two render passes, and the panel measured 4.4 ms per frame across 56 mounted panels with a 2,244-entry catalog. `catalogIdIndexes` (a `WeakMap` keyed on the catalog array identity) now builds one `Set<SessionId>` per identity and `navigableMembers` tests `childIds.has(member.childId)`, so one catalog pass covers the whole selector invocation; the status lookup keys on `member.childId` (value-equal to the replaced `child.id`), and the count/status semantics are unchanged. Files: `packages/client/ui-workflow-run/src/client/WorkflowRunPanel.tsx` (the index and the rewritten lookup, now carrying a `Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md)` marker) and `packages/client/ui-workflow-run/tests/workflow-run.client.spec.tsx` (a counting-Proxy case). Verified: red with the source stashed (`expected 11301 to be less than or equal to 200`) and green after; `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/client/ui-workflow-run packages/client/ui-workspace packages/client/ui-session` — 15 files / 417 tests, one more than the pristine 416; `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; oxlint 0 warnings / 0 errors on the two files. Not verified: the browser 4.4 ms/frame wall clock is not re-measured — the assertion counts catalog element reads (11,301 → 200), not time — and the bench catalog is 200 entries, not the production 2,244.
- ZH: 每次 Session-store 通知都会重跑工作流运行面板的成员 selector，而每个成员各自扫描父会话的 subagent 目录，因此一次调用成本为 O(成员数 × 目录大小)：200 条目目录、30 个成员的基准在两趟渲染中计得 11,301 次目录元素读取，2,244 条目目录下 56 个已挂载面板实测每帧 4.4 ms。`catalogIdIndexes`（按目录数组身份为键的 `WeakMap`）现在每个身份只建一个 `Set<SessionId>`，`navigableMembers` 用 `childIds.has(member.childId)` 判定成员，因此一趟目录遍历覆盖整个 selector 调用；状态查找键改为 `member.childId`（与原 `child.id` 值等价），计数/状态语义不变。文件：`packages/client/ui-workflow-run/src/client/WorkflowRunPanel.tsx`（索引与重写的查找，现带有 `Fork patch (FORK_SURFACE.md)` 标记）与 `packages/client/ui-workflow-run/tests/workflow-run.client.spec.tsx`（计数 Proxy 用例）。已验证：把源文件 stash 后为红（`expected 11301 to be less than or equal to 200`），恢复后为绿；`NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/client/ui-workflow-run packages/client/ui-workspace packages/client/ui-session`——15 文件 / 417 用例，比干净树的 416 多一例；`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；oxlint 在两个文件上 0 告警 / 0 错误。未验证：浏览器 4.4 ms/帧的墙钟未重测——断言计的是目录元素读取次数（11,301 → 200）而不是时间——且基准目录是 200 条目，不是生产的 2,244。

### 2026-09-26 — Reveal and opening window heads land on a Turn start / 揭示与开场窗口头部落在轮次开头

- EN: A window head is a history-loading boundary, and the fork's window grows over rows the client already holds: a head inside a Turn mounted that Turn's later rows without its control and its prompt, so the reader saw a Turn fragment whose start was off screen. The derived opening head and every reveal step now snap to the Turn start owning the requested row (`turnAlignedHead`, guided by the ascending resident `controls` indices), and the landing is pinned with `frozenAt` so `windowAt`'s tail normalization cannot pull an aligned head back to `tailHead` — a step that landed on the tail window was otherwise derived again from the reader's row and taken back. The reader's own row still clamps the step: when the owning Turn start sits farther above the row the step must keep than `MOUNTED_ROW_LIMIT` rows, `reveal`/`revealAtHead` refuse (and `revealable` reports false) while `pageEarlier()` serves the reader; `hold()` is unchanged, because a jump must land on the requested row. The reveal-path case of the fold-anchor gate added above kept reader `k70` under controls `[0, 60]`, which the aligned landing would drop, so that one fixture moves to `controls: [0, 20, 50]`, reader `k45`, head `k20` — the gate is 19/19 again with no assertion weakened. Files: `packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts` (+93/−22 as landed on its branch), the new `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/mounted-window-turn-aligned.client.spec.ts` (10 cases: 8 failed / 2 passed against the previous module, green after), and the one-fixture re-pin in the gate spec. Verified: the re-fixture run `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/client/ui-chat/tests/mounted-window-anchor-invariant.client.spec.ts packages/client/ui-chat/tests/mounted-window-turn-aligned.client.spec.ts` — exit 0, gate 19/19 and the Turn-aligned spec 10/10; at the merged tip `ui-chat` reports 44 files / 663 tests green (the two specs at 19 and 10 inside it); `pnpm run typecheck` exit 0; oxlint clean on the two changed files. Not verified: no browser lane and no real-GUI interaction run — the trade-off (inside one Turn longer than the row budget an explicit click no longer advances a step per click) rests on the new spec, not a browser measurement.
- ZH: 窗口头部是历史加载边界，而 fork 的窗口在客户端已持有的行之上生长：落在轮次内部的头部会挂载该轮次靠后的行却没有其控制行与提示词，于是读者看到的是起点在屏幕外的轮次残片。现在派生的开场头部与每一次揭示步都吸附到拥有目标行的轮次开头（`turnAlignedHead`，由常驻的升序 `controls` 索引引导），并用 `frozenAt` 钉住落点，使 `windowAt` 的尾部归一化不能把已对齐的头部拉回 `tailHead`——否则落在尾部窗口的步会被读者行重新派生而收回。读者自己的行仍然钳制该步：当拥有该步必须保留之行的轮次开头比它高出超过 `MOUNTED_ROW_LIMIT` 行时，`reveal`/`revealAtHead` 拒绝移动（`revealable` 报 false），改由 `pageEarlier()` 服务读者；`hold()` 不变，因为跳转必须落在读者请求的行上。上方加入的折叠锚点门禁中，揭示路径用例原本在 controls `[0, 60]` 下保留读者 `k70`，对齐落点会丢掉该行，因此该处单一夹具改为 `controls: [0, 20, 50]`、读者 `k45`、头部 `k20`——门禁恢复 19/19，未削弱任何断言。文件：`packages/client/ui-chat/src/client/chat/fork/mounted-window.ts`（按其分支落地时的 +93/−22）、新增 `packages/client/ui-chat/tests/mounted-window-turn-aligned.client.spec.ts`（10 用例：对改动前模块 8 失败 / 2 通过，改动后全绿），以及门禁规格里的单夹具重钉。已验证：重钉后运行 `NODE_ENV=test npx vitest run packages/client/ui-chat/tests/mounted-window-anchor-invariant.client.spec.ts packages/client/ui-chat/tests/mounted-window-turn-aligned.client.spec.ts`——退出 0，门禁 19/19、Turn 对齐规格 10/10；合并 tip 上 `ui-chat` 44 文件 / 663 用例全绿（两条规格各 19 与 10 含于其中）；`pnpm run typecheck` 退出 0；oxlint 在两个改动文件上干净。未验证：未跑浏览器泳道、未做真机 GUI 交互——权衡（在长于行预算的单个轮次内，显式点击不再每次前进一步）依据新规格而非浏览器测量。
